function fTx(t, x, y, fill, sz, o){
  o = o || {};
  t = String(t).replace(/&(?![a-z#0-9]+;)/gi, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;');   /* (Oct 23) an & in the words broke the whole picture ("saints & angels") */
  return '<text x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" text-anchor="' + (o.a || 'middle') + '" fill="' + fill + '" font-family="' + (o.f || F_JP) + '" font-weight="' + (o.w || 700) + '" font-size="' + sz + '"' + (o.ls ? ' letter-spacing="' + o.ls + '"' : '') + (o.op ? ' opacity="' + o.op + '"' : '') + (o.tr ? ' transform="' + o.tr + '"' : '') + '>' + t + '</text>';
}

/* ================================================================
   engine numbers.
   doc 23 said 320x180, but that is a third of the reading room's resolution
   and the pixels come out three times chunkier — the pages wouldn't match.
   so the station draws at 960x540 like the reading room, and steps down to
   640x360 and then 480x270 on its own if a machine can't keep up (the
   "degrade gracefully" rule). decided sept 29.
   1 cell = 1 unit, walls 2 units tall, my eye at 0.6 so I feel small.
   ================================================================ */
var RES = [[960,540],[800,450],[640,360],[480,270]], resStep = 0, RES_FORCE = -1;   /* Oct 6: a step between 960 and 640, so a busy view loses less sharpness; RES_FORCE: the super user panel can hold one size */

   _EYE_WAS = 0.66, TS = WALL_H - 2.0;

/* ================================================================
   the reading room's little tools, copied so the two rooms grain the same way
   ================================================================ */
function hx(s){ return [parseInt(s.substr(1,2),16), parseInt(s.substr(3,2),16), parseInt(s.substr(5,2),16)]; }
function hsh(a,b){ var n = (Math.imul(a|0,374761393) + Math.imul(b|0,668265263))|0; n = Math.imul(n ^ (n>>>13), 1274126177); n = n ^ (n>>>16); return (n>>>0) / 4294967296; }
function vn(x,y){ var xi = Math.floor(x), yi = Math.floor(y), fx = x-xi, fy = y-yi; fx = fx*fx*(3-2*fx); fy = fy*fy*(3-2*fy); var a = hsh(xi,yi), b = hsh(xi+1,yi), c = hsh(xi,yi+1), d = hsh(xi+1,yi+1); return a + (b-a)*fx + (c-a)*fy + (a-b-c+d)*fx*fy; }
function fbm(x,y){ return vn(x,y)*0.55 + vn(x*2.03+17.1,y*2.03-9.4)*0.3 + vn(x*4.1-3.3,y*4.1+5.7)*0.15; }
function frac(v){ return v - Math.floor(v); }
function clamp(v,a,b){ return v < a ? a : (v > b ? b : v); }
function sm(a,b,v){ var t = clamp((v - a)/(b - a), 0, 1); return t*t*(3 - 2*t); }

/* ================================================================
   ramps. every material is a short ramp, same as the reading room — shading
   picks a step, bayer picks between steps. wood, paper, black, brass, rustm,
   the book colours, phone and gap are copied from the reading room unchanged
   so shared materials match exactly.
   everything is built out of the core five. checked: nothing leans blue.
   ================================================================ */
var RAMP_SRC = {
  /* station marble: cream-grey, the platform walls and pillars */
  marble: ['#16120f','#221c18','#2f2822','#3d352d','#4b4239','#5a5046','#6a5f54','#7a6f62','#8b8071','#9c9181','#ada292','#bdb3a3','#cdc4b5','#dcd4c6'],
  /* veins go plum-grey, never blue-grey */
  vein:   ['#141115','#1d191e','#27222a','#312b34','#3c353f','#48404a','#544b56','#605763','#6c636f'],

/* (Oct 30, Aisling: the pinks "more muted", for the whole platform) Furusato's glowing rose (the neon, the blossom
   lights, the claw machine's posts, the dance pad's arrows …) is taken most of the way to a dusty, faded rose: the same
   brightness, much less colour, leaning warm. (not on the line's stops: F03 keeps its own) */
if (typeof F03 === 'undefined') (function(){
  function hx2(c){ return [parseInt(c.slice(1, 3), 16), parseInt(c.slice(3, 5), 16), parseInt(c.slice(5, 7), 16)]; }
  function mute(c, k){ var q = hx2(c), L = (q[0]*0.3 + q[1]*0.59 + q[2]*0.11)/255, d = [L*1.12 + 0.03, L*0.92, L*0.9];
    return '#' + q.map(function(v, i){ var o = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v/255*(1 - k) + d[i]*k))*255); return (o < 16 ? '0' : '') + o.toString(16); }).join(''); }
  RAMP_SRC.hglrose = RAMP_SRC.hglrose.map(function(c){ return mute(c, 0.72); });
  RAMP_SRC.tagmuted = RAMP_SRC.tagpink.map(function(c){ return mute(c, 0.55); });   /* (Oct 30, Aisling: her friend's tag "too bright … a muted orange") */
})();
var STREET_RAMPS = { sfog:1, sstone:1 }, STREET_FOGC = [122, 103, 97];   /* Furusato: every ink fades to the same pale grey */
var P2_RAMPS = { pwhite:1, pgreen:1, pneon:1 }, P2_FOGC = [122, 103, 97];
var HUB_RAMPS = { hcream:1, hgilt:1, hrose:1, hsage:1, hglrose:1, hglgreen:1, hglgold:1, hblood:1, hplas:1, hwood:1, terra:1, hgold:1, hnight:1, hblack:1, hrust:1, hplum:1, hgrey:1, holive:1, hamber:1 }, HUB_FOGC = [122, 103, 97];
var RNAMES = Object.keys(RAMP_SRC), RIX = {}, RN = new Int32Array(RNAMES.length);
RNAMES.forEach(function(k,i){ RIX[k] = i; RN[i] = RAMP_SRC[k].length; });

/* depth fog, the reading room's: far things lose contrast and drift toward a
   dark plum. prebaked into a table (ramp × fog level × step) so the frame loop
   only ever looks colours up. */
var FOGN = 16, RMAX = 16, FOGMAX = 0.95, FOGC = [122, 103, 97];   /* Furusato: bright, so far things fade toward a pale warm grey, and less of them goes */
var RF = new Uint32Array(RNAMES.length*FOGN*RMAX);
RNAMES.forEach(function(k, ri){
  var cols = RAMP_SRC[k].map(hx), FOGC0 = FOGC;
  if (HUB_RAMPS[k]) FOGC = HUB_FOGC;   /* the hub's ramps fade to warm brown */
  if (STREET_RAMPS[k]) FOGC = STREET_FOGC;
  if (P2_RAMPS[k]) FOGC = P2_FOGC;   /* the street's, into its fog */
  for (var f = 0; f < FOGN; f++) {
    var fk = f/(FOGN - 1)*FOGMAX;
    for (var j = 0; j < RMAX; j++) {
      var c = cols[Math.min(j, cols.length - 1)];
      var r = c[0] + (FOGC[0] - c[0])*fk, g = c[1] + (FOGC[1] - c[1])*fk, b = c[2] + (FOGC[2] - c[2])*fk;
      RF[(ri*FOGN + f)*RMAX + j] = 0xff000000 | ((b|0) << 16) | ((g|0) << 8) | (r|0);
    }
  }
  FOGC = FOGC0;
});
var BAY = new Float32Array([0,8,2,10,12,4,14,6,3,11,1,9,15,7,13,5].map(function(v){ return (v + 0.5)/16; }));

/* one texel = ramp (6 bits) + value 0..1 (9 bits) + lit-from-inside flag (1 bit).
   same idea as the reading room's M/V/E buffers, just packed. */
/* Oct 5 (Aisling): the deir sign — its own character: a lowercase д with a bar through its middle,
   a little wider than the letter (like ₽). it's one glyph in a tiny font of its own ("Deir", at U+E0D0,
   drawn by font/mkdeir.py — redraw it there and paste the new base64 here to change the sign everywhere).
   in the code a price is still written д + U+0336; pictures, captions and Plt 1's printed close-ups turn
   that into the sign; the dot fonts have their own barred д (FONT['д']) */
var DEIR_CH = '\uE0D0', DEIR_SRC = 'url(data:font/ttf;base64,AAEAAAAKAIAAAwAgT1MvMiWwNKYAAAEoAAAAYGNtYXAADOEjAAABkAAAADRnbHlmtK+07AAAAcwAAACAaGVhZC9o8bAAAACsAAAANmhoZWEFjgHjAAAA5AAAACRobXR4BHQARgAAAYgAAAAIbG9jYQBAAA0AAAHEAAAABm1heHAACgAeAAABCAAAACBuYW1lVUpPagAAAkwAAABXcG9zdGZvdmQAAAKkAAAAKwABAAAAAQAABeGSp18PPPUAAQPoAAAAAObp11AAAAAA5unXUAAe/3QCYgK8AAAAAwACAAAAAAAAAAEAAAMg/zgAAAKAABQAKAJYAAEAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAACAAEAAAACABwABwAAAAAAAgAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAwI6AZAABQAEAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAEAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAPz8/PwAA4NDg0AMg/zgAAAMgAMgAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAgAAAB9AAyAoAAFAAAAAIAAAADAAAAFAADAAEAAAAUAAQAIAAAAAQABAABAADg0P//AADg0P//HzEAAQAAAAAAAAANAEAAAAABADIAAAHCArwAAwAAMxEhETIBkAK8/UQAAAcAHv90AmICCAADAAcACwAPABMAFwAbAAAzNSEVBTUzFSE1MxUnETMRATUhFQETMwMnNSEVRgH0/gxGAWhGdkr+yAE4/oRESkS+AkRKSoyMjIyMjAII/fgBwEhI/ooBvv5CjD4+AAAABAA2AAEAAAAAAAEABAAAAAEAAAAAAAIABwAEAAMAAQQJAAEACAALAAMAAQQJAAIADgATRGVpclJlZ3VsYXIARABlAGkAcgBSAGUAZwB1AGwAYQByAAACAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAIAAAECBGRlaXIA) format("truetype")';
var DEIR_CSS = '@font-face{font-family:Deir;src:' + DEIR_SRC + '}';
(function(){ try { var st = document.createElement('style'); st.textContent = DEIR_CSS; document.head.appendChild(st); var ff = new FontFace('Deir', DEIR_SRC); ff.load().then(function(f){ document.fonts.add(f); }); } catch (e) {} })();
function deirSvg(s){
  if (s.indexOf('д\u0336') < 0) return s;
  s = s.replace(/д\u0336/g, '<tspan font-family="Deir">' + DEIR_CH + '</tspan>');
  var k = s.indexOf('<svg'), e = k >= 0 ? s.indexOf('>', k) : -1;
  return e >= 0 ? s.slice(0, e + 1) + '<defs><style>' + DEIR_CSS + '</style></defs>' + s.slice(e + 1) : s;
}
var encodeURIComponent = (function(enc){ return function(s){ return enc(typeof s === 'string' && s.indexOf('<svg') >= 0 ? deirSvg(s) : s); }; })(window.encodeURIComponent);
function setTextDeir(el, t){
  t = t == null ? '' : String(t);
  if (t.indexOf('д\u0336') < 0) { el.textContent = t; return; }
  el.textContent = ''; var parts = t.split('д\u0336');
  parts.forEach(function(q, i){ if (q) el.appendChild(document.createTextNode(q)); if (i < parts.length - 1) { var sp = document.createElement('span'); sp.textContent = DEIR_CH; sp.style.fontFamily = 'Deir'; el.appendChild(sp); } });
}
function PK(ri, v, e){ return (ri << 10) | (Math.round(clamp(v,0,1)*511) << 1) | (e ? 1 : 0); }

    R_BOCHRE = RIX.bOchre, R_BCREAM = RIX.bCream, R_PHONE = RIX.phone, R_GAP = RIX.gap, R_SILVER = RIX.silver, R_NEON = RIX.neon, R_NULLBLUE = RIX.nullblue, R_PLUMGLOW = RIX.plumglow, R_IFUR = RIX.ivanfur, R_IEYE = RIX.ivaneye, R_HEART = RIX.heart,
    R_HPLAS = RIX.hplas, R_HWOOD = RIX.hwood, R_TERRA = RIX.terra, R_HGOLD = RIX.hgold, R_HNIGHT = RIX.hnight, R_HBLACK = RIX.hblack,
    R_HRUST = RIX.hrust, R_HPLUM = RIX.hplum, R_HGREY = RIX.hgrey, R_HOLIVE = RIX.holive, R_HAMBER = RIX.hamber, R_SFOG = RIX.sfog, R_SSTONE = RIX.sstone, R_PWHITE = RIX.pwhite, R_PGREEN = RIX.pgreen, R_PNEON = RIX.pneon, R_HBLOOD = RIX.hblood, R_HCREAM = RIX.hcream, R_HGILT = RIX.hgilt, R_HROSE = RIX.hrose, R_HSAGE = RIX.hsage, R_HGLROSE = RIX.hglrose, R_HGLGREEN = RIX.hglgreen, R_HGLGOLD = RIX.hglgold, R_TAG = RIX.tagpink;
var BOX_CLEAR = (RIX.sfog << 10) | 1;   /* sfog, value 0, "lit": nothing paints this, so a hub box texel with it is see-through */

/* ================================================================
   textures. painted once at load from functions of world position — same as
   the reading room builds its room from depth instead of fixed shapes.
   128 texels per world unit, so a wall cell is 128 wide and 256 tall.
   each texture keeps smaller copies (mipmaps) for far away, or distant walls
   would sparkle as I walk. picked per column / per row by distance.
   ================================================================ */
var TEXRES = 128;
function newTex(w, h){ return { scale:1, lv:[{ w:w, h:h, d:new Uint16Array(w*h) }] }; }
/* shrinking a texture: average how bright the four texels actually look, keep the
   material most of them are, and find that brightness in its ramp. the first
   version averaged the raw values and kept the joint's near-black ramp on a tie,
   so every slab joint stayed pitch black at every distance and broke into dashes. */
var RLUM = RNAMES.map(function(k){ return RAMP_SRC[k].map(function(c){ var q = hx(c); return q[0]*0.3 + q[1]*0.59 + q[2]*0.11; }); });
function lumOf(ri, v){ var L = RLUM[ri], p = v*(L.length - 1), i = Math.floor(p); if (i >= L.length - 1) return L[L.length - 1]; return L[i] + (L[i + 1] - L[i])*(p - i); }
function vFor(ri, lum){
  var L = RLUM[ri], n = L.length;
  if (lum <= L[0]) return 0; if (lum >= L[n - 1]) return 1;
  for (var i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) if (lum <= L[i + 1]) return (i + (lum - L[i])/Math.max(1e-6, L[i + 1] - L[i]))/(n - 1);
  return 1;
}
function mix4(a,b,c,d){
  var q = [a, b, c, d], lum = 0, e = 0, best = -1, r = a >> 10, bestL = -1;
  for (var k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
    var rk = q[k] >> 10, lk = lumOf(rk, ((q[k] >> 1) & 511)/511);
    lum += lk; e += q[k] & 1;
    var cnt = 0; for (var m = 0; m < 4; m++) if ((q[m] >> 10) === rk) cnt++;
    if (cnt > best || (cnt === best && lk > bestL)) { best = cnt; r = rk; bestL = lk; }
  }
  /* a lone glitter fleck (lit silver or gold) survives into the smaller copy, so the floor still sparkles further off */
  var gm = -1; for (var g1 = 0; g1 < 4; g1++) if ((q[g1] & 1) && (q[g1] >> 10) === RIX.granite) gm = g1;
  if (gm >= 0) { var gv0 = Math.round(vFor(r, lum/4)*511); return (r << 10) | (gv0 << 1) | (gm === 0 ? 1 : 0); }   /* glitter marks thin out in smaller copies so there are never more of them further away (it was a disco floor) */
  if (e === 1) for (var g2 = 0; g2 < 4; g2++) { var gq = q[g2]; if ((gq & 1) && ((gq >> 10) === RIX.silver || (gq >> 10) === RIX.gold) && ((gq >> 1) & 511) > 460) return (gq & 0xFC01) | (Math.round(((gq >> 1) & 511)*0.9) << 1); }
  var v = Math.round(vFor(r, lum/4)*511);
  return (r << 10) | (v << 1) | (e >= 2 ? 1 : 0);
}
function buildMips(t){
  var want = t.scale > 1 ? 7 : 6;
  while (t.lv.length < want) {
    var s = t.lv[t.lv.length - 1], w = s.w >> 1, h = s.h >> 1, d = new Uint16Array(w*h);
    for (var y = 0; y < h; y++) for (var x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      var i = (y*2)*s.w + x*2;
      d[y*w + x] = mix4(s.d[i], s.d[i + 1], s.d[i + s.w], s.d[i + s.w + 1]);
    }
    t.lv.push({ w:w, h:h, d:d });
  }
  return t;
}

/* wall faces. face 0 = west side of the cell, 1 = east, 2 = north, 3 = south.
   u runs left to right as I see the face, so writing on a wall reads the right
   way round from any side. the painter gets u, the height h (0 floor, 2 top),
   and the world point (x, y) the texel sits on. */
/* (Oct 31) a wall picture's rows: its height in texels is rounded to 64, so a texel row was a hair shorter than a
   texel column (1216 rows for 2.4 at 512), and a grid of small tiles didn't land on whole texels: some grout lines
   came out thicker than others. with WALL_SNAP on (Furusato), rows are exactly as tall as columns are wide, and the
   picture is stretched the hair (about 1%) to fill the wall. off: as it always was */
var WALL_SNAP = false;
function wallRowH(j, Lh, WH, res){ if (WALL_SNAP) { var Hs = Lh/res; if (Math.abs(Hs - WH) < 0.03*WH) return (Lh - j - 0.5)/res; } return WH*(1 - (j + 0.5)/Lh); }
function paintWall(cx, cy, face, fn, res, hgt){
  res = res || TEXRES; TI_RES = res;
  /* height follows the wall (2.4 since the room got taller), rounded to 64.
     hub build: a wall can be taller than Plt 1's (the hall's walls rise into the vault) —
     hgt, or the room's own wall height; t.hgt tells the frame how tall the picture is */
  var WH = hgt || wallHFor(cx, cy, face);
  var t = newTex(res, Math.round(res*WH/64)*64), L = t.lv[0]; t.scale = res/TEXRES; t.hgt = WH;
  for (var j = 0; j < L.h; j++) {
    var h = wallRowH(j, L.h, WH, res);
    for (var i = 0; i < L.w; i++) {
      var u = (i + 0.5)/L.w, x, y;
      if (face === 0) { x = cx; y = cy + u; }
      else if (face === 1) { x = cx + 1; y = cy + 1 - u; }
      else if (face === 2) { x = cx + 1 - u; y = cy; }
      else { x = cx + u; y = cy + 1; }
      L.d[j*L.w + i] = fn(u, h, x, y, i, j);
    }
  }
  TI_RES = TEXRES;
  return buildMips(t);
}
/* floors and ceilings are painted straight in world x/y, so patterns run on
   across cells without seams */
/* direct = paint every smaller copy from scratch instead of averaging down.
   the mosaics need it: averaged grout turns a far panel into plaid, so the far
   copies are painted with no grout at all (PAINT_LOD tells the painter which copy). */
var PAINT_LOD = 0;
/* res = texels per unit. the mosaics use 256 (twice everything else) so the tesserae
   hold up when I'm standing right under a panel looking up. */
function paintFlat(cx, cy, fn, direct, res){
  res = res || TEXRES;
  var t = newTex(res, res); t.scale = res/TEXRES;
  for (var l = 0; l < (direct ? 7 : 1); l++) {
    if (l > 0) t.lv.push({ w:res >> l, h:res >> l, d:new Uint16Array((res >> l)*(res >> l)) });
    var L = t.lv[l]; PAINT_LOD = l; TI_RES = res >> l;
    for (var j = 0; j < L.h; j++) for (var i = 0; i < L.w; i++) {
      L.d[j*L.w + i] = fn(cx + (i + 0.5)/L.w, cy + (j + 0.5)/L.h, i, j);
    }
  }
  PAINT_LOD = 0; TI_RES = TEXRES;
  return direct ? t : buildMips(t);
}

/* is the point (s, h) inside a lit pixel of this string?
   s runs right, h runs up; s0 is the left edge, top is the top of the line, px is one font pixel in world units */
function textHit(str, s, h, s0, top, px){
  var gx = (s - s0)/px, gy = (top - h)/px;
  if (gy < 0 || gy >= 7 || gx < 0) return false;
  var ci = Math.floor(gx/6), col = Math.floor(gx - ci*6);
  if (ci >= str.length || col >= 5) return false;
  var g = FONT[str.charAt(ci)];
  return !!g && g[Math.floor(gy)].charAt(col) === '#';
}
function textW(str, px){ return (str.length*6 - 1)*px; }

/* the reading room's fake print: word-shaped dashes that read as text from a
   step back without saying anything. for poster small print and labels. */
function scribble(s, h, s0, s1, top, lineH, lines, seed){
  if (s < s0 || s > s1 || h > top || h < top - lines*lineH) return false;
  var line = Math.floor((top - h)/lineH), fy = ((top - h)/lineH) - line;
  if (fy < 0.25 || fy > 0.72) return false;
  var k = Math.floor((s - s0)/(lineH*0.9)), wk = hsh(k + line*31, seed);
  if (wk < 0.2) return false;
  if (line === lines - 1 && (s - s0)/(s1 - s0) > 0.55) return false;
  return hsh(Math.floor((s - s0)/(lineH*0.22)), line + seed*7) > 0.22;
}

;
/* ---- copied from null.grey-furusatostation/src/shared/hy2-journey.js ---- */

/* ================================================================
   GOING BETWEEN THE STATION'S PAGES (Oct 23, 2026; doc 30). shared by every station page
   (this one file is included by the station page and by Furusato's page as it is).
   - the pocket, passport, stamps and everything else live in the browser's storage
     ("nullgrey.*"). when both pages come from the same site address, that storage is
     shared and nothing else is needed.
   - as a backup, going between pages also carries those things in the link. the next
     page uses them only if its storage turns out not to be the same one (e.g. Safari
     with two local files).
   - inside Framer (an embed), the page asks the site to go to the other page's address;
     where you arrive is remembered in storage ("nullgrey.arrive").
   - the jump is hidden: a moment of dark and fog while the next page opens.
   runs first thing, before anything reads the storage.
   ================================================================ */
var NG_PAGES = {
  hub:      { path:'/between',  file:'null-grey-between-station.html' },
  furusato: { path:'/furusato', file:'furusato.html' },
  f03:      { path:'/mainstreet', file:'mainstreet.html' },   /* (Oct 25) F 03 · 本町 Main Street, Missing Mile: its little station */
  stop:     { path:'/stop', file:'stop.html' }   /* (Oct 31) a stand-in stop: where a ride ends until that stop is built (Embroidery). the stop is in the arrival code */
};
var NGJ = { leaving:false, fade:null };
function ngArrival(here){
  var q = null, A = null;
  try { q = new URLSearchParams(location.search); } catch (e) {}
  try { A = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('nullgrey.arrive') || 'null'); } catch (e) {}
  var tok = q && q.get('tok');
  if (tok) {
    if (!(A && A.tok === tok)) {   /* this page's storage isn't the one the last page wrote to: take what came in the link */
      try { var c = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(q.get('carry') || '')))); for (var k in c) if (k.indexOf('nullgrey.') === 0 && k !== 'nullgrey.arrive') localStorage.setItem(k, c[k]); } catch (e) {}
    }
    try { localStorage.removeItem('nullgrey.arrive'); history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname); } catch (e) {}
    return q.get('arrive') ? { at:q.get('arrive') } : null;
  }
  if (A && A.to === here && Date.now() - A.t < 180000) { try { localStorage.removeItem('nullgrey.arrive'); } catch (e) {} return { at:A.at }; }
  return null;
}
function ngCarry(){
  var c = {};
  try { for (var i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) { var k = localStorage.key(i); if (k && k.indexOf('nullgrey.') === 0 && k !== 'nullgrey.arrive') c[k] = localStorage.getItem(k); } } catch (e) {}
  try { return btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(c)))); } catch (e) { return ''; }
}
/* the dark and the fog over the jump */
function ngFade(on){
  if (!NGJ.fade) {
    var st = document.createElement('style');
    st.textContent = '#ng-fade{position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:100%;height:100%;pointer-events:none;opacity:0;transition:opacity .7s ease;z-index:45;' +
      'background:radial-gradient(ellipse 60% 45% at 30% 60%,rgba(120,114,106,.22),rgba(120,114,106,0) 70%),radial-gradient(ellipse 55% 40% at 72% 38%,rgba(150,144,134,.18),rgba(150,144,134,0) 70%),#0a0908;}' +
      '#ng-fade.on{opacity:1;}#ng-fade.on.drift{animation:ngdrift 6s ease-in-out infinite alternate;}@keyframes ngdrift{0%{background-position:0 0,0 0}100%{background-position:6% -3%,-5% 4%}}';
    document.head.appendChild(st);
    var root = document.getElementById('stage') || document.body;
    NGJ.fade = document.createElement('div'); NGJ.fade.id = 'ng-fade'; root.appendChild(NGJ.fade);
  }
  NGJ.fade.classList.toggle('on', !!on); NGJ.fade.classList.toggle('drift', !!on);
}
/* go to another station page: the fade, then the next page */
function ngGoTo(page, at){
  if (NGJ.leaving) return; NGJ.leaving = true;
  ngFade(true);
  setTimeout(function(){
    var P = NG_PAGES[page], tok = String(Date.now()) + Math.floor(Math.random()*1e6);
    try { localStorage.setItem('nullgrey.arrive', JSON.stringify({ to:page, at:at, tok:tok, t:Date.now() })); } catch (e) {}
    if (window.parent && window.parent !== window) { try { window.parent.postMessage({ nullgrey:'navigate', href:P.path }, '*'); } catch (e) {} return; }   /* inside Framer: the site moves */
    location.href = P.file + '?arrive=' + encodeURIComponent(at) + '&tok=' + tok + '&carry=' + encodeURIComponent(ngCarry());
  }, 750);
}

/* the hub's own dice: Furusato's things roll their own */
var HUB_RND = (function(){ var s = 20261023; return function(){ s ^= s << 13; s ^= s >>> 17; s ^= s << 5; return ((s >>> 0) % 1e9)/1e9; }; })();

/* ceilings: one height per cell (f1 sets Furusato's: the platform's mesh, the low side rooms) */
var CEILK = new Float32Array(MW*MH); for (var cki = 0; cki < MW*MH; cki++) CEILK[cki] = WALL_H;
var STEP_L = new Float32Array(MW*MH*4); STEP_L.fill(1);
function wallHFor(cx, cy, face){
  var nx = cx + (face === 0 ? -1 : face === 1 ? 1 : 0), ny = cy + (face === 2 ? -1 : face === 3 ? 1 : 0);
  if (nx >= 0 && ny >= 0 && nx < MW && ny < MH && !isWall(cellAt(nx, ny))) return CEILK[ny*MW + nx];
  return WALL_H;
}

/* ---- copied from null.grey-furusatostation/src/d5-pocket-art.js ---- */
/* ================================================================
   STEP 8 — THE POCKET: the things in it (doc 20).
   each thing is drawn once as an svg, then at load turned into station
   pixels (every pixel snapped into the station's own colour ramps and
   dithered), the same way the posters are. that's what lets it lie crooked
   and small in the pocket and lift up big and straight without blurring.
   vb: the drawing's size; k: pixels per unit when it's lifted to the
   middle; lie: how big it lies in the pocket (of the lifted size).
   Aisling can rewrite any caption here.
   ================================================================ */
var PKC = { cream:'#e4dcd0', card:'#e8dcc4', paper:'#ddd3c0', void:'#0a0908', rust:'#8a5240', red:'#9a2a22', plum:'#5c4a63', dplum:'#241b29', grey:'#8c8983', amber:'#d9a441', gold:'#c9a86a', silver:'#d8d6d0' };
function pkT(t, x, y, sz, fill, o){ o = o || {}; return '<text x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '"' + (o.anchor ? ' text-anchor="' + o.anchor + '"' : ' text-anchor="middle"') + (o.tf ? ' transform="' + o.tf + '"' : '') + ' style="fill:' + fill + ';font-family:' + (o.font || 'monospace') + ';font-size:' + sz + 'px;font-weight:' + (o.w || 400) + ';letter-spacing:' + (o.ls || 0) + 'px' + (o.it ? ';font-style:italic' : '') + '">' + t + '</text>'; }
/* the size a line of text needs to fit maxW, measured in this browser's own font (Aisling, Oct 2:
   the newspaper's changing lines ran off the page — fonts differ between machines) */
function pkFit(t, sz, maxW, o){
  o = o || {};
  try {
    var c = pkFit.c || (pkFit.c = document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d'));
    c.font = (o.it ? 'italic ' : '') + (o.w || 400) + ' ' + sz + 'px ' + (o.font || 'monospace');
    var w = c.measureText(t).width + (o.ls || 0)*t.length;
    return w > maxW ? Math.floor(sz*maxW/w*10)/10 : sz;
  } catch (e) { return sz; }
}
function pkWrap(s, n){ var w = s.split(' '), out = [], cur = ''; w.forEach(function(x){ if ((cur + ' ' + x).trim().length > n) { out.push(cur.trim()); cur = x; } else cur += ' ' + x; }); if (cur.trim()) out.push(cur.trim()); return out; }
function pkStar(x, y, r, fill){ return '<path d="M' + x + ' ' + (y - r) + ' L' + (x + r*0.28) + ' ' + (y - r*0.28) + ' L' + (x + r) + ' ' + y + ' L' + (x + r*0.28) + ' ' + (y + r*0.28) + ' L' + x + ' ' + (y + r) + ' L' + (x - r*0.28) + ' ' + (y + r*0.28) + ' L' + (x - r) + ' ' + y + ' L' + (x - r*0.28) + ' ' + (y - r*0.28) + 'Z" fill="' + fill + '"/>'; }

   one real hint, picked from where you are in the station ---- */

  var heads = [

    ['ТУМАН', 'ДО 27:00', 'fog until 27:00. stay below ground.'],

  ];
  var MON = ['ЯНВАРЯ', 'ФЕВРАЛЯ', 'МАРТА', 'АПРЕЛЯ', 'МАЯ', 'ИЮНЯ', 'ИЮЛЯ', 'АВГУСТА', 'СЕНТЯБРЯ', 'ОКТЯБРЯ', 'НОЯБРЯ', 'ДЕКАБРЯ'], d = new Date();
  return { head:heads[(Math.random()*heads.length) | 0], date:d.getDate() + ' ' + MON[d.getMonth()] + ' 1022', hint:null };

function paperHint(){
  if (PAPER.hint) return PAPER.hint;
  var gen = [
    'the machine gives back what isn\'t yours. try the coin return.',
    'follow the crack in the floor. it knows where it is going.',
    'door 13 is not for passengers.',
    'the black cat on platform 1 is not lost. please do not feed him.',
    'mind the seam.'
  ], pri = null;
  if (!FIGTALK.gone) pri = 'buy everything the machine on platform 1 has. a passenger is waiting for that.';
  else if (PASS_STATE === 1) pri = 'lost property: one pass, tucked into the corner of a photograph.';
  else if (PASS_STATE === 2) pri = 'the caboose has one seat, reserved. bring your pass when the train is in.';
  PAPER.hint = (pri && Math.random() < 0.65) ? pri : gen[(Math.random()*gen.length) | 0];
  return PAPER.hint;
}

/* ---- copied from null.grey-furusatostation/src/d5-pocket-art2.js ---- */
/* ================================================================
   STEP 8b — the pocket things, redrawn (Aisling: match the real designs,
   a few more details on each). the passport cover, the multipass and the
   Kodama ticket are the project's own art (art/passport-holographic-runes,
   art/multipass-ticket, art/kodama-ticket-foil) carried over line for line;
   the rest get small extra details. these replace the first drafts.
   ================================================================ */
/* the passport cover (art/passport-holographic-runes): runes up the spine,
   the tower in the seal, three rings and three stars */
var PK_RUNES = [
  function(x, y){ return 'M' + x + ' ' + y + ' V' + (y - 10) + ' M' + x + ' ' + (y - 10) + ' L' + (x + 5) + ' ' + (y - 13) + ' M' + x + ' ' + (y - 6) + ' L' + (x + 5) + ' ' + (y - 9); },
  function(x, y){ return 'M' + x + ' ' + y + ' V' + (y - 11) + ' L' + (x + 5) + ' ' + (y - 7) + ' V' + y; },
  function(x, y){ return 'M' + x + ' ' + y + ' V' + (y - 11) + ' M' + x + ' ' + (y - 8) + ' L' + (x + 4) + ' ' + (y - 5.5) + ' L' + x + ' ' + (y - 3); },
  function(x, y){ return 'M' + (x + 2.5) + ' ' + y + ' V' + (y - 11) + ' M' + (x + 2.5) + ' ' + (y - 6) + ' L' + x + ' ' + (y - 10) + ' M' + (x + 2.5) + ' ' + (y - 6) + ' L' + (x + 5) + ' ' + (y - 10); },
  function(x, y){ return 'M' + x + ' ' + (y - 11) + ' L' + (x + 5) + ' ' + y + ' M' + (x + 5) + ' ' + (y - 11) + ' L' + x + ' ' + y; },
  function(x, y){ return 'M' + (x + 2.5) + ' ' + y + ' V' + (y - 11) + ' M' + x + ' ' + (y - 8) + ' L' + (x + 2.5) + ' ' + (y - 11) + ' L' + (x + 5) + ' ' + (y - 8); },
  function(x, y){ return 'M' + (x + 5) + ' ' + (y - 11) + ' L' + x + ' ' + (y - 5.5) + ' L' + (x + 5) + ' ' + y; },
  function(x, y){ return 'M' + (x + 1) + ' ' + (y - 11) + ' L' + (x + 4) + ' ' + (y - 7) + ' L' + (x + 1) + ' ' + (y - 4) + ' L' + (x + 4) + ' ' + y; },
  function(x, y){ return 'M' + x + ' ' + y + ' L' + (x + 2.5) + ' ' + (y - 4) + ' L' + (x + 5) + ' ' + y + ' M' + (x + 2.5) + ' ' + (y - 4) + ' L' + x + ' ' + (y - 8) + ' L' + (x + 2.5) + ' ' + (y - 11) + ' L' + (x + 5) + ' ' + (y - 8) + ' L' + (x + 2.5) + ' ' + (y - 4); }
];
function pkHolo(id){ return '<linearGradient id="' + id + '" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse" x1="0" y1="0" x2="120" y2="60" spreadMethod="reflect"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#b9b7b2"/><stop offset="0.25" stop-color="#9c8fb8"/><stop offset="0.5" stop-color="#a8a6a0"/><stop offset="0.75" stop-color="#c9a86a"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#b9b7b2"/></linearGradient>'; }
function pkRuneRow(x, y, n, grad, seed, vertical, sw){ var d = '', r = seed; for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) { r = (r*9301 + 49297) % 233280; var f = PK_RUNES[r % PK_RUNES.length]; d += vertical ? f(x, y - i*16) + ' ' : f(x + i*9, y) + ' '; } return '<path d="' + d + '" fill="none" stroke="url(#' + grad + ')" stroke-width="' + (sw || 1.3) + '" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>'; }
function pkStar5(x, y, r, fill){ var d = ''; for (var i = 0; i < 10; i++) { var a = -Math.PI/2 + i*Math.PI/5, rr = i % 2 ? r*0.45 : r; d += (i ? 'L' : 'M') + (x + Math.cos(a)*rr).toFixed(2) + ' ' + (y + Math.sin(a)*rr).toFixed(2); } return '<path d="' + d + 'Z" fill="' + fill + '"/>'; }

PK_ART.passport.vb = [200, 280]; PK_ART.passport.k = 1.18; PK_ART.passport.lie = 0.33;
PK_ART.passport.svg = function(){
  var bg = '#17121d', bg2 = '#1d1724', spn = '#0f0b13', f = '#b9b7b2';
  var c = '<defs>' + pkHolo('hc') + '<clipPath id="seal"><circle cx="105" cy="150" r="43"/></clipPath></defs><rect width="200" height="280" rx="8" fill="' + bg + '"/>';
  for (var y = 4; y < 280; y += 3) c += '<line x1="0" y1="' + y + '" x2="200" y2="' + y + '" stroke="' + bg2 + '" stroke-width="1"/>';
  c += '<rect x="0" y="0" width="12" height="280" fill="' + spn + '"/>' + pkRuneRow(3.5, 262, 15, 'hc', 5, true, 1.2);
  c += '<text x="106" y="48" text-anchor="middle" style="fill:' + f + ';font-family:\'Arial Black\',Impact,sans-serif;font-size:19px;letter-spacing:3px">ПАСПОРТ</text><text x="106" y="70" text-anchor="middle" style="fill:' + f + ';font-family:serif;font-size:14px;letter-spacing:4px">旅券 · ウキヨ</text>';
  c += '<circle cx="105" cy="150" r="52" fill="none" stroke="' + f + '" stroke-width="2.5"/><circle cx="105" cy="150" r="47" fill="none" stroke="' + f + '" stroke-width="1"/>';
  c += '<g clip-path="url(#seal)" fill="none" stroke="' + f + '"><rect x="72" y="136" width="11" height="54" stroke-width="1.5"/><rect x="62" y="152" width="10" height="38" stroke-width="1.5"/><rect x="127" y="136" width="11" height="54" stroke-width="1.5"/><rect x="138" y="152" width="10" height="38" stroke-width="1.5"/><path d="M91 186 L102 152 M119 186 L108 152" stroke-width="1.8"/>';
  for (var k = 0; k < 4; k++) { var yy = 182 - k*8, w = 13 - k*2.5; c += '<line x1="' + (105 - w) + '" y1="' + yy + '" x2="' + (105 + w) + '" y2="' + yy + '" stroke-width="1.1"/>'; }
  c += '<line x1="55" y1="187" x2="155" y2="187" stroke-width="1.5"/></g>';
  [[105, 118], [93, 138], [117, 138]].forEach(function(p){ c += '<circle cx="' + p[0] + '" cy="' + p[1] + '" r="7" fill="none" stroke="' + f + '" stroke-width="1.8"/>'; });
  c += pkStar5(98, 128, 3, f) + pkStar5(112, 128, 3, f) + pkStar5(105, 141, 3, f) + '<line x1="30" y1="232" x2="180" y2="232" stroke="' + f + '" stroke-width="0.8" opacity="0.6"/><line x1="30" y1="262" x2="180" y2="262" stroke="' + f + '" stroke-width="0.8" opacity="0.6"/>';
  c += '<text x="105" y="252" text-anchor="middle" style="fill:' + f + ';font-family:monospace;font-size:9px;letter-spacing:3px;opacity:.7">UKIYO · ウキヨ</text>';
  c += '<path d="M190 6 q4 2 6 7 M6 272 q3 -1 8 1 M150 268 l12 -1" stroke="#2c2433" stroke-width="1.6" fill="none"/>';   /* worn corners */
  return c;
};

/* the Kodama ticket (art/kodama-ticket-foil), still out of reach */
PK_ART.kodama.vb = [680, 300]; PK_ART.kodama.k = 0.82; PK_ART.kodama.lie = 0.22; PK_ART.kodama.foil = true;
PK_ART.kodama.svg = function(){
  var R = '#9a2a22', K = '#17110f', C = '#e8dcc4', M = '#5e1512', G = 'url(#gold)', S = 'url(#silver)', s = 13;
  var rnd = function(){ s = (s*9301 + 49297) % 233280; return s/233280; };
  function runes(x, y, len, h, sw, col){ var d = 'M' + x + ',' + y, cx = x, dots = ''; col = col || R;
    while (cx < x + len) { var w = 7 + rnd()*7, t = Math.floor(rnd()*5);
      if (t === 0) d += ' C' + (cx + 2) + ',' + (y - h) + ' ' + (cx + w) + ',' + (y - h) + ' ' + (cx + w*0.5) + ',' + (y - h*0.5) + ' C' + cx + ',' + (y - h*0.2) + ' ' + (cx + w) + ',' + y + ' ' + (cx + w) + ',' + y;
      else if (t === 1) d += ' L' + (cx + w*0.3) + ',' + (y - h) + ' Q' + (cx + w*0.7) + ',' + (y - h*0.4) + ' ' + (cx + w*0.25) + ',' + (y - h*0.55) + ' C' + (cx + w*0.4) + ',' + (y - h*0.2) + ' ' + (cx + w*0.8) + ',' + (y + 1) + ' ' + (cx + w) + ',' + y;
      else if (t === 2) d += ' Q' + (cx + w/2) + ',' + (y - h*0.55) + ' ' + (cx + w) + ',' + y;
      else if (t === 3) d += ' C' + (cx + w*0.3) + ',' + (y + h*0.55) + ' ' + (cx + w*0.95) + ',' + (y + h*0.3) + ' ' + (cx + w*0.5) + ',' + (y - h*0.35) + ' Q' + (cx + w*0.7) + ',' + y + ' ' + (cx + w) + ',' + y;
      else d += ' L' + (cx + w*0.5) + ',' + (y - h*0.9) + ' L' + (cx + w*0.5) + ',' + (y - h*0.2) + ' Q' + (cx + w*0.75) + ',' + (y - h*0.7) + ' ' + (cx + w) + ',' + y;
      if (rnd() < 0.25) dots += '<circle cx="' + (cx + w*0.5) + '" cy="' + (y - h - 3) + '" r="1.3" fill="' + col + '"/>';
      cx += w; if (rnd() < 0.14 && cx < x + len - 20) { cx += 6; d += ' M' + cx + ',' + y; } }
    return '<path d="' + d + '" fill="none" stroke="' + col + '" stroke-width="' + (sw || 1.1) + '" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>' + dots; }
  function glyphs(x, y, n, h, sw, col){ var d = '', cx = x, w = h*0.62;
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) { if (rnd() < 0.12 && i > 0) { cx += w*0.7; continue; }
      var L = cx, Rt = cx + w, Tp = y - h, B = y, Mi = y - h/2, k = 2 + Math.floor(rnd()*3), used = {};
      for (var j = 0; j < k; j++) { var t = Math.floor(rnd()*9); if (used[t]) t = (t + 3) % 9; used[t] = 1;
        if (t === 0) d += 'M' + L + ',' + Tp + 'L' + L + ',' + B; if (t === 1) d += 'M' + Rt + ',' + Tp + 'L' + Rt + ',' + B; if (t === 2) d += 'M' + L + ',' + Tp + 'L' + Rt + ',' + Tp; if (t === 3) d += 'M' + L + ',' + Mi + 'L' + Rt + ',' + Mi;
        if (t === 4) d += 'M' + L + ',' + B + 'L' + Rt + ',' + Tp; if (t === 5) d += 'M' + L + ',' + Tp + 'L' + Rt + ',' + Mi; if (t === 6) d += 'M' + (L + w/2) + ',' + Tp + 'L' + (L + w/2) + ',' + B;
        if (t === 7) d += 'M' + L + ',' + B + 'Q' + (L + w/2) + ',' + Mi + ' ' + Rt + ',' + B; if (t === 8) d += 'M' + L + ',' + Mi + 'Q' + (L + w/2) + ',' + (Tp - h*0.25) + ' ' + Rt + ',' + Mi; }
      if (rnd() < 0.2) d += 'M' + (L + w/2 - 0.01) + ',' + (Tp - h*0.28) + 'L' + (L + w/2) + ',' + (Tp - h*0.28);
      cx += w*1.45; }
    return '<path d="' + d + '" fill="none" stroke="' + (col || R) + '" stroke-width="' + sw + '" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>'; }
  var bin = function(x, y, str, size, anchor, col, weight){ return '<text x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" text-anchor="' + (anchor || 'start') + '" font-family="monospace" font-size="' + size + '" font-weight="' + (weight || 400) + '" fill="' + (col || R) + '" letter-spacing=".5">' + str + '</text>'; };
  var star = function(x, y, r, col){ return '<path d="M' + x + ',' + (y - r) + ' L' + (x + r*0.28) + ',' + (y - r*0.28) + ' L' + (x + r) + ',' + y + ' L' + (x + r*0.28) + ',' + (y + r*0.28) + ' L' + x + ',' + (y + r) + ' L' + (x - r*0.28) + ',' + (y + r*0.28) + ' L' + (x - r) + ',' + y + ' L' + (x - r*0.28) + ',' + (y - r*0.28) + ' Z" fill="' + col + '"/>'; };
  function flyer(x, y, sc, rot){ return '<g transform="translate(' + x + ',' + y + ') rotate(' + (rot || 0) + ') scale(' + sc + ')"><path d="M12,-4 C16,-16 18,-30 14,-44 L11,-38 L13,-35 L8,-31 L10,-27 L5,-23 L6,-18 L1,-14 L2,-9 L-2,-5 Z" fill="' + K + '" opacity=".8"/><ellipse cx="0" cy="0" rx="17" ry="6" fill="' + K + '"/><path d="M-15,-1 L-25,-5 L-23,1 L-26,4 L-14,3 Z" fill="' + K + '"/><path d="M12,-3 Q26,-6 40,-8" stroke="' + K + '" stroke-width="4" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round"/><ellipse cx="42" cy="-8.3" rx="4.5" ry="2.8" fill="' + K + '"/><path d="M45,-9.6 L53,-7.6 L45,-6.4 Z" fill="' + R + '"/><circle cx="43" cy="-9" r=".9" fill="' + R + '"/><path d="M8,-4 C8,-22 2,-40 -12,-54 L-12,-47 L-7,-45 L-14,-40 L-8,-38 L-14,-32 L-9,-29 L-13,-23 L-8,-19 L-10,-13 L-5,-9 L-6,-4 Z" fill="' + K + '"/><path d="M-12,-54 L-13,-49 M-11,-46 L-14,-44 M-9,-38 L-13,-37 M-10,-30 L-13,-29" stroke="' + S + '" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round"/></g>'; }
  var corner = function(x, y, fx, fy){ return '<path d="M' + x + ',' + (y + fy*16) + ' L' + x + ',' + y + ' L' + (x + fx*16) + ',' + y + '" stroke="' + G + '" stroke-width="2.4" fill="none"/><path d="M' + (x + fx*5) + ',' + (y + fy*5) + ' l' + fx*4 + ',' + fy*4 + ' l' + (-fx*4) + ',' + fy*4 + ' l' + (-fx*4) + ',' + (-fy*4) + ' z" fill="' + G + '" transform="translate(' + fx*4 + ',0)"/>'; };
  var g = '<rect x="8" y="8" width="664" height="284" rx="5" fill="' + C + '"/><rect x="8" y="8" width="664" height="284" rx="5" fill="url(#grain)" opacity=".35"/>';
  var p = '<rect x="20" y="20" width="474" height="260" fill="none" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width="2"/><rect x="25.5" y="25.5" width="463" height="249" fill="none" stroke="' + G + '" stroke-width="2"/>';
  p += corner(30, 30, 1, 1) + corner(484, 30, -1, 1) + corner(30, 270, 1, -1) + corner(484, 270, -1, -1) + glyphs(48, 50, 8, 13, 1.8) + star(174, 44, 5, G) + glyphs(184, 50, 4, 13, 1.8) + bin(472, 49, '01001011 01001111', '12', 'end', R, 700);
  p += '<line x1="40" y1="57" x2="474" y2="57" stroke="' + G + '" stroke-width="2.2"/><rect x="36" y="64" width="442" height="146" fill="none" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width="1"/>';
  p += '<circle cx="300" cy="110" r="44" fill="' + M + '"/><circle cx="300" cy="110" r="44" fill="url(#hatch)" opacity=".55"/><circle cx="300" cy="110" r="44" fill="none" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width="1.4"/><circle cx="300" cy="110" r="51" fill="none" stroke="' + G + '" stroke-width="2"/>';
  for (var i = 0; i < 12; i++) { var a = i/12*Math.PI*2 + 0.13; p += '<line x1="' + (300 + Math.cos(a)*55) + '" y1="' + (110 + Math.sin(a)*55) + '" x2="' + (300 + Math.cos(a)*63) + '" y2="' + (110 + Math.sin(a)*63) + '" stroke="' + G + '" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="square"/>'; }
  p += '<g stroke="' + R + '" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M442,178 L442,128" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M442,150 Q432,142 424,130 M442,146 Q452,138 460,128 M442,138 Q436,128 432,118 M442,136 Q448,126 452,116" stroke-width="1.1"/><path d="M412,130 A14,12 0 0 1 424,110 A18,15 0 0 1 460,110 A14,12 0 0 1 472,130 Q442,138 412,130 Z" stroke-width="1.2"/><path d="M442,178 Q432,181 424,180 M442,178 Q452,181 460,180 M442,178 Q441,182 438,185" stroke-width="1"/></g>';
  p += star(442, 100, 6, S); [[70, 86], [120, 74], [250, 76], [380, 80], [60, 140]].forEach(function(q, i){ p += star(q[0], q[1], i % 2 ? 4 : 5, i % 2 ? S : G); });
  p += bin(44, 78, '1 0 1 0 0 1 1 0 1', '8', null, R) + bin(360, 160, '0 1 1', '7', null, R) + '<line x1="36" y1="186" x2="478" y2="186" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width="1.4"/><line x1="36" y1="190" x2="478" y2="190" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width=".7"/>';
  for (var x = 40; x < 478; x += 10) p += '<line x1="' + x + '" y1="190" x2="' + (x - 3) + '" y2="195" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width=".7"/>';
  p += '<path d="M36,150 L262,150 C288,150 312,158 330,168 L352,178 L330,183 L36,183 Z" fill="' + C + '" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width="1.5"/><line x1="36" y1="171" x2="276" y2="171" stroke="' + S + '" stroke-width="2.4"/><path d="M262,150 C288,150 312,158 330,168 L352,178 L330,183 L272,183 C276,170 272,156 262,150 Z" fill="' + K + '"/><path d="M330,168 L352,178 L330,183 C336,178 336,172 330,168 Z" fill="' + R + '"/><circle cx="292" cy="162" r="3.4" fill="' + S + '"/><circle cx="292" cy="162" r="1.4" fill="' + R + '"/><line x1="36" y1="176" x2="272" y2="176" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width="1"/>';
  for (x = 46; x < 250; x += 22) p += '<rect x="' + x + '" y="156" width="14" height="10" rx="2" fill="none" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width="1"/>';
  [120, 200].forEach(function(xx){ p += '<line x1="' + xx + '" y1="152" x2="' + xx + '" y2="183" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width=".8"/>'; });
  for (x = 60; x < 330; x += 38) p += '<circle cx="' + x + '" cy="184" r="3" fill="' + C + '" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width="1"/>';
  p += '<g stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width=".8" fill="none">'; for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) p += '<path d="M' + (44 + i*14) + ',' + (201 + i*3) + ' q10,-2 20,0 t20,0 t20,0 t20,0"/>'; p += '</g>';
  p += flyer(268, 118, 0.62, -8) + flyer(156, 124, 0.5, -4) + flyer(100, 110, 0.34, -10) + runes(214, 203, 256, 6, 1.8, S);
  p += glyphs(40, 232, 3, 8, 1.1) + '<circle cx="70" cy="228" r="1.5" fill="' + R + '"/>' + glyphs(40, 258, 6, 20, 2.6) + bin(40, 269, '01011001 01000111 01000100', '10', null, R) + glyphs(300, 232, 4, 8, 1.1) + glyphs(300, 254, 5, 13, 1.8) + '<text x="382" y="255" font-family="serif" font-size="20" font-weight="700" fill="' + S + '">∞</text>' + bin(300, 268, '0000 → 0000', '10', null, R) + bin(472, 262, '№ 101001101', '13', 'end', G, 700);
  var st = '<rect x="512" y="20" width="148" height="260" fill="none" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width="2"/><rect x="516" y="20" width="8" height="260" fill="' + S + '"/>' + glyphs(534, 46, 5, 12, 1.7) + '<line x1="530" y1="56" x2="652" y2="56" stroke="' + G + '" stroke-width="2.2"/><circle cx="590" cy="104" r="30" fill="' + M + '"/><circle cx="590" cy="104" r="30" fill="url(#hatch)" opacity=".55"/><circle cx="590" cy="104" r="30" fill="none" stroke="' + R + '" stroke-width="1.2"/><circle cx="590" cy="104" r="36" fill="none" stroke="' + G + '" stroke-width="2"/>' + flyer(580, 116, 0.5, -10);

/* ---- the rest: small extra details ---- */
/* a snowflake drawn by a ministry: six arms (the regulation says six), each
   tipped with a little star, a red five-point star at the heart that doesn't
   fit the count, and one arm with a branch too many */
function pkSovietFlake(cx, cy, r, col, heart){
  var s = '<g stroke="' + col + '" stroke-width="' + (r*0.09) + '" stroke-linecap="round" fill="none">';
  for (var i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    var a = -Math.PI/2 + i*Math.PI/3, ca = Math.cos(a), sa = Math.sin(a), ex = cx + ca*r*0.78, ey = cy + sa*r*0.78;
    s += '<line x1="' + (cx + ca*r*0.24).toFixed(2) + '" y1="' + (cy + sa*r*0.24).toFixed(2) + '" x2="' + ex.toFixed(2) + '" y2="' + ey.toFixed(2) + '"/>';
    var nb = i === 4 ? 3 : 2;   /* the wrong arm */
    for (var b = 0; b < nb; b++) {
      var t = 0.42 + b*0.17, bx = cx + ca*r*t, by = cy + sa*r*t, l = r*(0.22 - b*0.04);
      [1, -1].forEach(function(sg){ var ba = a + sg*0.75; s += '<line x1="' + bx.toFixed(2) + '" y1="' + by.toFixed(2) + '" x2="' + (bx + Math.cos(ba)*l).toFixed(2) + '" y2="' + (by + Math.sin(ba)*l).toFixed(2) + '"/>'; });
    }
  }
  s += '</g>';
  for (i = 0; i < 6; i++) { var a2 = -Math.PI/2 + i*Math.PI/3; s += pkStar5(cx + Math.cos(a2)*r*0.9, cy + Math.sin(a2)*r*0.9, r*0.13, col); }
  return s + '<circle cx="' + cx + '" cy="' + cy + '" r="' + (r*0.27) + '" fill="none" stroke="' + col + '" stroke-width="' + (r*0.05) + '"/>' + pkStar5(cx, cy + r*0.02, r*0.2, heart);
}
PK_ART.bottle.cap = 'газ. вода · снежок. a bottle of it. you only paid for the glass.';
PK_ART.bottle.svg = function(){
  var s = '<path d="M13 8 L23 8 L23 26 C23 31 31 35 31 42 L31 84 C31 87.5 29 89 26 89 L10 89 C7 89 5 87.5 5 84 L5 42 C5 35 13 31 13 26 Z" fill="#7b7874"/>';
  s += '<path d="M14.5 30 C14.5 33 7 36 7 42 L7 84 C7 86 8.5 87 10 87 L26 87 C27.5 87 29 86 29 84 L29 42 C29 36 21.5 33 21.5 30 Z" fill="#9a968f"/>';
  for (var r = 0; r < 4; r++) s += '<path d="M7 ' + (78 + r*2.4) + ' L29 ' + (78 + r*2.4) + '" stroke="#86827c" stroke-width="0.5"/>';   /* moulded rings near the foot */
  s += '<rect x="5" y="45" width="26" height="29" fill="' + PKC.card + '"/><rect x="5" y="45" width="26" height="1.8" fill="' + PKC.rust + '"/><rect x="5" y="72.2" width="26" height="1.8" fill="' + PKC.rust + '"/>';
  s += pkT('СНЕЖОК', 18, 50.8, 3.6, PKC.rust, { w:700, ls:0.6 }) + pkSovietFlake(18, 58.4, 6.4, PKC.plum, PKC.red);

/* ================================================================
   STEP 8 — THE POCKET (doc 20).
   a close-up scene like the reading room: looking down into a dark wool coat
   pocket, things lying loose and a little crooked, faint rings where it keeps
   going down (Mary Poppins: no limit). click a thing: it lifts to the middle,
   bigger and straight, the rest dims; click again and it goes back. things you
   can use ask (drink? / take one?). things too deep don't lift.
   the border: rust and plum frames, a band of glyphs, four seals, and on the
   right the seam: torn, stitched shut with cream x's, code showing through,
   glitching every few seconds.
   open with the pocket in the bottom-left corner, or P. remembered in this
   browser (nullgrey.pocket). the pass is read from nullgrey.between.pass.
   ================================================================ */

/* ---- colours: snapping a drawing into the station's ramps ---- */
var PK_INKS = (function(){
  var list = [];
  ['paper', 'black', 'silver', 'metal', 'brass', 'gold', 'rustm', 'bRust', 'bPlum', 'plumglow', 'wood', 'amber', 'granR'].forEach(function(nm){
    var ri = RIX[nm]; if (ri === undefined) return;
    var n = RN[ri]; for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) { var c = RF[ri*FOGN*RMAX + k]; list.push([ri, (k + 0.5)/n, c & 255, (c >> 8) & 255, (c >> 16) & 255]); }   /* the colours as they actually come out on screen */
  });
  return list;
})();
/* step 11: the hub's olive green too, but only for things that are green (ALIEN WAVE read grey) */
var PK_INKS_G = PK_INKS.concat((function(){ var l = [], ri = RIX.holive; if (ri === undefined) return l; var n = RN[ri]; for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) { var c = RF[ri*FOGN*RMAX + k]; l.push([ri, (k + 0.5)/n, c & 255, (c >> 8) & 255, (c >> 16) & 255]); } return l; })());
/* (Oct 13) loud things (the 1995 coupon) keep loud colours: the Japan tunnel's vivid green and neon gold, plum, rust, white and black. never blue */
var PK_INKS_L = PK_INKS_G.concat((function(){ var l = []; ['pgreen', 'pneon', 'pwhite', 'bPlum', 'bRust'].forEach(function(nm){ var ri = RIX[nm]; if (ri === undefined) return; var n = RN[ri]; for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) { var c = RF[ri*FOGN*RMAX + k]; l.push([ri, (k + 0.5)/n, c & 255, (c >> 8) & 255, (c >> 16) & 255]); } }); return l; })());
var PK_INKCL = {};
function pkInkL(r, g, b){
  var key = (r >> 2) << 12 | (g >> 2) << 6 | (b >> 2), c = PK_INKCL[key]; if (c) return c;
  var best = null, bd = 1e9;
  for (var k = 0; k < PK_INKS_L.length; k++) { var q = PK_INKS_L[k], dr = r - q[2], dg = g - q[3], db = b - q[4], d = dr*dr*0.3 + dg*dg*0.59 + db*db*0.11; if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = q; } }
  PK_INKCL[key] = best; return best;
}
var PK_INKC = {}, PK_INKCG = {};
function pkInkG(r, g, b){
  var key = (r >> 2) << 12 | (g >> 2) << 6 | (b >> 2), c = PK_INKCG[key]; if (c) return c;
  var best = null, bd = 1e9;
  for (var k = 0; k < PK_INKS_G.length; k++) { var q = PK_INKS_G[k], dr = r - q[2], dg = g - q[3], db = b - q[4], d = dr*dr*0.3 + dg*dg*0.59 + db*db*0.11; if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = q; } }
  PK_INKCG[key] = best; return best;
}
function pkInk(r, g, b){
  var key = (r >> 2) << 12 | (g >> 2) << 6 | (b >> 2), c = PK_INKC[key]; if (c) return c;
  var best = null, bd = 1e9;
  for (var k = 0; k < PK_INKS.length; k++) { var q = PK_INKS[k], dr = r - q[2], dg = g - q[3], db = b - q[4], d = dr*dr*0.3 + dg*dg*0.59 + db*db*0.11; if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = q; } }
  PK_INKC[key] = best; return best;
}
/* an svg turned into a map of (ramp, value) at its lifted size */
function pkMap(id, done){
  var A = PK_ART[id], w = Math.round(A.vb[0]*A.k), h = Math.round(A.vb[1]*A.k), img = new Image();
  img.onload = function(){
    var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h;
    var x = c.getContext('2d'); x.imageSmoothingEnabled = true; x.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high'; x.drawImage(img, 0, 0, w, h);
    var d = x.getImageData(0, 0, w, h).data, RI = new Int16Array(w*h), V = new Float32Array(w*h);
    var ink = A.ink || (A.loud ? pkInkL : (A.green ? pkInkG : pkInk));   /* (Oct 14) a thing can bring its own inks (the red paper unicorn) */
    for (var i = 0; i < w*h; i++) { if (d[i*4 + 3] < 120) { RI[i] = -1; continue; } var q = ink(d[i*4], d[i*4 + 1], d[i*4 + 2]); RI[i] = q[0]; V[i] = q[1]; }
    POK.maps[id] = { w:w, h:h, RI:RI, V:V }; done();
  };
  img.onerror = function(){ done(); };
  img.src = 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent('<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 ' + A.vb[0] + ' ' + A.vb[1] + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + h + '">' + A.svg() + '</svg>');
}
/* draw one thing: scale (of its lifted size), turn (radians), light (0..1+).
   returns a canvas, where its middle is, and a mask for clicking */
function pkSprite(id, sc, rot, light, now){
  var M = POK.maps[id], A = PK_ART[id]; if (!M) return null;
  var cs = Math.cos(rot), sn = Math.sin(rot), hw = M.w*sc/2, hh = M.h*sc/2;
  var W = Math.ceil(Math.abs(hw*cs) + Math.abs(hh*sn))*2 + 2, H = Math.ceil(Math.abs(hw*sn) + Math.abs(hh*cs))*2 + 2;
  var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = W; c.height = H;
  var x = c.getContext('2d'), id2 = x.createImageData(W, H), d = new Uint32Array(id2.data.buffer), mask = new Uint8Array(W*H);
  var ss = sc < 0.7, foil = A.foil && now, ft = now ? now/1000 : 0, inv = 1/sc;
  for (var j = 0; j < H; j++) for (var i = 0; i < W; i++) {
    var ri = -1, v = 0, n = 0, offs = ss ? [[-0.25, -0.25], [0.25, -0.25], [-0.25, 0.25], [0.25, 0.25]] : [[0, 0]];
    for (var o = 0; o < offs.length; o++) {
      var dx = i + 0.5 + offs[o][0] - W/2, dy = j + 0.5 + offs[o][1] - H/2;
      var mx = (dx*cs + dy*sn)*inv + M.w/2, my = (-dx*sn + dy*cs)*inv + M.h/2;
      if (mx < 0 || my < 0 || mx >= M.w || my >= M.h) continue;
      var k = (my | 0)*M.w + (mx | 0), r = M.RI[k]; if (r < 0) continue;
      if (ri < 0) ri = r; v += M.V[k]; n++;
      if (foil && o === 0 && (r === R_SILVER || r === R_GOLD || r === R_PLUMGLOW)) v += 0.32*Math.max(0, 1 - Math.abs(frac(mx/M.w*0.8 + my/M.h*0.5 - ft*0.22) - 0.5)*9);   /* the foil catches the light as it moves */
    }
    if (ri < 0 || n < offs.length/2) continue;
    v = v/n*light; var nR = RN[ri], b = BAY[((j & 3) << 2) | (i & 3)], idx = (v*(nR - 1) + b) | 0; if (idx >= nR) idx = nR - 1; if (idx < 0) idx = 0;
    d[j*W + i] = RF[ri*FOGN*RMAX + idx]; mask[j*W + i] = 1;
  }
  x.putImageData(id2, 0, 0);
  return { c:c, W:W, H:H, mask:mask };
}
function pkShadow(S){
  var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = S.W + 4; c.height = S.H + 4;
  var x = c.getContext('2d'), id = x.createImageData(c.width, c.height), d = id.data;
  for (var j = 0; j < c.height; j++) for (var i = 0; i < c.width; i++) {
    var on = 0; for (var dj = -2; dj <= 0 && !on; dj++) for (var di = -2; di <= 0; di++) { var si = i + di, sj = j + dj; if (si >= 0 && sj >= 0 && si < S.W && sj < S.H && S.mask[sj*S.W + si]) { on = 1; break; } }
    if (on) { var k = (j*c.width + i)*4; d[k] = 4; d[k + 1] = 3; d[k + 2] = 5; d[k + 3] = 255; }
  }
  x.putImageData(id, 0, 0); return c;
}
/* text cut out as hard pixels (for the code in the seam, and code names) */
function pkTextSprite(lines, px, col, lh){
  var c = document.createElement('canvas'), x = c.getContext('2d'); x.font = px + 'px monospace';
  var w = 0; lines.forEach(function(l){ w = Math.max(w, Math.ceil(x.measureText(l).width)); });
  c.width = w + 2; c.height = lines.length*lh + 2; x = c.getContext('2d'); x.font = px + 'px monospace'; x.fillStyle = col; x.textBaseline = 'top';
  lines.forEach(function(l, i){ x.fillText(l, 1, 1 + i*lh); });
  var id = x.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height), d = id.data; for (var k = 3; k < d.length; k += 4) d[k] = d[k] > 110 ? 255 : 0;
  x.putImageData(id, 0, 0); return c;
}

/* ---- the pocket itself: wool, the lip, the rings going down, the frame ---- */
function pkInside(x, y){ if (x < 64 || x > 704 || y < 78) return false; if (y < 276) return true; var ex = (x - 384)/320, ey = (y - 266)/100; return ex*ex + ey*ey <= 1; }
function pkEdge(x, y){ if (pkInside(x - 8, y) && pkInside(x + 8, y) && pkInside(x, y - 8) && pkInside(x, y + 8) && pkInside(x - 6, y + 6) && pkInside(x + 6, y + 6)) return 99; var e = 99; for (var a = 0; a < 16; a++) { var an = a/16*Math.PI*2, xx = x + Math.cos(an)*7, yy = y + Math.sin(an)*7; if (!pkInside(xx, yy)) { var dd = 7; for (var r = 1; r < 7; r++) if (!pkInside(x + Math.cos(an)*r, y + Math.sin(an)*r)) { dd = r; break; } e = Math.min(e, dd); } } return e; }
var PK_GLYPHS = 'ДОМЖЯФЭЮЩЛИКУРХБウキヨポケットイマスラナᚠᚢᚦᚨᚱᚲᚷᚹᚺᚾᛁᛃᛇᛈᛉᛊᛏᛒᛖᛗᛚᛜᛞᛟ';
function pkBand(n){ var s = ''; for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) { var t = Math.random(); s += t < 0.62 ? PK_GLYPHS[(Math.random()*PK_GLYPHS.length) | 0] : (Math.random() < 0.5 ? '0' : '1'); if (Math.random() < 0.1) s += '·'; } return s; }
function pkSeal(x, y){ return '<g transform="translate(' + x + ',' + y + ')"><circle r="12" fill="#0a0908" stroke="#8a5240" stroke-width="1.6"/><circle cx="-4.2" cy="2.2" r="2.8" fill="none" stroke="#e4dcd0" stroke-width="1"/><circle cx="4.2" cy="2.2" r="2.8" fill="none" stroke="#e4dcd0" stroke-width="1"/><circle cx="0" cy="-4.2" r="2.8" fill="none" stroke="#e4dcd0" stroke-width="1"/><circle cx="0" cy="-9" r="1" fill="#d9a441"/><circle cx="-8" cy="5.4" r="1" fill="#d9a441"/><circle cx="8" cy="5.4" r="1" fill="#d9a441"/></g>'; }
var PK_TEAR = [[741, 146], [737, 166], [745, 186], [736, 206], [744, 226], [737, 246], [743, 266], [739, 288]];
function pkFrameSvg(){
  var g = '<text x="384" y="25" text-anchor="middle" style="font-family:monospace;font-size:10px;fill:#8c8983;letter-spacing:2.6px">' + pkBand(62) + '</text>';
  g += '<text x="384" y="414" text-anchor="middle" style="font-family:monospace;font-size:10px;fill:#8c8983;letter-spacing:2.6px">' + pkBand(62) + '</text>';
  g += '<text transform="translate(25,216) rotate(-90)" text-anchor="middle" style="font-family:monospace;font-size:10px;fill:#8c8983;letter-spacing:2.6px">' + pkBand(30) + '</text>';
  g += '<text transform="translate(745,84) rotate(90)" text-anchor="middle" style="font-family:monospace;font-size:10px;fill:#8c8983;letter-spacing:2.6px">' + pkBand(5) + '</text>';
  g += '<text transform="translate(745,352) rotate(90)" text-anchor="middle" style="font-family:monospace;font-size:10px;fill:#8c8983;letter-spacing:2.6px">' + pkBand(8) + '</text>';
  var s = '<rect x="12" y="12" width="744" height="408" rx="4" fill="none" stroke="#8a5240" stroke-width="2.4"/><rect x="31" y="31" width="706" height="370" rx="3" fill="none" stroke="#5c4a63" stroke-width="1.8"/>' + g;
  s += '<text x="384" y="62" text-anchor="middle" style="font-family:Georgia,serif;font-size:16px;fill:#e4dcd0;letter-spacing:3.5px">ポケット · your pocket</text>';
  s += pkSeal(22, 22) + pkSeal(746, 22) + pkSeal(22, 410) + pkSeal(746, 410);
  var p = 'M' + PK_TEAR.map(function(q){ return q[0] + ',' + q[1]; }).join(' L');
  s += '<path d="' + p + '" stroke="#0a0908" stroke-width="17" fill="none" stroke-linejoin="bevel"/><path d="' + p + '" stroke="#8a5240" stroke-width="1.2" fill="none"/>';
  for (var y = 160; y < 284; y += 17) s += '<path d="M731,' + (y - 5) + ' L749,' + (y + 5) + ' M749,' + (y - 5) + ' L731,' + (y + 5) + '" stroke="#e4dcd0" stroke-width="1.5"/>';
  return s;
}
function pkBuildBg(done){
  var img = new Image();
  img.onload = function(){
    var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = PW; c.height = PH;
    var x = c.getContext('2d'); x.drawImage(img, 0, 0, PW, PH);
    var sv = x.getImageData(0, 0, PW, PH).data, id = x.createImageData(PW, PH), d = new Uint32Array(id.data.buffer);
    for (var j = 0; j < PH; j++) for (var i = 0; i < PW; i++) {
      var k = j*PW + i, ri, v;
      if (sv[k*4 + 3] > 128) { var q = pkInk(sv[k*4], sv[k*4 + 1], sv[k*4 + 2]); ri = q[0]; v = q[1]; }
      else if (j >= 70 && j < 92 && i > 52 && i < 716) {   /* the lip: a fold of wool along the top, stitched */
        ri = R_BPLUM; var lt = (j - 70)/22; v = 0.2 + 0.1*Math.sin(lt*Math.PI) - (lt > 0.85 ? 0.12 : 0) + (lt < 0.1 ? 0.08 : 0);
        v += (frac((i + j)*0.34) < 0.5 ? 0.025 : -0.01) + (hsh(i, j + 3) - 0.5)*0.05;
        if (Math.abs(j - 84) < 1 && frac(i/9) < 0.55) { ri = R_SILVER; v = 0.36; }
      } else if (pkInside(i, j)) {   /* the wool inside, darker the further down it goes */
        ri = R_BPLUM;
        var dd;
        var ex = (i - 384)/330, ey = (j - 292)/112; dd = Math.sqrt(ex*ex + ey*ey);
        v = 0.105 + (frac((i*0.5 + j*0.5)/2.6) < 0.5 ? 0.028 : 0) + (hsh(i + 17, j) - 0.5)*0.06 + (vn(i*0.05, j*0.07) - 0.5)*0.06 + (vn(i*0.6, j*0.25) - 0.5)*0.03;
        v *= 0.12 + 0.88*Math.pow(Math.min(1, dd), 0.9);
        [0.8, 0.58, 0.38, 0.2].forEach(function(r, n){ var q = Math.abs(dd - r); if (q < 0.018) v += (0.05 - n*0.007)*(1 - q/0.018); });
        var e = pkEdge(i, j); if (e < 7) v *= 0.55 + 0.45*e/7;
        if (e > 8.5 && e < 10 && j > 92 && frac((i + j*0.3)/8) < 0.5 && Math.abs(dd - 0.9) > 0.02) { ri = R_PLUMGLOW; v = 0.18; }   /* the dashed seam inside */
      } else { ri = R_BLACK; v = (i > 31 && i < 737 && j > 31 && j < 401) ? 0.07 + (hsh(i, j + 9) - 0.5)*0.03 : 0.035; }
      var nR = RN[ri], b = BAY[((j & 3) << 2) | (i & 3)], ix = (Math.max(0, v)*(nR - 1) + b) | 0; if (ix >= nR) ix = nR - 1;
      d[k] = RF[ri*FOGN*RMAX + ix];
    }
    x.putImageData(id, 0, 0); POK.bg = c; done();
  };
  img.onerror = function(){ done(); };
  img.src = 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent('<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 ' + PW + ' ' + PH + '" width="' + PW + '" height="' + PH + '">' + pkFrameSvg() + '</svg>');
}

/* ---- where things lie: the front first, then further down ---- */
var PK_SLOTS = [
  [170, 172, -0.16, 0], [332, 150, 0.1, 0], [505, 162, 0.2, 0], [626, 150, -0.07, 0], [214, 286, -0.24, 0], [614, 274, 0.14, 0],
  [300, 248, 0.08, 1], [462, 246, -0.17, 1], [392, 196, 0.05, 1], [540, 300, 0.12, 1], [596, 318, 0.3, 1],
  [330, 306, -0.1, 2], [446, 310, 0.15, 2], [262, 326, 0.07, 2], [512, 330, -0.13, 2], [384, 286, 0.2, 2]
];
var PK_DEPTH = [[1, 0.9], [0.8, 0.72], [0.64, 0.52], [0.5, 0.36]];
function pkList(){
  var L = KASSA.pass ? ['passport', 'multipass'] : [], got = PK_HAVE.filter(function(id){ return !!PK_ART[id]; }).reverse();   /* newest on top: older things sink */
  if (PASS_STATE === 2) got.unshift('pass');
  L = L.concat(got);
  L.push('note', 'lint');
  return L;
}
/* pages (Aisling): twelve things a layer; past that the pocket goes deeper.
   the Kodama ticket is always at the very bottom of the last layer */
var PK_PER = 12;
function pkPages(){ return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(pkList().length/PK_PER)); }
function pkLayout(){
  var L = pkList(), out = [], pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(L.length/PK_PER));
  POK.page = Math.min(POK.page || 0, pages - 1); POK.pages = pages;
  L.slice(POK.page*PK_PER, (POK.page + 1)*PK_PER).forEach(function(id, n){
    var s = PK_SLOTS[n], j = POK.page*7;   /* each layer lies a little differently */
    out.push({ id:id, x:s[0] + (hsh(n + j, 21) - 0.5)*(j ? 26 : 0), y:s[1] + (hsh(n + j, 22) - 0.5)*(j ? 14 : 0), rot:s[2] + (j ? (hsh(n + j, 23) - 0.5)*0.4 : 0), depth:s[3], n:n });
  });
  if (POK.page === pages - 1) out.push({ id:'kodama', x:384, y:336, rot:-0.07, depth:3, deep:true, n:99 });
  out.sort(function(a, b){ return b.depth - a.depth || a.n - b.n; });   /* deeper drawn first */
  out.forEach(function(it){
    var D = PK_DEPTH[it.depth], A = PK_ART[it.id];
    it.sc = A.lie*D[0]; it.light = (it.deep ? 0.32 : D[1])*(1 - 0.08*Math.min(3, POK.page || 0));
    it.S = pkSprite(it.id, it.sc, it.rot, it.light); it.sh = it.S ? pkShadow(it.S) : null;
    it.Sh = null;   /* the hover version, made when needed */
  });
  POK.items = out;
  pkNav();
}
/* going deeper / back up */
var pkDeep = document.createElement('div'); pkDeep.id = 'pk-nav';
pkDeep.innerHTML = '<button type="button" data-d="-1">↑ back up · выше</button><span id="pk-layer"></span><button type="button" data-d="1">↓ deeper · глубже</button>';
pkEl.appendChild(pkDeep);
(function(){ var st = document.createElement('style'); st.textContent = '#pk-nav{position:absolute;right:6%;bottom:8.6%;display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center;z-index:3;}#pk-nav button{font-family:inherit;font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.16em;color:rgba(228,220,208,.5);background:none;border:0;cursor:pointer;padding:2px 0;}#pk-nav button:hover{color:rgba(228,220,208,.95);}#pk-nav button[hidden]{display:none;}#pk-layer{font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.2em;color:rgba(140,137,131,.7);}@media (max-width:640px){#pk-nav{bottom:4%;right:4%;}}'; document.head.appendChild(st); })();
pkDeep.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); });
pkDeep.addEventListener('pointerup', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); });
pkDeep.addEventListener('click', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); var b = e.target.closest('button'); if (b) pkTurn(+b.getAttribute('data-d')); });
function pkNav(){
  var bs = pkDeep.querySelectorAll('button'), P = POK.pages || 1, p = POK.page || 0;
  bs[0].hidden = p <= 0; bs[1].hidden = p >= P - 1;
  document.getElementById('pk-layer').textContent = P > 1 ? (p + 1) + ' / ' + P : '';
}
function pkTurn(d){
  var np = (POK.page || 0) + d; if (!POK.ready || POK.lift || POK.turn || np < 0 || np >= (POK.pages || 1)) return;
  POK.turn = { t0:performance.now(), d:d, np:np, swapped:false }; POK.hover = -1;
  pkCap.textContent = d > 0 ? 'you reach further down.' : 'you bring something back up.';
}

/* ---- building it (the first time it's opened, or quietly after load) ---- */
function pkBuild(done){
  if (POK.ready) { if (done) done(); return; }
  if (POK.building) { POK.after = done; return; }
  POK.building = true; POK.after = done;
  var ids = Object.keys(PK_ART), left = ids.length + 1;
  function one(){ if (--left > 0) return; pkLayout(); POK.ready = true; POK.building = false; drawPkIcon(); var f = POK.after; POK.after = null; if (f) f(); }
  pkBuildBg(one); ids.forEach(function(id){ pkMap(id, one); });
}
function pkRelayout(){ if (POK.ready) pkLayout(); }

/* ---- opening and closing ---- */
function pkOpen(){
  if (POK.open || CU.open || LOOK.open) return;
  POK.open = true; drag = null; keys = {}; POK.lift = null; POK.ask = null; POK.turn = null; pkAsk.style.display = 'none';
  if (POK.page) { POK.page = 0; if (POK.ready) pkLayout(); }
  setCaption('', 0); walkAskHide();
  pkCap.textContent = POK.ready ? 'tap something.' : 'you reach into your pocket…';
  pkEl.classList.add('open'); pkBtn.style.display = 'none'; stage.classList.add('pk-open');
  POK.glitchAt = performance.now() + 2500 + Math.random()*3000; POK.nameAt = performance.now() + 20000 + Math.random()*25000;
  pkBuild(function(){ if (POK.open && !POK.lift) pkCap.textContent = 'tap something.'; });
}
function pkClose(){ POK.open = false; POK.lift = null; POK.ask = null; pkAsk.style.display = 'none'; pkEl.classList.remove('open'); pkBtn.style.display = ''; stage.classList.remove('pk-open'); }
pkBtn.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); });
pkBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); pkOpen(); });
document.getElementById('pk-back').addEventListener('click', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); pkClose(); });
pkEl.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); });
var pkWheelAt = 0;
pkEl.addEventListener('wheel', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); var t = performance.now(); if (t - pkWheelAt < 500 || Math.abs(e.deltaY) < 8) return; pkWheelAt = t; pkTurn(e.deltaY > 0 ? 1 : -1); }, { passive:false });
function pkPos(e){ var r = pkCv.getBoundingClientRect(), s = Math.min(r.width/PW, r.height/PH), ox = r.left + (r.width - PW*s)/2, oy = r.top + (r.height - PH*s)/2; return [(e.clientX - ox)/s, (e.clientY - oy)/s]; }
function pkHit(p){
  if (POK.turn) return -1;
  for (var n = POK.items.length - 1; n >= 0; n--) {
    var it = POK.items[n], S = it.S; if (!S) continue;
    var i = Math.floor(p[0] - (it.x - S.W/2)), j = Math.floor(p[1] - (it.y + pkFloat(it) - S.H/2));
    if (i >= 0 && j >= 0 && i < S.W && j < S.H && S.mask[j*S.W + i]) return n;
  }
  return -1;
}
pkEl.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e){
  e.stopPropagation();
  if (!POK.ready || POK.lift) { pkCv.style.cursor = POK.lift ? HANDS.pointer : ''; return; }
  var h = pkHit(pkPos(e)); POK.hover = h; pkCv.style.cursor = h >= 0 ? HANDS.pointer : '';
});
pkEl.addEventListener('pointerup', function(e){
  e.stopPropagation();
  if (e.target.tagName === 'BUTTON' || !POK.ready) return;
  if (POK.lift) { if (!POK.ask && POK.lift.t1 < 0 && POK.lift.done) pkPutBack(); return; }
  var h = pkHit(pkPos(e)); if (h < 0) return;
  var it = POK.items[h];
  if (it.deep) { setTextDeir(pkCap, PK_ART[it.id].cap); return; }
  pkLift(it);
});
window.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
  if (e.target && e.target.tagName === 'INPUT') return;
  var k = (e.key || '').toLowerCase();
  if (POK.open) {
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    if (!POK.lift && (k === 'arrowdown' || k === 'pagedown' || k === 's')) { e.preventDefault(); pkTurn(1); return; }
    if (!POK.lift && (k === 'arrowup' || k === 'pageup' || k === 'w')) { e.preventDefault(); pkTurn(-1); return; }
    if (k === 'escape' || k === 'p' || k === 'backspace') { e.preventDefault(); if (POK.lift && !POK.ask) pkPutBack(); else if (!POK.lift) pkClose(); }
    return;
  }
  if (k === 'p' && !e.repeat && !CU.open && !LOOK.open && FIGTALK.i < 0) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation(); pkOpen(); }
}, true);

/* ---- lifting, putting back, using ---- */
function pkLift(it){
  var A = PK_ART[it.id];
  POK.lift = { it:it, t0:performance.now(), t1:-1, done:false }; if (PK_ART[it.id] && PK_ART[it.id].onLift) PK_ART[it.id].onLift(POK.lift);
  pkCap.textContent = '';
  if (it.id === 'newspaper') A.cap = 'между · between. ' + paperHint();
}
function pkPutBack(msg){
  if (!POK.lift) return;
  POK.lift.t1 = performance.now(); POK.lift.msg = msg;
  pkAsk.style.display = 'none'; POK.ask = null;
}
pkAsk.addEventListener('click', function(e){
  e.stopPropagation(); var b = e.target.closest('button'); if (!b || !POK.ask) return;
  var yes = b.getAttribute('data-a') === '1', it = POK.lift && POK.lift.it, A = it && PK_ART[it.id];
  pkAsk.style.display = 'none'; POK.ask = null;
  if (!it) return;
  if (!yes) { pkPutBack('maybe later.'); return; }
  if (A.gone) {   /* drunk: it fizzes away */
    POK.lift.fizz = performance.now(); pkCap.textContent = A.yes;
    var k = PK_HAVE.indexOf(it.id); if (k >= 0) PK_HAVE.splice(k, 1); pkSave();
  } else { pkCap.textContent = A.yes; POK.lift.used = true; if (A.onYes) A.onYes(it, POK.lift); }   /* (Oct 14) a thing can change when used: the compact opens */
});
function pkFloat(it){   /* the 2002 quarter, now and then, floats up instead of settling */
  if (it.id !== 'quarter') return 0;
  var t = performance.now()/1000, c = frac(t/23);
  return c > 0.8 ? -Math.sin((c - 0.8)/0.2*Math.PI)*9 : 0;
}

/* ---- every frame while it's open ---- */
var PK_CODE = null;
function pkFrame(now){
  var x = pkCx; x.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
  if (!POK.ready) { x.fillStyle = '#060505'; x.fillRect(0, 0, PW, PH); return; }
  x.drawImage(POK.bg, 0, 0);
  var L = POK.lift, liftIt = L ? L.it : null;
  /* the seam in the border: code through the gap, glitching every few seconds */
  if (!PK_CODE) PK_CODE = pkTextSprite(['0x7Я leak( 1022 4235 23333 ); //seam'], 7, '#d9a441', 8);
  var gl = now > POK.glitchAt && now < POK.glitchAt + 260, jx = gl ? ((now/40) | 0) % 2 ? 3 : -2 : 0;
  if (now > POK.glitchAt + 260) POK.glitchAt = now + 4500 + Math.random()*3500;
  if (gl) { x.drawImage(POK.bg, 726, 140, 30, 160, 726 + jx, 140 + (jx > 0 ? 2 : -1), 30, 160); }
  x.save(); x.translate(746 + (gl ? jx : 0), 150); x.rotate(Math.PI/2); x.globalAlpha = gl ? 1 : 0.5;   /* the code, read down the tear */
  x.drawImage(PK_CODE, Math.round((138 - PK_CODE.width)/2), 0); x.restore(); x.globalAlpha = 1;
  /* the stitches go over it */
  if (!POK.stitch) { POK.stitch = document.createElement('canvas'); POK.stitch.width = 30; POK.stitch.height = 160; POK.stitch.getContext('2d').drawImage(POK.bg, 726, 140, 30, 160, 0, 0, 30, 160); var sd = POK.stitch.getContext('2d').getImageData(0, 0, 30, 160); for (var q = 0; q < sd.data.length; q += 4) if (sd.data[q] + sd.data[q + 1] + sd.data[q + 2] < 330) sd.data[q + 3] = 0; POK.stitch.getContext('2d').putImageData(sd, 0, 0); }
  x.drawImage(POK.stitch, 726 + (gl ? jx : 0), 140);
  /* a code name instead of a thing, for a moment */
  if (now > POK.nameAt && !L) { POK.nameIdx = (Math.random()*POK.items.length) | 0; POK.nameAt = now + 30000 + Math.random()*40000; POK.nameEnd = now + 140; }
  /* turning a layer: the things sink toward the middle and fade; the next layer rises out of it */
  var TU = POK.turn, tf = 0;
  if (TU) {
    var tp = (now - TU.t0)/460;
    if (tp >= 0.5 && !TU.swapped) { TU.swapped = true; POK.page = TU.np; pkLayout(); }
    if (tp >= 1) { POK.turn = null; TU = null; pkCap.textContent = 'tap something.'; }
    else tf = tp < 0.5 ? tp*2 : (1 - tp)*2;
    if (TU) tf = tf*tf*(3 - 2*tf);
  }
  /* deeper layers are darker down there */
  var dk = Math.min(0.42, 0.16*(POK.page || 0));
  if (dk > 0) { x.save(); x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(64, 92); x.lineTo(704, 92); x.lineTo(704, 266); x.ellipse(384, 266, 320, 100, 0, 0, Math.PI); x.closePath(); x.fillStyle = 'rgba(4,3,5,' + dk.toFixed(3) + ')'; x.fill(); x.restore(); }
  /* the things lying in the pocket */
  for (var n = 0; n < POK.items.length; n++) {
    var it = POK.items[n], S = it.S; if (!S) continue;
    if (it === liftIt && !(L.t1 >= 0 && now - L.t1 > 330)) continue;
    var fy = pkFloat(it), ox = Math.round(it.x - S.W/2), oy = Math.round(it.y + fy - S.H/2);
    if (now < POK.nameEnd && n === POK.nameIdx) { var ts = pkTextSprite(['item_' + it.id], 10, '#d9a441', 12); x.drawImage(ts, Math.round(it.x - ts.width/2), Math.round(it.y - 6)); continue; }
    if (tf <= 0) x.globalAlpha = 0.55*(fy < -1 ? 0.5 : 1); else x.globalAlpha = 0;
    x.drawImage(it.sh, ox + 2 - (fy < -1 ? 0 : 0), oy + 3 - Math.round(fy)); x.globalAlpha = 1;
    var img = S.c;
    if (n === POK.hover && !L) { if (!it.Sh) it.Sh = pkSprite(it.id, it.sc, it.rot, it.light*1.22); img = it.Sh.c; }
    if (tf > 0) {   /* mid-turn: smaller, pulled toward the dark middle, fading */
      var dir = TU.d*(TU.swapped ? -1 : 1), k = dir > 0 ? 1 - 0.55*tf : 1 + 0.35*tf, mx = it.x + (384 - it.x)*tf*0.55, my = it.y + (300 - it.y)*tf*0.55;
      x.globalAlpha = 1 - tf; x.drawImage(img, Math.round(mx - S.W*k/2), Math.round(my + fy - S.H*k/2), Math.round(S.W*k), Math.round(S.H*k)); x.globalAlpha = 1;
      continue;
    }
    x.drawImage(img, ox, oy);
  }
  if (L) pkLiftFrame(now, L);
}
function pkEase(t){ return 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3); }
function pkLiftFrame(now, L){
  var x = pkCx, it = L.it, A = PK_ART[it.id], vid = (L.view && POK.maps[L.view]) ? L.view : it.id, M = POK.maps[vid];   /* (Oct 14) a lifted thing can show another view of itself: the compact, opened */
  var big = Math.min(1, 330/M.h, 560/M.w);   /* the lifted size, so it fits */
  var e = L.t1 >= 0 ? 1 - pkEase(Math.min(1, (now - L.t1)/320)) : pkEase(Math.min(1, (now - L.t0)/320));
  if (L.t1 >= 0 && now - L.t1 > 330) {   /* back where it was */
    POK.lift = null; pkCap.textContent = L.msg || 'tap something.'; if (L.fizz) pkGoneAfterDrink(); return;
  }
  x.fillStyle = 'rgba(6,5,5,' + (0.74*e).toFixed(3) + ')'; x.fillRect(0, 0, PW, PH);
  var g = x.createRadialGradient(PKX, PKY, 10, PKX, PKY, 260); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(228,220,208,' + (0.09*e).toFixed(3) + ')'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(228,220,208,0)'); x.fillStyle = g; x.fillRect(0, 0, PW, PH);
  var cx = it.x + (PKX - it.x)*e, cy = it.y + (PKY - it.y)*e, sc = it.sc + (big - it.sc)*e, rot = it.rot*(1 - e), light = it.light + (1.02 - it.light)*e;
  var fz = L.fizz ? Math.min(1, (now - L.fizz)/900) : 0;
  if (fz >= 1) { if (!L.fizzDone) { L.fizzDone = true; L.t1 = now - 330; L.msg = A.yes; } return; }
  var animating = e < 1 || A.foil || fz > 0;
  if (animating || !L.S || L.Sk !== sc || L.Sv !== vid) { L.S = pkSprite(vid, sc, rot, light*(1 - fz*0.9), A.foil ? now : 0); L.Sk = sc; L.Sv = vid; }
  var S = L.S, wob = fz > 0 ? Math.sin(now/30)*1.5 : 0;
  x.drawImage(S.c, Math.round(cx - S.W/2 + wob), Math.round(cy - S.H/2));
  if (fz > 0) {   /* fizz */
    for (var b = 0; b < 26; b++) { var ph = frac(b*0.137 + (now - L.fizz)/700), bx = cx + (hsh(b, 3) - 0.5)*S.W*0.6, by = cy + S.H*0.3 - ph*(S.H*0.9 + 40); x.fillStyle = 'rgba(228,220,208,' + (0.75*(1 - ph)).toFixed(2) + ')'; var r = 1 + (b % 3); x.fillRect(Math.round(bx), Math.round(by), r, r); }
  }
  if (e >= 1 && !L.done) {   /* up: say what it is, or ask */
    L.done = true;
    if (A.use) { POK.ask = A.use; pkCap.textContent = A.use; pkAsk.style.display = 'flex'; }
    else setTextDeir(pkCap, A.cap);
  }
}
function pkGoneAfterDrink(){ pkLayout(); }

/* ---- the little pocket in the corner ---- */
function drawPkIcon(){
  var x = pkIc.getContext('2d'), id = x.createImageData(22, 22), d = new Uint32Array(id.data.buffer);
  for (var j = 0; j < 22; j++) for (var i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
    var ins = i > 2 && i < 19 && j > 3 && (j < 13 || ((i - 10.5)*(i - 10.5))/72 + ((j - 13)*(j - 13))/56 < 1), ri, v;
    if (!ins) continue;
    if (j < 7) { ri = R_BPLUM; v = 0.66 - (j === 6 ? 0.25 : 0); if (j === 5 && i % 3 !== 0) { ri = R_PAPER; v = 0.75; } }
    else { ri = R_BPLUM; v = 0.5 + (((i + j) & 1) ? 0.05 : 0) - (i === 3 || i === 18 ? 0.14 : 0); if (j > 9 && Math.abs(i - 10.5) + (j - 9)*0.2 < 1.2 && j < 17) { ri = R_BRUST; v = 0.75; } }
    var nR = RN[ri], b = BAY[((j & 3) << 2) | (i & 3)], ix = (v*(nR - 1) + b) | 0; if (ix >= nR) ix = nR - 1; d[j*22 + i] = RF[ri*FOGN*RMAX + ix];
  }
  x.putImageData(id, 0, 0);
}
drawPkIcon();

}

};

}

function twCompactShut(){
  var cx = 21, cy = 21, R = 19;
  var s = '<defs><radialGradient id="tcg" cx="0.35" cy="0.3" r="0.8"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#f2f0ea"/><stop offset="0.55" stop-color="#b8b4ac"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#6a6660"/></radialGradient></defs>';
  s += '<circle cx="' + cx + '" cy="' + cy + '" r="' + R + '" fill="url(#tcg)" stroke="#55524c" stroke-width="0.8"/>';
  s += '<path d="M' + (cx - 14) + ' ' + (cy + 4) + ' Q' + cx + ' ' + (cy - 2) + ' ' + (cx + 14) + ' ' + (cy + 6) + '" stroke="#5a5852" stroke-width="2.4" fill="none" opacity="0.25"/>';   /* the room, reflected */
  s += '<path d="M' + (cx - 8) + ' ' + (cy - 5) + ' L' + (cx + 7) + ' ' + (cy - 7) + '" stroke="#6a6660" stroke-width="0.4"/>';   /* the fine line between two stars */
  s += twStarSvg(cx - 8, cy - 5, 5.2) + twStarSvg(cx + 7, cy - 7, 4.8) + twStarSvg(cx + 1, cy + 8, 5.2);
  [[-12, 3], [12, 2], [-2, 0], [6, 12], [-7, 13]].forEach(function(d){ s += twDomeSvg(cx + d[0], cy + d[1], 1.6); });
  return s + '<rect x="' + (cx - 3) + '" y="' + (cy - R - 1.6) + '" width="6" height="2" fill="#8a8680"/>';
}
function twCompactOpen(){
  var s = '<defs><radialGradient id="tcm" cx="0.4" cy="0.35" r="0.75"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#ece8e0"/><stop offset="0.7" stop-color="#a8a49c"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#77736c"/></radialGradient></defs>';
  /* the mirror (left), a soft face-shaped blur in it, and round its rim, very small: the three names */
  s += '<circle cx="21" cy="21" r="19" fill="#8a8680"/><circle cx="21" cy="21" r="16.6" fill="url(#tcm)"/>';
  s += '<ellipse cx="22" cy="20" rx="5.4" ry="7" fill="#c8c0b4" opacity="0.5"/><ellipse cx="22" cy="31" rx="9" ry="4" fill="#9a948a" opacity="0.4"/>';
  s += '<path id="tcr" d="M 21 3.6 A 17.4 17.4 0 1 1 20.9 3.6" fill="none"/><text style="fill:#4a4842;font-family:Georgia,serif;font-size:2.1px;letter-spacing:0.3px"><textPath href="#tcr">the constant · the null · the complete · </textPath></text>';
  /* the screen (right): dark glass, faint lines across, a dead pixel, and burned into it, almost gone: a long number */
  s += '<circle cx="63" cy="21" r="19" fill="#8a8680"/><circle cx="63" cy="21" r="16.6" fill="#1a1c18"/>';
  s += '<rect x="51" y="11" width="24" height="20" rx="2" fill="#22251f" stroke="#3a3c34" stroke-width="0.6"/>';
  for (var k = 0; k < 10; k++) s += '<path d="M51.5 ' + (12 + k*2) + ' L74.5 ' + (12 + k*2) + '" stroke="#2c3028" stroke-width="0.4"/>';
  s += '<text x="63" y="22" text-anchor="middle" style="fill:#3a4034;font-family:monospace;font-size:2.4px;letter-spacing:0.2px;opacity:0.6">1022423523333</text>';
  s += '<rect x="68.6" y="15.2" width="0.7" height="0.7" fill="#6a7060"/><path d="M53 13 L58 12.2" stroke="#4a4e44" stroke-width="0.5" opacity="0.6"/>';
  return s + '<rect x="40" y="18" width="4" height="6" fill="#6a6660"/>';
}
