/* ---- copied from the station: hx4-passport.js (the passport opens; the stamps) ---- */

/* ================================================================
   THE PASSPORT OPENS (Oct 16, doc 17 "Passport"): lift it from the pocket and it asks to be opened. inside, two
   facing pages that shouldn't face each other: 12 and 14. the 13th was never there (the archive gap).
   - page 12, ПРОПИСКА: three photos, all labelled 21, a laminate strip of holographic runes over them, the
     registration stamp МЕЖДУ, срок ∞, series XIII-УК № (your passport number: the moment you bought it)
   - page 14, ОТМЕТКИ: room for the stamps. the ones you've earned are there (so far: Tír na nÓg), the rest are
     faint dashed rings. one line of runes along the foot.
   guilloché behind both, МЕЖДУ in microprint round the edges. drawn like the other pocket things (an svg turned into
   the pocket's own inks), redrawn each time it opens, so a new stamp shows. at the Tír door, lifting it still
   presents it to the aos sí, as before.
   ================================================================ */
var PSPT = { open:false };
function psptNum(){ return KASSA && KASSA.pass ? String(KASSA.pass) : '—'; }
function psptHas(id){
  if (id === 'tir' && typeof TWS !== 'undefined' && TWS.stamped) return true;
  try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('nullgrey.stamps') || '[]').indexOf(id) >= 0; } catch (e) { return false; }
}
function psptGuilloche(x0, y0, w, h, seed){
  var s = '', col = '#cfc3b2';
  for (var k = 0; k < 9; k++) { var d = '', ph = k*0.7 + seed;
    for (var i = 0; i <= 40; i++) { var t = i/40, x = x0 + t*w, y = y0 + h*(0.08 + k*0.105) + Math.sin(t*12.566 + ph)*h*0.035 + Math.sin(t*31.4 + ph*2)*h*0.012; d += (i ? ' L' : 'M') + x.toFixed(1) + ' ' + y.toFixed(1); }
    s += '<path d="' + d + '" fill="none" stroke="' + col + '" stroke-width="0.6"/>'; }
  return s;
}
function psptMicro(x0, y0, w){ var s = '', n = Math.floor(w/17); for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) s += 'МЕЖДУ '; return pkT(s, x0, y0, 3.2, '#b8ab98', { anchor:'start', ls:0.3 }); }
function psptFace(cx, cy){   /* a head-and-shoulders photo, plain: the same person three times */
  return '<rect x="' + (cx - 19) + '" y="' + (cy - 24) + '" width="38" height="48" fill="#b7aa98"/>' +
    '<path d="M' + (cx - 17) + ' ' + (cy + 24) + ' q2 -14 17 -15 q15 1 17 15 Z" fill="#4a3d44"/>' +
    '<ellipse cx="' + cx + '" cy="' + (cy - 5) + '" rx="8" ry="10" fill="#6b5a5e"/>' +
    '<path d="M' + (cx - 9) + ' ' + (cy - 3) + ' q-1 -15 9 -16 q10 1 9 16 q-2 -9 -9 -10 q-7 1 -9 10 Z" fill="#2a2126"/>';
}
function psptTirStamp(cx, cy, k){   /* the Tír na nÓg stamp, the same drawing that thumps in at the door, smaller */
  var c = '#2f6e4f', g = '<g transform="translate(' + cx + ',' + cy + ') rotate(-9) scale(' + k + ') translate(-75,-75)" opacity="0.88">';
  g += '<ellipse cx="75" cy="75" rx="68" ry="60" fill="none" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="4"/><ellipse cx="75" cy="75" rx="62" ry="54" fill="none" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="1.6"/>';
  g += '<circle cx="75" cy="72" r="11" fill="none" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="3"/><path d="M61 76 q-9 -2 -12 6 q9 3 12 -6 M89 76 q9 -2 12 6 q-9 3 -12 -6" fill="' + c + '"/>';
  g += '<path d="M42 96 q8 -5 16 0 t16 0 t16 0 t16 0" fill="none" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="2.4"/>';
  g += '<text x="75" y="46" text-anchor="middle" style="fill:' + c + ';font-family:monospace;font-size:15px;font-weight:700">Tír na nÓg</text><text x="75" y="124" text-anchor="middle" style="fill:' + c + ';font-family:monospace;font-size:11px">nothing grows old</text></g>';
  return g;
}
function psptFuruStamp(cx, cy, k){   /* (Oct 26) Furusato's stamp, from the station-stamp stand on its platform: a wind bell (風鈴), its paper strip, a little moon */
  var c = '#854F0B', g = '<g transform="translate(' + cx + ',' + cy + ') rotate(6) scale(' + k + ') translate(-75,-75)" opacity="0.88">';
  g += '<circle cx="75" cy="75" r="66" fill="none" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="4"/><circle cx="75" cy="75" r="59" fill="none" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="1.6"/>';
  g += '<path d="M75 50 V56 M60 70 Q60 56 75 56 Q90 56 90 70 L92 74 L58 74 Z" fill="none" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="3" stroke-linejoin="round"/>';   /* the bell */
  g += '<path d="M66 64 Q70 60 75 61" fill="none" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="1.6"/><path d="M75 74 V82" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="1.6"/><circle cx="75" cy="84" r="2.6" fill="' + c + '"/>';   /* a painted curl, the clapper */
  g += '<path d="M75 86 V90 M69 90 H81 L80 108 L70 108 Z" fill="none" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linejoin="round"/>';   /* the paper strip that catches the wind */
  g += '<path d="M101 50 A7 7 0 1 0 107 60 A5.4 5.4 0 1 1 101 50 Z" fill="' + c + '"/>';   /* a little moon */
  g += '<text x="75" y="43" text-anchor="middle" style="fill:' + c + ';font-family:sans-serif;font-size:14px;font-weight:700">ふるさと</text><text x="75" y="127" text-anchor="middle" style="fill:' + c + ';font-family:sans-serif;font-size:10px">おかえり</text></g>';
  return g;
}
PK_ART.passportOpen = { vb:[400, 280], k:1.18, lie:0.3, hub:true, green:true, svg:function(){
  var P = '#e4dcd0', ink = '#3a2f33', rust = '#8a5240', s = '<defs>' + pkHolo('hp') + '</defs>';
  s += '<rect x="0" y="0" width="400" height="280" rx="9" fill="#17121d"/>';                                         /* the cover, open, behind */
  s += '<rect x="7" y="7" width="192" height="266" rx="3" fill="' + P + '"/><rect x="201" y="7" width="192" height="266" rx="3" fill="' + P + '"/>';
  s += psptGuilloche(10, 10, 186, 260, 1.3) + psptGuilloche(204, 10, 186, 260, 4.1);
  s += '<rect x="193" y="7" width="14" height="266" fill="#c9bdab" opacity="0.55"/><line x1="200" y1="7" x2="200" y2="273" stroke="#8a7d6c" stroke-width="1"/>';   /* the gutter */
  s += psptMicro(14, 16, 180) + psptMicro(14, 268, 180) + psptMicro(208, 16, 180) + psptMicro(208, 268, 180);
  /* page 12: ПРОПИСКА */
  s += pkT('ПРОПИСКА', 103, 36, 15, ink, { font:'Georgia,serif', w:700, ls:2 }) + pkT('registration · 登録', 103, 48, 7, '#6a5d58', { font:'Georgia,serif' });
  [44, 103, 162].forEach(function(cx){ s += '<rect x="' + (cx - 22) + '" y="58" width="44" height="58" fill="none" stroke="#6a5d58" stroke-width="1"/>' + psptFace(cx, 86) + pkT('21', cx, 128, 10, ink, { font:'monospace', w:700 }); });
  s += '<rect x="18" y="66" width="170" height="13" fill="url(#hp)" opacity="0.32"/>' + pkRuneRow(24, 77, 17, 'hp', 3, false, 1.1);   /* the laminate strip, over the photos */
  s += '<g transform="rotate(-6 120 172)"><rect x="70" y="148" width="104" height="44" fill="none" stroke="' + rust + '" stroke-width="2.6"/><rect x="74" y="152" width="96" height="36" fill="none" stroke="' + rust + '" stroke-width="0.9"/>' +
       pkT('МЕЖДУ', 122, 176, 19, rust, { font:'Arial Black,Impact,sans-serif', ls:3 }) + '</g>';                       /* the registration stamp */
  s += pkT('срок:', 22, 214, 9, ink, { anchor:'start', font:'Georgia,serif' }) + pkT('∞', 58, 216, 15, ink, { anchor:'start', font:'Georgia,serif' });
  s += pkT('серия XIII-УК', 22, 236, 8, ink, { anchor:'start', font:'monospace' }) + pkT('№ ' + psptNum(), 22, 248, 8.5, ink, { anchor:'start', font:'monospace', w:700 });
  s += '<circle cx="172" cy="232" r="13" fill="url(#hp)" opacity="0.5"/><circle cx="172" cy="232" r="13" fill="none" stroke="#9c8fb8" stroke-width="0.8"/>' + pkStar(172, 232, 5, '#d8d6d0');   /* the round hologram seal */
  s += pkT('12', 18, 262, 8, '#6a5d58', { anchor:'start', font:'Georgia,serif' });
  /* page 14: ОТМЕТКИ */
  s += pkT('ОТМЕТКИ', 297, 36, 15, ink, { font:'Georgia,serif', w:700, ls:2 }) + pkT('stamps · 印', 297, 48, 7, '#6a5d58', { font:'Georgia,serif' });
  var slots = [[250, 92], [344, 92], [250, 168], [344, 168]];
  slots.forEach(function(p){ s += '<ellipse cx="' + p[0] + '" cy="' + p[1] + '" rx="36" ry="31" fill="none" stroke="#b5a896" stroke-width="0.9" stroke-dasharray="3 3"/>'; });
  if (psptHas('tir')) s += psptTirStamp(250, 92, 0.52);
  if (psptHas('furusato')) s += psptFuruStamp(344, 92, 0.5);
  s += pkRuneRow(214, 238, 19, 'hp', 11, false, 1.1);
  s += pkT('14', 382, 262, 8, '#6a5d58', { anchor:'end', font:'Georgia,serif' });
  return s;
} };

(function(){
  var A = PK_ART.passport; if (!A) return;
  var dU = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(A, 'use'), dY = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(A, 'yes'), on1 = A.onYes, cap0 = A.cap;
  var gU = function(){ return dU ? (dU.get ? dU.get.call(A) : dU.value) : undefined; }, gY = function(){ return dY ? (dY.get ? dY.get.call(A) : dY.value) : undefined; };
  var door = function(){ return typeof TWD !== 'undefined' && TWD.state === 'ask' && typeof twIn === 'function' && twIn(); };
  Object.defineProperty(A, 'use', { configurable:true, get:function(){ return door() ? gU() : (PSPT.open ? 'close it?' : cap0 + ' open it?'); } });
  Object.defineProperty(A, 'yes', { configurable:true, get:function(){ return door() ? gY() : (PSPT.open ? 'you close it. it stays warm in your hand.' : 'page 12, then page 14. there was never a 13.'); } });
  var lift0 = A.onLift;
  A.onLift = function(L){ PSPT.open = false; L.view = null; if (lift0) lift0(L); };   /* it always comes out shut */
  A.onYes = function(it, L){
    if (door()) { if (on1) on1(it, L); return; }
    PSPT.open = !PSPT.open;
    var again = function(){ setTimeout(function(){ if (POK.lift === L && L.t1 < 0) { L.used = false; POK.ask = A.use; pkCap.textContent = A.use; pkAsk.style.display = 'flex'; } }, 2600); };
    if (!PSPT.open) { if (L) { L.view = null; L.S = null; } again(); return; }
    pkMap('passportOpen', function(){ if (L && POK.lift === L) { L.view = 'passportOpen'; L.S = null; } again(); });   /* redrawn each time: a new stamp shows */
  };
})();


/* ---- copied from the station: hz-hub-secrets.js (the CD wallet coupon) ---- */

/* ================================================================
   №333's SECRETS (Oct 13, Aisling)
   1. buy every can of ALIEN WAVE (all three in one visit) and something is stuck to the bottom of the
      last one: a coupon for a CD WALLET, very 1995. it goes in your pocket. (later, on the music page,
      you'll be able to "go on the internet" and redeem it.)
   2. buy everything in the machine and step back: the hall's big lights go out, the wrong constellations
      in the vault shine, and the whole waltz plays through from its first bar to its last, clear.
      then the lights come back and everything is as it was. the rest keeps running as normal.
   both are in the super-user panel too.
   ================================================================ */

/* ---- copied from the station: r3a-note-fonts.js (the stranger's handwriting) ---- */

/* the stranger's handwriting (Oct 20): Reenie Beanie and Nothing You Could Do (Google Fonts, SIL Open Font License),
   cut down to the letters the note needs. drawn shaky, letter by letter, so it never looks like a font */

/* ---- copied from the station: r3b-note.js (the stranger's note) ---- */

/* ================================================================
   THE STRANGER'S NOTE, AND THE STATE NEWSPAPER, IN THE КУКУРИ CARRIAGE (Oct 20; doc 21, "The note on the floor"
   and "One small thing to click in each carriage").
   the note: a small crumpled paper half under a seat, dark on the dark floor, only on the ride toward Vahitorn. click it:
   it lifts up close. take it, and it goes in your pocket. written by a stranger, not a good person, never found out:
   big shaky letters leaning different ways, "ruined" struck through, "broke" scribbled over, CORRUPTED gone over three
   times, a tear where the pen went through, "it" trailing off the edge. then, smaller, cramped in the corner: your own
   passport number, your real local time, how long you've been in the station (all visits added up). after 3 hours,
   a new message pressed hard in rust in the empty top corner. it's written fresh every time you look at it.
   (Aisling's text, final Sept 28. the lettering is a handwriting font drawn letter by letter, shaky, until there's
   hand-drawn lettering, doc 21.)
   the newspaper: МЕЖДУ, folded on a seat. click it: you read the front page (a headline that changes each visit),
   then put it back. it isn't yours to keep (the one on Plt 1's bench is).
   ================================================================ */

/* ---- how long you've been in the station, all visits added up (kept in this browser only) ---- */
var NGT = { ms:0, last:performance.now() };
try { NGT.ms = +localStorage.getItem('nullgrey.between.time') || 0; } catch (e) {}
function ngTick(){ var n = performance.now(); if (!document.hidden) NGT.ms += Math.min(20000, n - NGT.last); NGT.last = n; try { localStorage.setItem('nullgrey.between.time', String(Math.round(NGT.ms))); } catch (e) {} }
setInterval(ngTick, 10000);
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function(){ if (document.hidden) ngTick(); else NGT.last = performance.now(); });
function ngTimeMs(){ return NGT.ms + (document.hidden ? 0 : performance.now() - NGT.last); }
function noteSpent(){
  var m = Math.floor(ngTimeMs()/60000), h = Math.floor(m/60); m = m % 60;
  if (!h && !m) return 'less than a minute';
  var s = h ? h + (h === 1 ? ' hour' : ' hours') : '';
  if (m) s += (s ? ' ' : '') + m + (m === 1 ? ' minute' : ' minutes');
  return s;
}
function noteLocalTime(){ var d = new Date(), h = d.getHours(), m = d.getMinutes(), ap = h >= 12 ? 'pm' : 'am'; h = h % 12 || 12; return h + ':' + (m < 10 ? '0' : '') + m + ap; }
function noteAfter3h(){ return ngTimeMs() >= 3*3600000; }

/* ---- the paper and the hand ---- */
var NOTE = { bg:null, fonts:null, W:600, H:450 };
var NOTE_PAPER = 'M38,34 L562,22 L574,410 L300,428 L26,420 Z';
function noteReady(cb){
  if (NOTE.bg && NOTE.fontsOk) { cb(); return; }
  var left = 2; function one(){ if (--left === 0) cb(); }
  if (!NOTE.fonts) {
    try { NOTE.fonts = Promise.all([['NoteReenie', NOTE_FONTS.reenie], ['NoteNothing', NOTE_FONTS.nothing]].map(function(f){ var ff = new FontFace(f[0], 'url(' + f[1] + ')'); document.fonts.add(ff); return ff.load(); })); }
    catch (e) { NOTE.fonts = Promise.resolve(); }
  }
  NOTE.fonts.then(function(){ NOTE.fontsOk = true; one(); }, function(){ NOTE.fontsOk = true; one(); });
  if (NOTE.bg) one();
  else {   /* the crumpled paper: drawn once (its creases are lit from the top left), the writing goes on each time */
    var svg = '<defs><filter id="cr" x="0" y="0" width="100%" height="100%"><feTurbulence type="fractalNoise" baseFrequency=".018" numOctaves="3" seed="9" result="n"/><feDiffuseLighting in="n" surfaceScale="3.2" lighting-color="#e2d9c8" result="l"><feDistantLight azimuth="235" elevation="52"/></feDiffuseLighting><feComposite in="l" in2="SourceGraphic" operator="in"/></filter>' +
      '<filter id="gr"><feTurbulence type="fractalNoise" baseFrequency=".9" numOctaves="2" seed="4"/><feColorMatrix values="0 0 0 0 .3  0 0 0 0 .25  0 0 0 0 .2  0 0 0 .18 0"/><feComposite in2="SourceGraphic" operator="in"/></filter>' +
      '<clipPath id="pc"><path d="' + NOTE_PAPER + '"/></clipPath></defs>' +
      '<g transform="rotate(-2.5 300 225)"><path d="' + NOTE_PAPER + '" fill="#000" opacity=".5" transform="translate(6,8)"/><path d="' + NOTE_PAPER + '" fill="#d8cfbf"/>' +
      '<g clip-path="url(#pc)"><rect width="600" height="450" fill="#d8cfbf" filter="url(#cr)" opacity=".45"/><rect width="600" height="450" fill="#d8cfbf" filter="url(#gr)"/>' +
      '<path d="M40,150 Q300,140 572,160" stroke="#b3a893" stroke-width="1.2" fill="none"/><path d="M300,20 Q296,220 306,430" stroke="#b3a893" stroke-width="1.2" fill="none"/><path d="M40,300 Q260,290 572,306" stroke="#bdb29e" stroke-width=".8" fill="none"/>' +
      '<path d="M520,370 q20,10 40,-6 q-6,30 16,40 L574,410 Z" fill="#b8ad98" opacity=".6"/></g>' +
      '<path d="M426,158 l9,-5 l3,2 l8,-8 l3,1 l7,-7 l1,3 l-7,8 l-4,-1 l-7,7 l-4,-1 l-8,5 z" fill="#15110f"/></g>';   /* the tear, where the pen went through */
    var img = new Image();
    img.onload = function(){ var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = 600; c.height = 450; c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, 0, 0); NOTE.bg = c; one(); };
    img.onerror = function(){ var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = 600; c.height = 450; var g = c.getContext('2d'); g.fillStyle = '#d8cfbf'; g.fill(new Path2D(NOTE_PAPER)); NOTE.bg = c; one(); };
    img.src = 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent('<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="600" height="450" viewBox="0 0 600 450">' + svg + '</svg>');
  }
}
/* one line, letter by letter: each letter leans its own way and jumps a little, the pen shakes (drawn twice, a hair apart) */
function noteScrawl(g, t, x, y, size, o){
  o = o || {}; var seed = o.seed || 1; function R(){ seed = (seed*9301 + 49297) % 233280; return seed/233280; }
  if (o.maxW) {   /* (Oct 20: a line that would run off the paper is written smaller, the way a hand squeezes it in) */
    g.save(); g.font = (o.w ? '700 ' : '') + size + 'px ' + (o.font || 'NoteReenie, cursive'); var tw = g.measureText(t).width*(o.scary ? 1.08 : 1) + (o.ls || 0)*t.length; g.restore();
    if (tw > o.maxW) size = Math.floor(size*o.maxW/tw);
  }
  var font = (o.w ? '700 ' : '') + size + 'px ' + (o.font || 'NoteReenie, cursive');
  g.save(); g.translate(x, y); g.rotate((o.r || 0)*Math.PI/180); g.font = font; g.fillStyle = o.col || '#1c1714'; g.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  if (o.op) g.globalAlpha = o.op;
  var cx = 0, prev = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < t.length; i++) {
    var ch = t[i], rot = (R() - 0.5)*(o.wob || 14)*Math.PI/180, d = (R() - 0.5)*(o.jit || 3), dy = d;
    if (ch === '№') {   /* №: the hand writes N, a small raised o, a line under it */
      g.save(); g.translate(cx, dy); g.rotate(rot); g.fillText('N', 0, 0); var nw = g.measureText('N').width;
      g.font = (o.w ? '700 ' : '') + Math.round(size*0.6) + 'px ' + (o.font || 'NoteReenie, cursive'); g.fillText('o', nw*0.95, -size*0.28); var ow = g.measureText('o').width;
      g.fillRect(nw*0.95, -size*0.2, ow, Math.max(1, size*0.05)); g.restore(); g.font = font; cx += nw + ow + size*0.12; continue;
    }
    var sz = o.scary ? size*(0.86 + R()*0.34) : size, sx = o.scary ? 0.85 + R()*0.35 : 1;   /* (the big lines: each letter its own size, squashed or stretched, pushed hard) */
    if (o.scary) { g.font = (o.w ? '700 ' : '') + Math.round(sz) + 'px ' + (o.font || 'NoteReenie, cursive'); rot *= 2.1; dy *= 1.8; }
    if (o.scary && ch === '.') { cx += size*0.08; rot *= 0.2; dy = Math.abs(dy)*0.3; }   /* (a full stop sits on the line, a little apart, so it doesn't vanish into the last letter) */
    var dig = ch >= '0' && ch <= '9', drift = o.drift ? Math.sin(i*0.31 + (o.seed || 0))*size*0.08 + i*size*(o.drift - 0.5)*0.025 : 0;   /* (the cramped lines wander up and down a little as the hand moves along) */
    if (dig && !o.scary) {   /* numbers by hand: never two the same size or lean, crowded or spread, a little higher or lower (Aisling: they read "printed") */
      sz = size*(0.9 + R()*0.2); sx = 0.86 + R()*0.24; g.font = (o.w ? '700 ' : '') + Math.round(sz) + 'px ' + (o.font || 'NoteReenie, cursive');
      rot = (R() - 0.5)*18*Math.PI/180; dy += (R() - 0.5)*size*0.14; cx += (R() - 0.4)*size*0.08;
    }
    dy += drift;
    var w = g.measureText(ch).width*sx;
    if (dig) g.globalAlpha = (o.op || 1)*(0.78 + R()*0.22);   /* (and the pen presses unevenly) */
    g.save(); g.translate(cx + w/2, dy); g.rotate(rot); g.scale(sx, 1); g.fillText(ch, -w/2/sx, 0); if (o.heavy) { g.strokeStyle = g.fillStyle; g.lineWidth = o.heavy; g.lineJoin = 'round'; g.strokeText(ch, -w/2/sx, 0); } g.globalAlpha *= 0.4; g.fillText(ch, -w/2/sx + 0.9, 0.7); if (o.scary) { g.globalAlpha *= 0.8; g.fillText(ch, -w/2/sx - 0.6, -0.8); } g.restore();
    cx += w + (o.ls || 0);
    if (dig) { g.globalAlpha = o.op || 1; g.font = font; }
  }
  g.restore();
  return cx;
}
function notePaint(g, w, h){
  var ink = '#1c1714', rust = '#73361f';
  g.save(); g.scale(w/600, h/450);
  if (NOTE.bg) g.drawImage(NOTE.bg, 0, 0);
  g.translate(300, 225); g.rotate(-2.5*Math.PI/180); g.translate(-300, -225);
  noteScrawl(g, "3 aisling's.", 66, 80, 48, { r:-3, wob:16, jit:4, seed:3, scary:1 });
  noteScrawl(g, '2 created 1.', 92, 132, 46, { r:2, wob:18, jit:5, seed:7, scary:1 });

  noteScrawl(g, 'CORRUPTED', 304, 291, 46, { r:-3.4, wob:7, jit:2, seed:31, w:1, op:0.8 });

  noteScrawl(g, 'i know which one you are.', 304, 409, 19, { wob:11, jit:2.5, seed:53, drift:0.6 });
  g.restore();
  if (noteAfter3h()) {   /* in rust, the same hand, pressed hard enough to dent the paper, squeezed into the empty top corner (and kept on the paper) */
    g.save(); g.translate(440, 90); g.rotate(4*Math.PI/180); g.translate(-440, -90);
    [[1.1, 1.5, 'rgba(110,100,80,0.5)'], [0, 0, rust]].forEach(function(L){ g.save(); g.translate(L[0], L[1]);
      var o = { col:L[2], scary:1, heavy:1.6 };   /* (pressed hard: a heavier line) */

      noteScrawl(g, 'side of the leak.', 340, 126, 30, Object.assign({ wob:14, jit:3.5, seed:71, maxW:196 }, o)); g.restore(); });

  }
  g.restore();
}

PK_ART.strangerNote = { vb:[300, 225], k:2.2, lie:0.2, unique:true, hub:true, fresh:true,
  cap:'reading the note makes you feel quite uneasy, your skin starts to crawl. you don’t recognize the handwriting.',
  ready:noteReady, paint:notePaint };
var _pkMapNote = pkMap;
pkMap = function(id, done){
  var A = PK_ART[id]; if (!A || !A.paint) return _pkMapNote(id, done);
  (A.ready || function(cb){ cb(); })(function(){
    var w = Math.round(A.vb[0]*A.k), h = Math.round(A.vb[1]*A.k), c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h;
    var x = c.getContext('2d'); A.paint(x, w, h);
    var d = x.getImageData(0, 0, w, h).data, RI = new Int16Array(w*h), V = new Float32Array(w*h), ink = A.ink || pkInk;
    for (var i = 0; i < w*h; i++) { if (d[i*4 + 3] < 120) { RI[i] = -1; continue; } var q = ink(d[i*4], d[i*4 + 1], d[i*4 + 2]); RI[i] = q[0]; V[i] = q[1]; }
    POK.maps[id] = { w:w, h:h, RI:RI, V:V }; done();
  });
};

/* ================================================================
   THE POCKET, SMALL FIXES (Oct 21, Aisling's playthrough).
   - the sprats can be eaten now (one at a time, a soft bite); the tin runs out after three.
   - the two buckeyes run out: after you've eaten both, they're gone.
   - the candle: "light it?" → "you don't have matches."
   - drop: everyday things (drinks, food, the candle, the glove, a postcard, the newspaper…) can be dropped, so the
     pocket doesn't fill up; the things that matter to the story (the passport, the passes, the notes, the quarter,
     the unicorn, the compact, the coupon, the ticket, the lint, what's deeper down) can't.
   ================================================================ */
PK_ART.sprats.use = 'eat one?'; PK_ART.sprats.uses = 3;
PK_ART.sprats.yes = 'oily, smoky, salty. the one that was looking at you is still looking at you.';
PK_ART.sprats.yesLast = 'the last one. the tin is empty now. you leave it behind.';
DRK_EAT.sprats = 'soft';
PK_ART.buckeye.uses = 2; PK_ART.buckeye.yesLast = 'the last one. peanut butter and chocolate. it tastes like ohio.';
PK_ART.buckeye.capOne = 'one buckeye left. peanut butter, chocolate.';
PK_ART.candle.use = 'light it?'; PK_ART.candle.yes = 'you don’t have matches.';
var PK_DROP = { alienwave:1, bottle:1, buckeye:1, candle:1, coffee:1, fogcan:1, glove:1, newspaper:1, onigiri:1, postcard:1, sleep:1, smile:1, sprats:1 };
var PK_USED = (function(){ try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('nullgrey.pocket.used') || '{}') || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } })();
function pkUsedSave(){ try { localStorage.setItem('nullgrey.pocket.used', JSON.stringify(PK_USED)); } catch (e) {} }
/* things that run out: count each "yes"; the last one makes it go, like a drink */
(function(){
  var ask = document.getElementById('pk-ask');
  ask.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var b = e.target.closest('button'); if (!b || !POK.ask || b.getAttribute('data-a') !== '1') return;
    var it = POK.lift && POK.lift.it, A = it && PK_ART[it.id]; if (!A || !A.uses) return;
    var n = (PK_USED[it.id] || 0) + 1;
    if (n >= A.uses) { A.gone = true; A._yes = A.yes; A.yes = A.yesLast || A.yes; PK_USED[it.id] = 0; setTimeout(function(){ A.gone = false; A.yes = A._yes; }, 1600); }
    else PK_USED[it.id] = n;
    pkUsedSave();
  }, true);   /* (before the pocket's own answer, so it knows whether this is the last one) */
})();
/* the buckeyes say how many are left */
var _pkLiftUses = pkLift;
pkLift = function(it){
  var A = PK_ART[it.id];
  if (it.id === 'buckeye') A.cap = (PK_USED.buckeye || 0) >= 1 ? A.capOne : 'two buckeyes. peanut butter, chocolate.';
  if (it.id === 'sprats') A.cap = ['sprats. one of them is looking at you.', 'sprats. fewer now. one of them is still looking at you.', 'one sprat left. it is looking at you.'][Math.min(2, PK_USED.sprats || 0)];
  _pkLiftUses(it);
  pkDropShow(false);
};
/* ---- drop ---- */
var pkDropBtn = document.createElement('button'); pkDropBtn.type = 'button'; pkDropBtn.id = 'pk-drop'; pkDropBtn.textContent = 'drop it · выбросить';
(function(){ var st = document.createElement('style'); st.textContent = '#pk-drop{position:absolute;right:7%;bottom:14.5%;font-family:inherit;font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.2em;color:rgba(228,220,208,.4);background:none;border:0;cursor:pointer;z-index:3;display:none;}#pk-drop:hover{color:rgba(228,220,208,.85);}'; document.head.appendChild(st); })();
pkEl.appendChild(pkDropBtn);
function pkDropShow(on){ pkDropBtn.style.display = on ? 'block' : 'none'; }
pkDropBtn.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); });
pkDropBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e){
  e.stopPropagation(); var L = POK.lift, it = L && L.it; if (!it || !PK_DROP[it.id]) return;
  var k = PK_HAVE.indexOf(it.id); if (k >= 0) PK_HAVE.splice(k, 1); pkSave(); PK_USED[it.id] = 0; pkUsedSave();
  pkDropShow(false); pkAsk.style.display = 'none'; POK.ask = null;
  L.t1 = performance.now(); L.msg = 'you leave it behind.'; L.dropped = true; POK.dropping = L;
});
/* show it once a droppable thing is up; tidy the pocket after one is dropped */
var _pkFrameDrop = pkFrame;
pkFrame = function(now){
  _pkFrameDrop(now);
  var L = POK.lift; pkDropShow(!!(L && L.done && L.t1 < 0 && !L.fizz && PK_DROP[L.it.id]));
  if (POK.dropping && POK.lift !== POK.dropping) { POK.dropping = null; pkLayout(); }

       own, the line and the stop along the bottom */

    s += mtx('ふるさと線 二丁目', 30, 84.4, '#5d554e', 3.3, { w:700 });
    return s;
  }

/* (Oct 28) white girl's matchbook brings its own inks: the usual ones, and the dusty rose and greens of its sky and tags
   (the pocket's own inks would turn the rose lilac, which leans blue) */
var PK_INKS_WG = null, PK_INKC_WG = {};
function pkInkWG(r, g, b){
  if (!PK_INKS_WG) { PK_INKS_WG = PK_INKS.slice(); ['hmauve', 'hrose', 'hglgreen', 'hcream'].forEach(function(nm){ var ri = RIX[nm]; if (ri === undefined) return; var n = RN[ri]; for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) { var c = RF[ri*FOGN*RMAX + k]; PK_INKS_WG.push([ri, (k + 0.5)/n, c & 255, (c >> 8) & 255, (c >> 16) & 255]); } }); }
  var key = (r >> 2) << 12 | (g >> 2) << 6 | (b >> 2), c = PK_INKC_WG[key]; if (c) return c; var best = null, bd = 1e9;
  for (var k = 0; k < PK_INKS_WG.length; k++) { var q = PK_INKS_WG[k], dr = r - q[2], dg = g - q[3], db = b - q[4], d = dr*dr*0.3 + dg*dg*0.59 + db*db*0.11; if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = q; } }
  PK_INKC_WG[key] = best; return best;
}

/* the receipt: what it says is kept with it (nullgrey.atm), so it shows the same on every page */
function fAtmRcptData(){ var A = {}; try { A = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('nullgrey.atm') || '{}') || {}; } catch (e) {} return A.last || { no:'0013-0001', code:'NG3-K7Q-1029', date:[101, 10, 27], time:'01:13', bal:'*,***,***' }; }
function fAtmRcptSvg(){
  var D = fAtmRcptData(), P = '#7a3a6a', T = function(x, y, t, sz, a, w){ return '<text x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" font-family="IBM Plex Mono, MS Gothic, monospace" font-size="' + (sz || 2.2) + '" fill="' + P + '"' + (a ? ' text-anchor="' + a + '"' : '') + (w ? ' font-weight="' + w + '"' : '') + '>' + t + '</text>'; };
  var L = function(y){ return '<path d="M2.5,' + y + ' h33" stroke="' + P + '" stroke-width="0.3" stroke-dasharray="0.6 0.5"/>'; }, W = 38, c = W/2;
  var s = '<rect x="0.5" y="0.5" width="' + (W - 1) + '" height="77" fill="#f2eee6"/>';
  s += T(c, 5, 'ご利用ありがとうございます', 2, 'middle') + T(c, 9.8, 'ふるさと駅 ATM', 3, 'middle', 700) + T(c, 12.8, '現金自動支払機 No.0013', 1.8, 'middle') + L(14.6);
  s += T(2.5, 18.2, '昭和' + D.date[0] + '年' + D.date[1] + '月' + D.date[2] + '日', 2) + T(35.5, 18.2, D.time, 2, 'end');
  s += T(2.5, 22, 'ｵﾄﾘﾋｷ') + T(35.5, 22, 'ｵﾋｷﾀﾞｼ', 2.2, 'end') + T(2.5, 25.6, 'ｷﾝｶﾞｸ') + T(35.5, 25.8, '¥100', 3, 'end', 700);
  s += T(2.5, 29.2, 'ﾃｽｳﾘｮｳ') + T(35.5, 29.2, '¥0', 2.2, 'end') + T(2.5, 32.8, 'ｻﾞﾝﾀﾞｶ') + T(35.5, 32.8, '¥' + D.bal, 2.2, 'end') + L(34.8);
  s += T(2.5, 38.2, 'ﾄﾘﾋｷﾊﾞﾝｺﾞｳ') + T(35.5, 38.2, D.no, 2.2, 'end') + T(2.5, 41.8, 'ｼｮｳｶｲｺｰﾄﾞ') + T(35.5, 45.2, D.code, 2.8, 'end', 700);
  s += T(2.5, 48.8, 'ｶｰﾄﾞ') + T(35.5, 48.8, 'ﾅｼ / NONE', 2.2, 'end') + T(2.5, 52.4, 'ﾃﾞﾝﾋﾟｮｳ') + T(35.5, 52.4, '1922', 2.2, 'end') + L(54.4);
  s += T(c, 58, 'このレシートは大切に保管して下さい', 1.7, 'middle') + T(c, 61.6, 'おといあわせ', 1.9, 'middle') + T(c, 65, '☎ 1029-1929', 2.8, 'middle', 700) + T(c, 68.2, '24ｼﾞｶﾝ ｳｹﾂｹ', 1.8, 'middle');

}

/* ---- (Oct 27) the claw machine's plush (Aisling's list), drawn the same in the machine and in the pocket; won about one
   go in a hundred, which one is luck ---- */
function fPlush(x, y, c, k){
  var s = '';
  if (k === 'mush') return '<rect x="' + (x - 3) + '" y="' + (y - 4) + '" width="6" height="8" fill="#f2ece0"/><path d="M' + (x - 8) + ',' + (y - 3) + ' Q' + x + ',' + (y - 14) + ' ' + (x + 8) + ',' + (y - 3) + ' Z" fill="#c8502e"/><circle cx="' + (x - 3) + '" cy="' + (y - 7) + '" r="1.4" fill="#f2ece0"/><circle cx="' + (x + 3) + '" cy="' + (y - 6) + '" r="1.2" fill="#f2ece0"/>';
  if (k === 'saffron') return '<ellipse cx="' + x + '" cy="' + (y + 4) + '" rx="5" ry="5" fill="' + c + '"/><ellipse cx="' + x + '" cy="' + (y - 5) + '" rx="7" ry="8.5" fill="' + c + '"/>' +   /* Saffron: a mint-green oval head, big slanted almond eyes apart, each with a glint, a small mouth */
    '<path d="M' + (x - 6) + ',' + (y - 6) + ' Q' + (x - 4) + ',' + (y - 10) + ' ' + (x - 1) + ',' + (y - 6) + ' Q' + (x - 4) + ',' + (y - 3) + ' ' + (x - 6) + ',' + (y - 6) + ' Z" fill="#1a1416"/><path d="M' + (x + 6) + ',' + (y - 6) + ' Q' + (x + 4) + ',' + (y - 10) + ' ' + (x + 1) + ',' + (y - 6) + ' Q' + (x + 4) + ',' + (y - 3) + ' ' + (x + 6) + ',' + (y - 6) + ' Z" fill="#1a1416"/>' +
    '<circle cx="' + (x - 4.4) + '" cy="' + (y - 7.2) + '" r="0.8" fill="#ffffff"/><circle cx="' + (x + 2.6) + '" cy="' + (y - 7.2) + '" r="0.8" fill="#ffffff"/><path d="M' + (x - 1.2) + ',' + (y - 0.6) + ' q1.2,0.8 2.4,0" stroke="#1a1416" stroke-width="0.6" fill="none"/>';
  if (k === 'owl') return '<ellipse cx="' + x + '" cy="' + (y + 1) + '" rx="8" ry="9.5" fill="' + c + '"/><path d="M' + (x - 7) + ',' + (y - 5) + ' l-1,-6 l4,3 Z M' + (x + 7) + ',' + (y - 5) + ' l1,-6 l-4,3 Z" fill="' + c + '"/>' +   /* a plain owl: ear tufts, two big eyes, a little beak, a pale belly */
    '<ellipse cx="' + x + '" cy="' + (y + 5) + '" rx="5" ry="4" fill="#e2c7a0"/><circle cx="' + (x - 3.2) + '" cy="' + (y - 3) + '" r="3" fill="#f2ece0"/><circle cx="' + (x + 3.2) + '" cy="' + (y - 3) + '" r="3" fill="#f2ece0"/><circle cx="' + (x - 3.2) + '" cy="' + (y - 3) + '" r="1.6" fill="#1a1416"/><circle cx="' + (x + 3.2) + '" cy="' + (y - 3) + '" r="1.6" fill="#1a1416"/>' +
    '<circle cx="' + (x - 2.7) + '" cy="' + (y - 3.6) + '" r="0.5" fill="#ffffff"/><circle cx="' + (x + 3.7) + '" cy="' + (y - 3.6) + '" r="0.5" fill="#ffffff"/><path d="M' + (x - 1) + ',' + (y - 0.6) + ' l1,1.8 l1,-1.8 Z" fill="#f0b83a"/>';
  if (k === 'cat') return '<ellipse cx="' + x + '" cy="' + (y + 3) + '" rx="7" ry="6" fill="' + c + '"/><circle cx="' + x + '" cy="' + (y - 5) + '" r="6.4" fill="' + c + '"/><path d="M' + (x - 6) + ',' + (y - 7) + ' l0,-7 l5,4 Z M' + (x + 6) + ',' + (y - 7) + ' l0,-7 l-5,4 Z" fill="' + c + '"/>' +   /* a black cat, yellow-green eyes (you know him) */
    '<ellipse cx="' + (x - 2.4) + '" cy="' + (y - 5) + '" rx="1.5" ry="1.2" fill="#c8d84a"/><ellipse cx="' + (x + 2.4) + '" cy="' + (y - 5) + '" rx="1.5" ry="1.2" fill="#c8d84a"/><rect x="' + (x - 2.6) + '" y="' + (y - 5.8) + '" width="0.5" height="1.6" fill="#1a1416"/><rect x="' + (x + 2.2) + '" y="' + (y - 5.8) + '" width="0.5" height="1.6" fill="#1a1416"/><path d="M' + (x + 6) + ',' + (y + 6) + ' q5,0 4,-6 q-1,-3 1,-4" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="2" fill="none"/>';
  if (k === 'star') return '<path d="M' + x + ',' + (y - 12) + ' l3.4,8 l8.6,0.6 l-6.6,5.6 l2.2,8.4 l-7.6,-4.6 l-7.6,4.6 l2.2,-8.4 l-6.6,-5.6 l8.6,-0.6 Z" fill="' + c + '"/><circle cx="' + (x - 2.2) + '" cy="' + (y - 2) + '" r="0.9" fill="#1a1416"/><circle cx="' + (x + 2.2) + '" cy="' + (y - 2) + '" r="0.9" fill="#1a1416"/><path d="M' + (x - 1.4) + ',' + (y + 0.6) + ' q1.4,1.2 2.8,0" stroke="#1a1416" stroke-width="0.6" fill="none"/><ellipse cx="' + (x - 4) + '" cy="' + y + '" rx="1.2" ry="0.6" fill="#e05a4a" opacity="0.5"/><ellipse cx="' + (x + 4) + '" cy="' + y + '" rx="1.2" ry="0.6" fill="#e05a4a" opacity="0.5"/>';
  if (k === 'ghost') return '<path d="M' + (x - 7) + ',' + (y + 6) + ' L' + (x - 7) + ',' + (y - 3) + ' Q' + x + ',' + (y - 14) + ' ' + (x + 7) + ',' + (y - 3) + ' L' + (x + 7) + ',' + (y + 6) + ' l-3.5,-3 l-3.5,3 l-3.5,-3 Z" fill="' + c + '"/><circle cx="' + (x - 2.5) + '" cy="' + (y - 3) + '" r="1" fill="#1a1416"/><circle cx="' + (x + 2.5) + '" cy="' + (y - 3) + '" r="1" fill="#1a1416"/>';
  s += '<ellipse cx="' + x + '" cy="' + (y + 3) + '" rx="7" ry="6" fill="' + c + '"/><circle cx="' + x + '" cy="' + (y - 5) + '" r="6" fill="' + c + '"/>';
  if (k === 'bear') s += '<circle cx="' + (x - 5) + '" cy="' + (y - 10) + '" r="2.4" fill="' + c + '"/><circle cx="' + (x + 5) + '" cy="' + (y - 10) + '" r="2.4" fill="' + c + '"/>';
  if (k === 'bunny') s += '<ellipse cx="' + (x - 3) + '" cy="' + (y - 14) + '" rx="1.8" ry="5" fill="' + c + '"/><ellipse cx="' + (x + 3) + '" cy="' + (y - 14) + '" rx="1.8" ry="5" fill="' + c + '"/>';
  if (k === 'chick') s += '<path d="M' + (x - 1.5) + ',' + (y - 4) + ' l3,0 l-1.5,2 Z" fill="#c8502e"/>';
  if (k === 'frog') s += '<circle cx="' + (x - 3.5) + '" cy="' + (y - 10) + '" r="2.4" fill="' + c + '"/><circle cx="' + (x + 3.5) + '" cy="' + (y - 10) + '" r="2.4" fill="' + c + '"/>';
  s += '<circle cx="' + (x - 2.2) + '" cy="' + (y - 5) + '" r="0.9" fill="#1a1416"/><circle cx="' + (x + 2.2) + '" cy="' + (y - 5) + '" r="0.9" fill="#1a1416"/><ellipse cx="' + (x - 3.6) + '" cy="' + (y - 3) + '" rx="1.2" ry="0.6" fill="#e05a4a" opacity="0.5"/><ellipse cx="' + (x + 3.6) + '" cy="' + (y - 3) + '" rx="1.2" ry="0.6" fill="#e05a4a" opacity="0.5"/>';
  return s;
}

/* ---- copied from null.grey-furusatostation/src/shared/pocket-folders.js ---- */
/* ================================================================
   POCKETS IN THE POCKET (step 6, round 1b, Oct 24). shared: the same file is in both pages.
   Aisling: "pockets in pockets, with different real stitches on each one … a mix of colours on all of them,
   but each pocket's stitch has a little more of a different colour … enough to notice and memorize and pick out
   by sight, but not obtrusive … slightly reflective and interesting thread … stitched-in labels, readability
   more important than it looking like real stitching … a tiny tiny sound as they open … pages like we have now
   in the sub folders … keep the layers deeper thing … change none of the rest".
   - the top layer: the little pockets sewn along the wall under the lip, the loose things (passport, multipass,
     the pass, Ivan's note, the lint) lying below them.
   - one layer deeper, always: the Kodama ticket, too far down to lift, as before.
   - tap a little pocket: a tiny soft sound, the pocket sinks into it, and you're inside: its own things, its
     own layers going deeper, its thread round the rim and its label stitched under the lip. ↑ out comes back.
   no folder for clues: nothing here says what matters.
   ================================================================ */
var PK_FOLDERS = [
  { id:'food',    jp:'食べもの', en:'food & drink', ink:'amber' },
  { id:'sweets',  jp:'おかし',   en:'sweets',       ink:'hglrose', late:true, when:function(){ return PK_HAVE.some(function(id){ var F = typeof FU_ITEMS !== 'undefined' && FU_ITEMS[id]; return F && F.kind === 'sweet'; }); } },   /* (Oct 28) sweets get their own little pocket once you have one */
  { id:'paper',   jp:'紙',       en:'paper',        ink:'paper' },
  { id:'reading', jp:'本',       en:'reading',      ink:'plumglow' },
  { id:'things',  jp:'もの',     en:'things',       ink:'holive' },
  { id:'camera',  jp:'カメラ',   en:'cameras & film', ink:'hamber', late:true, when:function(){ return PK_HAVE.some(function(id){ var A = PK_ART[id]; return A && A.folder === 'camera'; }); } },   /* (Oct 28) from the kiosk */
  { id:'capsule', jp:'ガチャ',   en:'capsules',     ink:'pgreen', late:true },
  { id:'sticker', jp:'シール',   en:'stickers',     ink:'hrose',  late:true, when:function(){ return PK_HAVE.indexOf('f_stkbook') >= 0; } },   /* (Oct 26) sewn in with your first sticker */
  { id:'junk',    jp:'がらくた', en:'junk',         ink:'bRust' }
];
var PK_LOOSE = { passport:1, multipass:1, pass:1, note:1, lint:1, kodama:1, passportOpen:1, compactOpen:1 };
var PK_FOLDER_OF = {
  bottle:'food', sprats:'food', sleep:'food', smile:'food', coffee:'food', onigiri:'food', buckeye:'food', alienwave:'food', fogcan:'food',
  ticket:'paper', postcard:'paper', cdcoupon:'paper', strangerNote:'paper',
  newspaper:'reading',
  quarter:'things', glove:'things', candle:'things', unicorn:'things', compact:'things'
};
function pkFolderOf(id){
  var A = PK_ART[id];
  if (A && A.folder) return A.folder;
  if (PK_FOLDER_OF[id]) return PK_FOLDER_OF[id];
  if (/^f_gc_/.test(id)) return 'capsule';
  if (/^f_pack_/.test(id)) return 'junk';
  if (/^f_(tk_|match|oldtickets)/.test(id)) return 'paper';
  if (/^f_(button|gothic|buildings|moss|rails|radio|puzzle|edge|furupaper|ukiyopaper|dreambook)$/.test(id)) return 'reading';
  var F = typeof FU_ITEMS !== 'undefined' && FU_ITEMS[id];
  if (F && F.kind === 'sweet') return pkFoldersShown().some(function(Q){ return Q.id === 'sweets'; }) ? 'sweets' : 'food';
  if (F && (F.kind === 'drink' || F.kind === 'food')) return 'food';
  return 'things';
}
function pkFoldersShown(){
  var gc = false; try { gc = !!localStorage.getItem('nullgrey.capsules'); } catch (e) {}
  if (!gc) gc = PK_HAVE.some(function(id){ return /^f_gc_/.test(id); });
  return PK_FOLDERS.filter(function(F){ return !F.late || (F.when ? F.when() : gc); });
}
POK.folder = null;

/* ---- the thread: every pocket's stitches mix all the inks, one of them a little more ---- */
function pkThreadRamps(){
  var all = ['amber', 'paper', 'plumglow', 'silver', 'holive', 'bRust', 'gold', 'amber', 'paper'].map(function(n){ return RIX[n] !== undefined ? RIX[n] : R_GOLD; });
  return all;
}
function pkThreadPick(F, seed){
  var mine = RIX[F.ink] !== undefined ? RIX[F.ink] : R_GOLD, h = hsh(seed, 77);
  if (h < 0.58) return mine;
  var all = pkThreadRamps(); return all[(hsh(seed, 78)*all.length) | 0];
}
function pkPx(d, W, i, j, ri, v){ var nR = RN[ri], b = BAY[((j & 3) << 2) | (i & 3)], ix = (Math.max(0, v)*(nR - 1) + b) | 0; if (ix >= nR) ix = nR - 1; d[j*W + i] = RF[ri*FOGN*RMAX + ix]; }
/* the label as hard pixels (readable first) */
function pkLabelMask(t, font, px){
  var c = document.createElement('canvas'), x = c.getContext('2d'); x.font = font; var w = Math.ceil(x.measureText(t).width) + 2;
  c.width = w; c.height = px + 4; x = c.getContext('2d'); x.font = font; x.fillStyle = '#fff'; x.textBaseline = 'top'; x.fillText(t, 1, 1);
  var d = x.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height).data, m = new Uint8Array(c.width*c.height);
  for (var k = 0; k < m.length; k++) m[k] = d[k*4 + 3] > 120 ? 1 : 0;
  return { w:c.width, h:c.height, m:m };
}
var PK_POUCH_W = 100, PK_POUCH_H = 80;   /* (Oct 24) a little bigger, for reading */
var PK_JPFONT = '"IBM Plex Sans JP","Hiragino Sans","Noto Sans CJK JP",sans-serif';   /* the site's fonts (style reference): Plex Mono, its Japanese from Plex Sans JP */
var PK_ENFONT = '"IBM Plex Mono",monospace';
/* the labels' fonts: Plex Sans JP only for the few characters the labels use, and Plex Mono's heavier weight; redrawn once they arrive */
(function(){
  var chars = ''; PK_FOLDERS.forEach(function(F){ chars += F.jp; }); chars += '外へ';
  try {
    var l = document.createElement('link'); l.rel = 'stylesheet';
    l.href = 'https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=IBM+Plex+Sans+JP:wght@600&text=' + encodeURIComponent(chars) + '&family=IBM+Plex+Mono:wght@600&display=swap';
    document.head.appendChild(l);
    if (document.fonts && document.fonts.load) Promise.all([document.fonts.load('600 18px "IBM Plex Sans JP"', chars), document.fonts.load('600 12px "IBM Plex Mono"', 'food & drink')]).then(function(){ POK.pouch = null; if (POK.ready) pkLayout(); }, function(){});
  } catch (e) {}
})();
function pkPouchSprite(F, light, fi){
  var W = PK_POUCH_W, H = PK_POUCH_H, c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = W; c.height = H;
  var x = c.getContext('2d'), id = x.createImageData(W, H), d = new Uint32Array(id.data.buffer), mask = new Uint8Array(W*H), thr = [];
  function inside(i, j){ if (i < 1 || i > W - 2 || j < 0) return false; if (j < H - 22) return true; var ex = (i - W/2)/(W/2 - 1), ey = (j - (H - 22))/21; return ex*ex + ey*ey <= 1; }
  function edge(i, j){ var e = 9; for (var r = 1; r < 9; r++) if (!inside(i - r, j) || !inside(i + r, j) || !inside(i, j + r)) { e = r; break; } return e; }
  for (var j = 0; j < H; j++) for (var i = 0; i < W; i++) {
    if (!inside(i, j)) continue; mask[j*W + i] = 1;
    var v = 0.2 + (frac((i*0.5 + j*0.5)/2.6) < 0.5 ? 0.03 : 0) + (hsh(i + fi*31, j + 5) - 0.5)*0.06 + (vn(i*0.07 + fi, j*0.09) - 0.5)*0.05;   /* the same wool, a shade lighter: sewn on top */
    var e = edge(i, j); if (e < 3) v *= 0.7 + 0.1*e;
    if (j < 9) v = j < 2 ? 0.3 : (j < 4 ? 0.06 : 0.11 + j*0.008);   /* its mouth: a folded edge, then the dark of the opening */
    pkPx(d, W, i, j, R_BPLUM, v*light);
  }
  /* the running stitch round its sides and bottom, 3 in from the edge, each stitch its own thread */
  var path = [];
  for (var j = 6; j < H - 22; j++) path.push([4, j]);
  for (var a = 0; a <= 60; a++) { var an = Math.PI - a/60*Math.PI, ex = (W/2 - 5), ey = 18; path.push([Math.round(W/2 + Math.cos(an)*ex), Math.round(H - 22 + Math.sin(an)*ey)]); }
  for (var j = H - 23; j >= 6; j--) path.push([W - 5, j]);
  var seen = {}, s = 0;
  path.forEach(function(p, n){
    var key = p[0] + ',' + p[1]; if (seen[key]) return; seen[key] = 1; s++;
    var st = ((s / 5) | 0), on = (s % 5) < 3; if (!on) return;
    var ri = pkThreadPick(F, st*7 + fi*101), v = 0.62 + hsh(st, fi + 9)*0.18;
    pkPx(d, W, p[0], p[1], ri, v*light); thr.push([p[0], p[1], ri]);
  });
  /* its label, stitched in: the Japanese, and the English under it. one thread, the pocket's own colour, on one
     even step of its ramp (no dither, so no banding: Aisling, Oct 24, readability first) */
  var Lj = pkLabelMask(F.jp, '600 18px ' + PK_JPFONT, 18), Le = null; for (var fz = 12; fz >= 9; fz -= 0.5) { Le = pkLabelMask(F.en, '600 ' + fz + 'px ' + PK_ENFONT, 12); if (Le.w <= W - 16) break; }   /* the English shrinks a little if it would touch the stitching (food & drink) */
  var lri = RIX[F.ink] !== undefined ? RIX[F.ink] : R_GOLD, lv = (RN[lri] - 2)/(RN[lri] - 1);
  [[Lj, 15], [Le, 41]].forEach(function(q){
    var M = q[0], ox = Math.round((W - M.w)/2), oy = q[1];
    for (var jj = 0; jj < M.h; jj++) for (var ii = 0; ii < M.w; ii++) {
      if (!M.m[jj*M.w + ii]) continue; var X = ox + ii, Y = oy + jj + 1; if (X < 0 || Y >= H || X >= W) continue;
      if (!(jj + 1 < M.h && M.m[(jj + 1)*M.w + ii])) pkPx(d, W, X, Y, R_BPLUM, 0);   /* a little shadow under each stroke */
    }
    for (var jj = 0; jj < M.h; jj++) for (var ii = 0; ii < M.w; ii++) {
      if (!M.m[jj*M.w + ii]) continue; var X = ox + ii, Y = oy + jj; if (X < 0 || Y < 0 || X >= W || Y >= H) continue;
      pkPx(d, W, X, Y, lri, lv*Math.min(1, light)); thr.push([X, Y, lri]);
    }
  });
  x.putImageData(id, 0, 0);
  return { c:c, W:W, H:H, mask:mask, thr:thr };
}
/* the thread catches the light: a slow sheen sliding across each little pocket */
function pkPouchSheen(S){
  var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = S.W; c.height = S.H; var x = c.getContext('2d'), id = x.createImageData(S.W, S.H), d = new Uint32Array(id.data.buffer);
  S.thr.forEach(function(p){ pkPx(d, S.W, p[0], p[1], p[2], 1.0); });
  x.putImageData(id, 0, 0); return c;
}
/* inside a little pocket: its thread round the rim, its label under the lip */
var PK_FBG = {};
function pkFolderBg(F, fi){
  if (PK_FBG[F.id]) return PK_FBG[F.id];
  var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = PW; c.height = PH; var x = c.getContext('2d'); x.drawImage(POK.bgMain, 0, 0);
  var id = x.getImageData(0, 0, PW, PH), d = new Uint32Array(id.data.buffer), s = 0;
  for (var a = 0; a < 900; a++) {   /* a running stitch just inside the rim */
    var t = a/900, X, Y;
    if (t < 0.18) { X = 72; Y = 96 + t/0.18*170; } else if (t < 0.82) { var an = Math.PI - (t - 0.18)/0.64*Math.PI; X = 384 + Math.cos(an)*311; Y = 266 + Math.sin(an)*92; } else { X = 696; Y = 266 - (t - 0.82)/0.18*170; }
    X = Math.round(X); Y = Math.round(Y); s++; if (((s/6) | 0) % 2) continue;
    var st = (s/6) | 0, ri = pkThreadPick(F, st*5 + fi*19); pkPx(d, PW, X, Y, ri, 0.5 + hsh(st, fi)*0.14);
  }
  var Lj = pkLabelMask(F.jp + ' · ' + F.en, '600 13px ' + PK_JPFONT, 13), ox = Math.round((PW - Lj.w)/2), oy = 96;
  for (var jj = 0; jj < Lj.h; jj++) for (var ii = 0; ii < Lj.w; ii++) if (Lj.m[jj*Lj.w + ii]) { var run = ((ii + jj*3)/4) | 0; pkPx(d, PW, ox + ii, oy + jj, pkThreadPick(F, run*13 + jj + fi*57), 0.56 + hsh(run, jj)*0.12); }
  x.putImageData(id, 0, 0); PK_FBG[F.id] = c; return c;
}

/* ---- what's where ---- */
pkList = function(){
  var have = PK_HAVE.filter(function(id){ return !!PK_ART[id]; }).reverse();
  if (POK.folder) return have.filter(function(id){ return !PK_LOOSE[id] && pkFolderOf(id) === POK.folder; });
  var L = KASSA.pass ? ['passport', 'multipass'] : [];
  if (PASS_STATE === 2) L.push('pass');
  have.forEach(function(id){ if (PK_LOOSE[id] && L.indexOf(id) < 0) L.push(id); });
  L.push('note', 'lint');
  return L;
};
var PK_LOOSE_SLOTS = [[196, 268, -0.2], [566, 262, 0.14], [384, 236, 0.05], [290, 322, -0.1], [478, 318, 0.13], [636, 318, 0.22], [128, 316, 0.2]];
pkPages = function(){ return POK.folder ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil(pkList().length/PK_PER)) : 2; };
pkLayout = function(){
  var T = POK.turn;
  if (T && T.swapped && T.fold !== undefined && !T.foldDone) { T.foldDone = true; POK.folder = T.fold; POK.page = T.np; }
  if (POK.bgMain) { var Fi = -1; PK_FOLDERS.forEach(function(F, i){ if (F.id === POK.folder) Fi = i; }); POK.bg = Fi >= 0 ? pkFolderBg(PK_FOLDERS[Fi], Fi) : POK.bgMain; }
  var L = pkList(), out = [], pages = pkPages();
  POK.page = Math.min(POK.page || 0, pages - 1); POK.pages = pages;
  if (POK.folder) {
    L.slice(POK.page*PK_PER, (POK.page + 1)*PK_PER).forEach(function(id, n){
      var s = PK_SLOTS[n], j = POK.page*7 + PK_FOLDERS.map(function(F){ return F.id; }).indexOf(POK.folder)*3;
      out.push({ id:id, x:s[0] + (hsh(n + j, 21) - 0.5)*26, y:s[1] + (hsh(n + j, 22) - 0.5)*14, rot:s[2] + (hsh(n + j, 23) - 0.5)*0.4, depth:s[3], n:n });
    });
  } else if (POK.page === 0) {
    var shown = pkFoldersShown(), gap = 10, rows = shown.length > 6 ? 2 : 1, perRow = Math.ceil(shown.length/rows);   /* (Oct 28) more than six pockets: two rows */
    shown.forEach(function(F, n0){
      var row = Math.floor(n0/perRow), n = n0 - row*perRow, inRow = row === rows - 1 ? shown.length - row*perRow : perRow;
      var tot = inRow*PK_POUCH_W + (inRow - 1)*gap, x0 = 384 - tot/2 + PK_POUCH_W/2, yRow = rows === 1 ? 142 : (row === 0 ? 110 : 196);
      var fi = PK_FOLDERS.indexOf(F); if (!POK.pouch) POK.pouch = {};
      var P = POK.pouch[F.id]; if (!P) { P = POK.pouch[F.id] = { S:pkPouchSprite(F, 1, fi), Sh:null, sheen:null }; P.Sh = pkPouchSprite(F, 1.16, fi); P.sheen = pkPouchSheen(P.S); P.sh = pkShadow(P.S); }
      out.push({ id:'pouch_' + F.id, pouch:F, x:Math.round(x0 + n*(PK_POUCH_W + gap)), y:yRow + Math.round((hsh(fi, 31) - 0.5)*6), rot:0, depth:3, n:-10 + n0, S:P.S, Sh:P.Sh, sh:P.sh, sheen:P.sheen, fi:fi });
    });
    L.forEach(function(id, n){ var s = PK_LOOSE_SLOTS[n % PK_LOOSE_SLOTS.length]; out.push({ id:id, x:s[0], y:s[1], rot:s[2], depth:n < 3 ? 0 : 1, n:n }); });
  } else {
    out.push({ id:'kodama', x:384, y:300, rot:-0.07, depth:3, deep:true, n:99 });   /* one layer down, always: too far to reach */
  }
  out.sort(function(a, b){ return b.depth - a.depth || a.n - b.n; });
  out.forEach(function(it){
    if (it.pouch) return;
    var D = PK_DEPTH[it.depth], A = PK_ART[it.id];
    it.sc = A.lie*D[0]; it.light = (it.deep ? 0.32 : D[1])*(1 - 0.08*Math.min(3, POK.page || 0));
    it.S = pkSprite(it.id, it.sc, it.rot, it.light); it.sh = it.S ? pkShadow(it.S) : null; it.Sh = null;
  });
  POK.items = out;
  pkNav();
};
/* the background is kept as the main one, so each little pocket's inside can be drawn over it */
var _pkBuildBgF = pkBuildBg;
pkBuildBg = function(done){ _pkBuildBgF(function(){ POK.bgMain = POK.bg; done(); }); };

/* ---- going in and out ---- */
/* the pocket's sounds (Aisling, Oct 24: "very small for the pocket and an even smaller version for the sub pocket …
   sensory friendly … tiny and subtle, but add to the sensory in a pleasant way"). made here, not recorded: soft wool
   brushing wool, low-passed so nothing is sharp, a slow start so nothing clicks, never quite the same twice, and never
   twice in a quarter second. the pocket opening is the "big" one; a little pocket is smaller and softer; coming back
   out is smaller still. (a recording of real fabric could replace these later.) */
var PK_SND = { last:0 };
function pkRustle(len, level, tone){
  var S = SND; if (!S || !S.on || !S.ctx || !S.dry) return;
  var now = performance.now(); if (now - PK_SND.last < 250) return; PK_SND.last = now;
  try {
    /* (Oct 24, v2: Aisling: "too hard, like something hard hitting, instead of soft fabric") no low end and no
       bumps now: a hand sliding over cloth. it swells in and out (no edge anywhere), a soft hiss in the middle
       frequencies, a slow uneven flutter, and a dust of very small catches, the way wool drags */
    var c = S.ctx, t = c.currentTime + 0.01, n = Math.floor(c.sampleRate*len), b = c.createBuffer(1, n, c.sampleRate), d = b.getChannelData(0);
    var fl = 0, flT = 0, cr = 0, peak = 0.38 + Math.random()*0.12;
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      var e = i/n, env = e < peak ? Math.pow(Math.sin(e/peak*Math.PI/2), 2) : Math.pow(Math.cos((e - peak)/(1 - peak)*Math.PI/2), 2);
      if ((i & 255) === 0) flT = 0.7 + Math.random()*0.3; fl += (flT - fl)*0.0015;   /* slow, uneven flutter */
      if (Math.random() < 0.004) cr = 0.5 + Math.random()*0.5; cr *= 0.985;   /* tiny catches, each fading in a couple of ms */
      d[i] = (Math.random()*2 - 1)*env*fl*(1 + cr*0.6);
    }
    var src = c.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = b; src.playbackRate.value = tone*(0.97 + Math.random()*0.06);
    var hp = c.createBiquadFilter(); hp.type = 'highpass'; hp.frequency.value = 650*tone; hp.Q.value = 0.5;
    var lp = c.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 3600*tone; lp.Q.value = 0.4;
    var lp2 = c.createBiquadFilter(); lp2.type = 'lowpass'; lp2.frequency.value = 5200; lp2.Q.value = 0.3;
    var g = c.createGain(); g.gain.value = level;
    src.connect(hp); hp.connect(lp); lp.connect(lp2); lp2.connect(g); g.connect(S.dry); src.start(t);
  } catch (e) {}
}
var PK_SND_LV = { open:0.016, into:0.0102, out:0.0079 };   /* (Oct 24) v2: matched by ear-weighted loudness (A-weighting) to the first version after it was made 5% softer */   /* set against the pickup sound: the pocket opening a touch above it, a little pocket below it, out below that */
function pkFoldSnd(open){ if (open) pkRustle(0.26, PK_SND_LV.into, 1.05); else pkRustle(0.22, PK_SND_LV.out, 0.9); }
function pkInto(F){
  if (!POK.ready || POK.lift || POK.turn) return;
  POK.turn = { t0:performance.now(), d:1, np:0, swapped:false, fold:F.id }; POK.hover = -1;
  pkCap.textContent = 'you open the little pocket.'; pkFoldSnd(true);
}
function pkOut(){
  /* (Oct 30, Aisling: "out not working on capsule toys": with a prize held up, out did nothing. now it puts the prize back down, then goes out) */
  if (POK.ready && POK.lift && !POK.turn && POK.folder) { var L = POK.lift; if (L.t1 < 0) L.t1 = performance.now(); setTimeout(pkOut, 360); return; }
  if (!POK.ready || POK.lift || POK.turn || !POK.folder) return;
  POK.turn = { t0:performance.now(), d:-1, np:0, swapped:false, fold:null }; POK.hover = -1;
  pkCap.textContent = 'you come back out.'; pkFoldSnd(false);
}
var _pkTurnF = pkTurn;
pkTurn = function(d){ if (POK.folder && d < 0 && !(POK.page || 0)) { pkOut(); return; } _pkTurnF(d); };
pkNav = function(){
  var bs = pkDeep.querySelectorAll('button'), P = POK.pages || 1, p = POK.page || 0;
  bs[0].hidden = p <= 0 && !POK.folder; bs[0].textContent = (POK.folder && p === 0) ? '↑ out · наружу' : '↑ back up · выше';
  bs[1].hidden = p >= P - 1;
  document.getElementById('pk-layer').textContent = P > 1 ? (p + 1) + ' / ' + P : '';
};
/* tapping a little pocket opens it (before the pocket's own tap, which would try to lift it) */
pkEl.addEventListener('pointerup', function(e){
  if (e.target.tagName === 'BUTTON' || !POK.ready || POK.lift || POK.turn) return;
  var h = pkHit(pkPos(e)); if (h < 0) return; var it = POK.items[h]; if (!it.pouch) return;
  e.stopPropagation(); pkInto(it.pouch);
}, true);
/* empty in there */
var _pkFrameF = pkFrame;
pkFrame = function(now){
  var TU = POK.turn;
  _pkFrameF(now);
  if (TU && !POK.turn && POK.folder && !pkList().length) pkCap.textContent = 'nothing in here yet.';
  if (!POK.ready || POK.lift || POK.turn) return;
  var x = pkCx;
  POK.items.forEach(function(it){
    if (!it.pouch || !it.sheen) return;
    var ox = Math.round(it.x - it.S.W/2), oy = Math.round(it.y - it.S.H/2), p = frac(now/5200 + it.fi*0.23)*1.8 - 0.4, bx = ox + p*it.S.W;
    x.save(); x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(bx, oy); x.lineTo(bx + 14, oy); x.lineTo(bx + 14 - it.S.H*0.5, oy + it.S.H); x.lineTo(bx - it.S.H*0.5, oy + it.S.H); x.closePath(); x.clip();
    x.globalAlpha = 0.55; x.drawImage(it.sheen, ox, oy); x.restore(); x.globalAlpha = 1;
  });
};
/* opening the pocket always starts at the top, outside the little pockets */
var _pkOpenF = pkOpen;
pkOpen = function(){ var was = POK.open; if (!was) { POK.folder = null; POK.page = 0; } _pkOpenF(); if (!was && POK.open) pkRustle(0.34, PK_SND_LV.open, 1); if (!was && POK.ready) pkLayout(); };
/* Esc (or P) inside a little pocket comes back out first; the close button still closes */
var _pkCloseF = pkClose;
pkClose = function(){
  var ev = window.event;
  if (POK.open && POK.folder && ev && ev.type === 'keydown') { if (!POK.turn) pkOut(); return; }
  _pkCloseF();
};

/* the sound switch sat on the pocket's frame, hard to see and hit (Aisling, Oct 24): while the pocket is open
   it moves into the clear dark inside the frame, right of the title */
(function(){ var st = document.createElement('style'); st.textContent = '#stage.pk-open #snd-switch{top:8.6%!important;right:7.2%!important;background:rgba(10,9,8,.6)!important;}'; document.head.appendChild(st); })();

/* a key trap for overlays inside the pocket (the album, the dream book): the pocket's own key handling steps
   aside for a focused input, so while one of these is open a hidden input holds the focus and the overlay
   gets the arrows and Esc. pointer-fine screens only (a phone would pop its keyboard). */
var PK_TRAP = document.createElement('input'); PK_TRAP.readOnly = true; PK_TRAP.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); PK_TRAP.tabIndex = -1;
PK_TRAP.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:-9999px;top:0;width:1px;height:1px;opacity:0;';
pkEl.appendChild(PK_TRAP);
function pkTrap(on){ try { if (on && window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(pointer:fine)').matches) PK_TRAP.focus({ preventScroll:true }); else PK_TRAP.blur(); } catch (e) {} }

;

/* ---- copied from null.grey-furusatostation/src/f8-sound.js ---- */

/* ================================================================
   f8 — FURUSATO'S SOUND (the immersive-space kit, doc 29). the same rules as the station:
   off until asked for (the switch, top right, the same memory as the station page, so it's
   on here if you turned it on there), quiet, no hover sounds.
     - footsteps: the station's own recording, crisp on tile, a short bright echo
     - the tubes: the station's real fluorescent hum, low, everywhere on the platform
     - the pocket: the pickup sound for each kind of thing; cans open, bottles pop, a sip, a bite, a wrapper
   Furusato's own sounds (its station drifting in, the departure melody, the gacha, the ramen counter)
   come later (step 9). the code is the station's hr-sound.js / hr-sound-rec.js / r3k / hph, trimmed.
   ================================================================ */
var SND = { on:false, ctx:null, el:null, step:0, lx:0, ly:0, buf:{} };
try { SND.on = localStorage.getItem('nullgrey.between.sound') === 'on'; } catch (e) {}
function sndImpulse(ctx, sec, decay, bright){
  var n = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate*sec), b = ctx.createBuffer(2, n, ctx.sampleRate);
  for (var c = 0; c < 2; c++) { var d = b.getChannelData(c), lp = 0; for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) { var w = Math.random()*2 - 1; lp += (w - lp)*bright; d[i] = lp*Math.pow(1 - i/n, decay); } }
  return b;
}
function sndBuild(){
  var ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(), S = SND; S.ctx = ctx;
  S.master = ctx.createGain(); S.master.gain.value = 0; S.lim = ctx.createDynamicsCompressor(); S.lim.threshold.value = -10; S.lim.ratio.value = 12; S.master.connect(S.lim); S.lim.connect(ctx.destination);
  S.revTile = ctx.createConvolver(); S.revTile.buffer = sndImpulse(ctx, 1.1, 3.2, 0.5);   /* tile all round: a short, bright echo */
  S.sendTile = ctx.createGain(); S.sendTile.gain.value = 0.32; S.sendTile.connect(S.revTile); S.revTile.connect(S.master);
  S.dry = ctx.createGain(); S.dry.connect(S.master); S.dry.connect(S.sendTile);
  S.hum = ctx.createGain(); S.hum.gain.value = 0; S.hum.connect(S.master);
  Object.keys(SND_DATA).forEach(function(k){
    try { fetch(SND_DATA[k]).then(function(r){ return r.arrayBuffer(); }).then(function(a){ return ctx.decodeAudioData(a); }).then(function(b){ S.buf[k] = b; if (k === 'fl_hum') { var h = ctx.createBufferSource(); h.buffer = b; h.loop = true; h.connect(S.hum); h.start(); } }).catch(function(){}); } catch (e) {}
  });
}
function sndOneShot(k, out, vol, rate, off){
  var S = SND; if (!S.on || !S.ctx || !S.buf[k]) return null;
  var s = S.ctx.createBufferSource(), g = S.ctx.createGain(); s.buffer = S.buf[k]; s.playbackRate.value = rate || 1; g.gain.value = vol; s.connect(g); g.connect(out); s.start(0, off || 0); return s;
}
var SND_VOL = 3.2;
var SND_STEPS = [0.0,0.35,0.7,1.05,1.4,1.75,2.1,2.45,2.8,3.15,3.5,3.85], SND_STEPLEN = 0.31, SND_LASTSTEP = -1;
function sndFoot(){
  var S = SND; if (!S.on || !S.ctx || !S.buf.steps) return;
  var k; do { k = Math.floor(Math.random()*SND_STEPS.length); } while (k === SND_LASTSTEP); SND_LASTSTEP = k;
  var src = S.ctx.createBufferSource(), g = S.ctx.createGain();
  src.buffer = S.buf.steps; src.playbackRate.value = 1.06 + (Math.random() - 0.5)*0.08; g.gain.value = 0.0675;
  src.connect(g); g.connect(S.dry); src.start(0, SND_STEPS[k], SND_STEPLEN);
}
function sndStep(now, dt){
  var S = SND; if (!S.on || !S.ctx) return;
  var T = S.ctx.currentTime, ch = cellAt(Math.floor(me.x), Math.floor(me.y));
  S.master.gain.setTargetAtTime(NGJ.leaving ? 0 : SND_VOL, T, 0.3);
  var under = ch === 'n' ? 0 : (ch === 'g' || ch === 'k' || ch === 'r' || ch === 'l' ? 0.5 : 1);   /* the side rooms are quieter; the narrow way has no tubes */
  S.hum.gain.setTargetAtTime(0.007*under, T, 0.3);
  var mv = Math.hypot(me.x - S.lx, me.y - S.ly); S.lx = me.x; S.ly = me.y;
  if (mv > 0 && mv < 0.5) { S.step += mv; if (S.step > 0.62) { S.step = 0; sndFoot(); } }
}
function sndSwitch(){
  if (SND.el) return;
  var el = document.createElement('button'); el.type = 'button'; el.id = 'snd-switch'; SND.el = el;
  el.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;right:2%;top:3%;z-index:30;font:10px "IBM Plex Mono",monospace;letter-spacing:.08em;color:rgba(228,220,208,.55);background:rgba(10,9,8,.35);border:0;padding:3px 7px;cursor:' + HANDS.pointer + ';';
  function label(){ el.textContent = SND.on ? '音 · sound on' : '音 · sound off'; }
  label();
  ['pointerdown', 'mousedown', 'touchstart'].forEach(function(t){ el.addEventListener(t, function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); }); });
  el.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    e.stopPropagation(); SND.on = !SND.on; label();
    try { localStorage.setItem('nullgrey.between.sound', SND.on ? 'on' : 'off'); } catch (er) {}
    if (SND.on) { if (!SND.ctx) { try { sndBuild(); } catch (er2) { SND.on = false; label(); return; } } SND.ctx.resume(); SND.lx = me.x; SND.ly = me.y; }
    else if (SND.ctx) SND.master.gain.setTargetAtTime(0, SND.ctx.currentTime, 0.1);
  });
  document.getElementById('stage').appendChild(el);
}
function sndWake(){ if (!SND.on) return; try { if (!SND.ctx) sndBuild(); SND.ctx.resume(); SND.lx = me.x; SND.ly = me.y; } catch (e) {} }
window.addEventListener('pointerdown', sndWake, { once:true }); window.addEventListener('keydown', sndWake, { once:true });
var _seamStepSnd = seamStep;
seamStep = function(now, dt){ _seamStepSnd(now, dt); if (!SND.el && document.getElementById('stage')) sndSwitch();
  if (SND.el) { var hide = typeof POK !== 'undefined' && POK.open; if (SND.el.style.visibility !== (hide ? 'hidden' : 'visible')) SND.el.style.visibility = hide ? 'hidden' : 'visible'; }   /* (Oct 30, Aisling: it overlapped the pocket's frame and its windows; it steps aside while the pocket is open) */
  try { sndStep(now, dt); } catch (e) {} };

/* ---- the pocket: drinking and eating (the station's hph-phone-sound.js) ---- */
var DRK_CAN = { coffee:1, alienwave:1, fogcan:1 }, DRK_BOT = { smile:1, bottle:1 }, DRK_POPPED = {}, DRK_SIP = { coffee:1, alienwave:1, smile:1, bottle:1 };
var DRK_WRAP = { onigiri:'long', sleep:'short' }, DRK_EAT = { buckeye:'crisp', onigiri:'soft' };
function drkFx(k, vol, rate, delay){ setTimeout(function(){ var S = SND; if (S.on && S.ctx) sndOneShot(k, S.dry, vol, rate); }, delay || 0); }
function drkSip(delay){ setTimeout(function(){ var S = SND; if (S.on && S.ctx) sndOneShot('dr_sip', S.dry, 0.16, 0.97 + Math.random()*0.06); }, delay || 0); }
(function(){
  var ask = document.getElementById('pk-ask'); if (!ask) return;
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    try {
      if (!ask.contains(e.target)) return;
      var b = e.target.closest('button'); if (!b || !POK.ask || b.getAttribute('data-a') !== '1') return;
      var it = POK.lift && POK.lift.it; if (!it) return;
      var can = DRK_CAN[it.id], sip = DRK_SIP[it.id], S = SND;
      var bot = DRK_BOT[it.id] && !(it.id === 'bottle' && DRK_POPPED.bottle); if (bot) { DRK_POPPED[it.id] = 1; if (S.on && S.ctx) sndOneShot('dr_bopen', S.dry, 0.32, 0.97 + Math.random()*0.06); }
      if (can && S.on && S.ctx) sndOneShot('dr_open', S.dry, 0.3, 0.96 + Math.random()*0.08);
      if (sip) drkSip(can ? 520 : (bot ? 900 : 120));
      var wr = DRK_WRAP[it.id], eat = DRK_EAT[it.id];
      if (wr) drkFx(wr === 'long' ? 'wrap_long' : 'wrap_short', 0.22, 0.95 + Math.random()*0.1, 0);
      if (eat) drkFx('eat_crunch', eat === 'soft' ? 0.12 : 0.16, eat === 'soft' ? 0.82 : 0.95 + Math.random()*0.08, wr ? 950 : 80);
    } catch (e2) {}
  }, true);
})();

/* ---- pickup sounds by kind (the station's r3k-pickup-sound.js) ---- */
var PK_TIER = {
  passport:'none', passportOpen:'none', multipass:'none', note:'none', lint:'none', kodama:'none', compactOpen:'none',
  bottle:'basic', sprats:'basic', sleep:'basic', smile:'basic', coffee:'basic', buckeye:'basic', onigiri:'basic', alienwave:'basic', candle:'basic',
  newspaper:'special', postcard:'special', cdcoupon:'special', compact:'special',
  fogcan:'weird', glove:'weird', unicorn:'weird', quarter:'weird', strangerNote:'weird',
  pass:'plot', ticket:'plot'
};
var PK_TIER_SND = { basic:'pk_basic', special:'pk_special', weird:'pk_weird', plot:'pk_plot' };
var PK_TIER_VOL = { basic:0.12, special:0.14, weird:0.14, plot:0.16 };
function pkTierSound(id){
  var t = PK_TIER[id] || 'basic', k = PK_TIER_SND[t], S = SND;
  if (!k || !S.on || !S.ctx || !S.buf || !S.buf[k]) return;
  setTimeout(function(){ try { sndOneShot(k, S.dry, PK_TIER_VOL[t], 1); } catch (e) {} }, 120);
}
var _pocketPutTier = pocketPut;
pocketPut = function(id){ var ok = _pocketPutTier(id); if (ok) pkTierSound(id); return ok; };

;

/* ================================================================
   F03 — THE DRINKS MACHINE WORKS (Oct 29; Aisling: "i would liek to see these sodas, the machine would work in the same
   as the other vending machines, but not a beep for the button push i think it would just have the same sounds as
   platform one buttons and machine")
   Main Street borrows Furusato's machine close-up (f6x-vend.js) as it is. This page has no shop of its own, so this
   file gives it the few things f6x asks for (its sound slots, how many are left, the names), before f6x loads. the
   sounds are Platform 1's: its heavy button (vend_btn1, the hub's v_btn1), the coin, the whirr, the drop, the change,
   the flap. no ピポ.
   ================================================================ */
var FSND = typeof FSND !== 'undefined' ? FSND : {};
var F_SMOKES = typeof F_SMOKES !== 'undefined' ? F_SMOKES : (function(){ var a = []; for (var i = 0; i < 12; i++) a.push(['', '#5e5a56']); return a; })();
var SHOP = typeof SHOP !== 'undefined' ? SHOP : { got:{} }, SHOP_ONCE = typeof SHOP_ONCE !== 'undefined' ? SHOP_ONCE : {}, F_MYSTERY = typeof F_MYSTERY !== 'undefined' ? F_MYSTERY : [];
function shopName(id){ var f = FU_ITEMS[id]; return f ? { jp:f.jp, en:f.en, price:f.price } : { jp:id, en:'', price:1 }; }
function shopLeft(id){ return 3 - (SHOP.got[id] || 0); }   /* three of each a visit, like Furusato's machines */
function shopOpen(){}
var F3_VSND = { vend_btn:['vend_btn1', 0.4], vend_coin:['vend_coin', 0.32], vend_drop:['vend_drop', 0.42], vend_hum:['vend_hum', 0.05], vend_change:['vend_change', 0.3], vend_flap:['vend_flap', 0.42] };
function fSnd(k, gain){ var q = F3_VSND[k]; if (!q) return false; try { var S = SND; if (!S || !S.on || !S.ctx || !S.buf || !S.buf[q[0]]) return !!(S && S.buf && S.buf[q[0]]); sndOneShot(q[0], S.dry, q[1]*(gain || 1)*2.2, k === 'vend_btn' ? 0.92 + Math.random()*0.1 : 1); } catch (e) {} return true; }   /* (true even with the sound off, so nothing beeps instead) */

;
/* ---- copied from null.grey-furusatostation/src/f6x-vend.js ---- */
/* ================================================================
   f6x — THE VENDING MACHINES UP CLOSE (Oct 27; Aisling: "the function of the vending machine and views should match
   the hub and station one, just the aesthetics are different. But the ticket thing for ramen should stay the same.")
   the Smile Soda machine and the two tobacco machines now work the way №333 does in the hub (doc 19): the machine drawn
   big, taller than the screen (drag, scroll or the arrow keys to look up and down it). press the button under a thing →
   the money is somehow in your pocket → coins drop one by one, the display counts down, the machine shudders → it falls
   into the tray; push the flap and it's in your pocket. three of each a visit (the same count the old panel kept), the
   wrapped packages once ever. sold out is sold out. the ramen ticket machine, the kiosk and the boba keep their panels.
   ================================================================ */
var FVM = { open:false, w:null, viewY:0, viewT:0, disp:'', busy:false, flap:null, shake:0, first:true, raf:0, press:null, coin:0, fall:null, flapT:0, sc:{}, drag:null };
FSND.vend_btn = { kind:'one', vol:0.3, what:'a vending machine button pressed (under 1 s). until there is a file, a made one plays: a soft plastic click and a little two-note ピポ, cute and Japanese (Aisling, Oct 27: "something cute and japan coded"; she may swap it later)' };
FSND.vend_coin = { kind:'one', vol:0.32, what:'a coin going into a vending machine (Oct 27: the hub\'s v_coin, copied over)' };
FSND.vend_drop = { kind:'one', vol:0.42, what:'the thing falling and landing in the vending machine\'s tray (Oct 27: the hub\'s v_drop, copied over)' };
FSND.vend_hum = { kind:'one', vol:0.035, what:'a vending machine working as it drops something: a short low whirr, very quiet (Oct 27: the hub\'s v_rumble, copied over, at the hub\'s soft level)' };
FSND.vend_change = { kind:'one', vol:0.3, what:'a vending machine: change rattling down inside after the thing drops (Oct 27: the hub\'s v_change, copied over)' };
FSND.vend_flap = { kind:'one', vol:0.42, what:'a vending machine\'s tray flap pushed open and swinging back (Oct 27: the hub\'s v_flap, copied over)' };
function fvmClick(){   /* the stand-in button: a soft plastic click, then ピポ, two little sine notes (C7, E7), quiet and short */
  var S = SND; if (!S || !S.on || !S.ctx || !S.dry) return;
  try { var c = S.ctx, t = c.currentTime + 0.01, L = Math.floor(c.sampleRate*0.015), b = c.createBuffer(1, L, c.sampleRate), d = b.getChannelData(0); for (var i = 0; i < L; i++) d[i] = (Math.random()*2 - 1)*Math.pow(1 - i/L, 4);
    var n = c.createBufferSource(), f = c.createBiquadFilter(), g = c.createGain(); n.buffer = b; f.type = 'bandpass'; f.frequency.value = 2800; f.Q.value = 1.2; g.gain.value = 0.12; n.connect(f); f.connect(g); g.connect(S.dry); n.start(t);
    [[2093, 0.03], [2637, 0.11]].forEach(function(q){ var o = c.createOscillator(), e = c.createGain(), t0 = t + q[1]; o.type = 'sine'; o.frequency.value = q[0]; e.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t0); e.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.045, t0 + 0.008); e.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0008, t0 + 0.09); o.connect(e); e.connect(S.dry); o.start(t0); o.stop(t0 + 0.1); });
  } catch (e) {}
}
function fvmSnd(k){ try { if (k === 'btn') { if (!fSnd('vend_btn')) fvmClick(); } else if (k === 'coin') { if (!fSnd('vend_coin')) fSnd('coin', 0.8); } else if (k === 'drop') { if (!fSnd('vend_drop')) fSnd('shop_thunk'); } else fSnd('vend_' + k); } catch (e) {} }

/* each machine: its picture, where its things are (in its own units), its display, slot, tray */
var FVM_DEFS = {};
(function(){
  var ids = ['f_smile_sakura', 'f_smile_ichigo', 'f_smile_lemon', 'f_smile_cherry', 'f_smile_static', '_out', 'smile', 'f_vsplesk', 'coffee', 'onigiri'], sl = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < 10; i++) { var c = i % 5, r = (i/5) | 0, x = 9 + c*17.5, y = 40 + r*38; sl.push({ x:x, y:y, w:15, h:35, id:ids[i], btn:[x + 5, y + 32.5, 5, 2.2], col:F_SODAS[i][0] }); }
  FVM_DEFS.smile = { pic:'smile', K:1.7, slots:sl, disp:[70, 122, 22, 12], coin:[74, 138, 3, 8], note:[80, 139, 10, 2.5], tray:[10, 155, 80, 20], win:[6, 36, 88, 80], top:28, out:'売切 · sold out. it always has been.' };
  var t0 = [];
  for (var j = 0; j < 12; j++) { var x0 = 5 + (j % 4)*10.6, y0 = 18 + ((j/4) | 0)*16; t0.push({ x:x0, y:y0, w:8.4, h:15, id:j === 0 ? 'f_pack_seven' : (j === 1 ? 'f_pack_mir' : '_so'), btn:[x0 + 2.2, y0 + 12.4, 4, 1.6], col:F_SMOKES[j][1] }); }
  var tob = { K:3.4, disp:[37.5, 80.5, 9.5, 6], coin:[34, 81, 2, 6], tray:[8, 88, 34, 7], win:[3, 16, 44, 50], top:12, out:'売切 · sold out. most of them always are.' };
  FVM_DEFS.smokes = Object.assign({ pic:'tobacco0', slots:t0, side:'smokes2' }, tob);
  var t1 = [];
  for (var k = 0; k < 4; k++) t1.push({ x:5 + k*10.6, y:18, w:8.4, h:15, id:'_so', btn:[7.2 + k*10.6, 30.4, 4, 1.6], col:F_SMOKES[(k + 5) % 12][1] });
  t1.push({ x:4, y:35, w:8.6, h:15, id:'f_matchpatches', col:'#d6c39f' }, { x:13.6, y:35, w:8.6, h:15, id:'f_matchmomo', col:'#5e4652' }, { x:23.2, y:35, w:8.6, h:15, id:'f_matchwg', col:'#b08890' }, { x:33, y:34, w:11, h:16, id:'f_cornsoup', col:'#c9a067' });
  [5, 19, 33].forEach(function(x, q){ t1.push({ x:x, y:55, w:12, h:11, id:['f_mys_book', 'f_mys_robot', 'f_mys_tickets'][q], col:'#a38b73' }); });
  FVM_DEFS.smokes2 = Object.assign({ pic:'tobacco1', slots:t1, side:'smokes' }, tob);
})();

var fvmEl = document.createElement('div'); fvmEl.id = 'fvm';
fvmEl.innerHTML = '<canvas id="fvmv" width="320" height="180"></canvas><div id="fvm-cap"></div><div id="fvm-btns"><button type="button" data-a="back">戻る · step back</button></div>';
stage.appendChild(fvmEl);
(function(){ var st = document.createElement('style'); st.textContent =
  '#fvm{position:absolute;inset:0;display:none;z-index:41;background:#0a0908;}#fvm.open{display:block;}' +
  '#fvmv{position:absolute;left:50%;top:44%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);width:min(100%,calc(74vh*16/9));aspect-ratio:16/9;image-rendering:pixelated;cursor:pointer;touch-action:none;}' +
  '#fvm-cap{position:absolute;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);bottom:12.5%;width:min(680px,90%);text-align:center;font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.06em;line-height:1.5;color:rgba(228,220,208,.94);text-shadow:0 0 6px #000,0 0 2px #000;min-height:16px;}' +
