  '#fvm-btns button{font-family:inherit;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:rgba(228,220,208,.9);background:rgba(42,32,41,.92);border:1px solid rgba(140,137,131,.7);padding:3px 12px;cursor:pointer;}#fvm-btns button:hover{background:rgba(92,74,99,.95);}';
  document.head.appendChild(st); })();
['wheel', 'click'].forEach(function(ev){ fvmEl.addEventListener(ev, function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); }); });
fvmEl.querySelector('#fvm-btns').addEventListener('click', function(e){ if (e.target.closest('button')) fvmClose(); });
fvmEl.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); if (e.target.id !== 'fvmv') return; FVM.drag = { y:e.clientY, v:FVM.viewT, moved:false }; });
fvmEl.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e){
  if (e.target.id !== 'fvmv') return;
  if (FVM.drag) { var R = e.target.getBoundingClientRect(), dy = (e.clientY - FVM.drag.y)*180/R.height; if (Math.abs(dy) > 3) FVM.drag.moved = true; FVM.viewT = fvmClamp(FVM.drag.v - dy); return; }
  if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && !FVM.busy) { var s = fvmHit(e); if (s && s !== FVM.hov) { FVM.hov = s; fvmCap(fvmLabel(s)); } }
});
fvmEl.addEventListener('pointerup', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); var d = FVM.drag; FVM.drag = null; if (e.target.id === 'fvmv' && d && !d.moved) fvmClickAt(e); });
fvmEl.addEventListener('wheel', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); FVM.viewT = fvmClamp(FVM.viewT + e.deltaY*0.3); }, { passive:false });
window.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if (!FVM.open) return; var k = e.key || '';
  if (k === 'Escape') fvmClose(); else if (k === 'ArrowUp' || k === 'w' || k === 'W') FVM.viewT = fvmClamp(FVM.viewT - 24); else if (k === 'ArrowDown' || k === 's' || k === 'S') FVM.viewT = fvmClamp(FVM.viewT + 24);
  e.stopImmediatePropagation(); }, true);
window.addEventListener('keyup', function(e){ if (FVM.open) e.stopImmediatePropagation(); }, true);
function fvmCap(t){ document.getElementById('fvm-cap').textContent = t; }

/* the scene: the machine at K px a unit, the wall behind it, its neighbour's edge, the floor; put through the inks */
function fvmScene(w){
  var D = FVM_DEFS[w], P = FPIC[D.pic], K = D.K, mw = P.vbw*K, mh = P.vbh*K, MX = Math.round((320 - mw)/2), MY = 22, H = Math.ceil(MY + mh + 22);
  var s = '<rect width="320" height="' + H + '" fill="#cfcac0"/>';
  for (var y = 0; y < H - 22; y += 14) for (var x = (y/14 % 2)*7; x < 320; x += 14) s += '<rect x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" width="13.4" height="13.4" fill="#dcd8d0"/>';   /* the back wall's tiles */
  s += '<rect y="' + (MY + mh) + '" width="320" height="22" fill="#8e877c"/><rect y="' + (MY + mh) + '" width="320" height="2" fill="#6c6560"/>';   /* the floor */
  s += '<rect x="' + (MX + mw) + '" y="' + (MY + 6) + '" width="10" height="' + (mh - 6) + '" fill="#000" opacity="0.16"/><ellipse cx="' + (MX + mw/2) + '" cy="' + (MY + mh + 2) + '" rx="' + (mw/2 + 8) + '" ry="5" fill="#000" opacity="0.28"/>';   /* its shadow on the wall and the floor */
  if (D.side) { var nx = D.side === 'smokes2' ? MX - mw - 0.02*K*100 : MX + mw + 0.02*K*100;   /* (facing them, the east one is on the left) */ s += '<g transform="translate(' + nx + ' ' + MY + ') scale(' + K + ')">' + FPIC[FVM_DEFS[D.side].pic].svg() + '</g><rect x="' + nx + '" y="' + MY + '" width="' + mw + '" height="' + mh + '" fill="#0a0908" opacity="0.32"/>'; }
  s += '<g transform="translate(' + MX + ' ' + MY + ') scale(' + K + ')">' + P.svg();
  /* its edges: the steel case's rounded corners catching the light on the left, in shade on the right */
  s += '<rect x="0" y="0" width="1.2" height="' + P.vbh + '" fill="#ffffff" opacity="0.22"/><rect x="' + (P.vbw - 1.6) + '" y="0" width="1.6" height="' + P.vbh + '" fill="#000" opacity="0.22"/>';
  /* the glass: a soft sheen, scuffs round the buttons */
  var W = D.win; s += '<path d="M' + W[0] + ',' + (W[1] + W[3]*0.5) + ' L' + (W[0] + W[2]*0.45) + ',' + W[1] + ' L' + (W[0] + W[2]*0.62) + ',' + W[1] + ' L' + W[0] + ',' + (W[1] + W[3]*0.78) + ' Z" fill="#ffffff" opacity="0.07"/>';
  D.slots.forEach(function(q, i){ if (q.btn) s += '<ellipse cx="' + (q.btn[0] + q.btn[2]/2) + '" cy="' + (q.btn[1] + q.btn[3]/2) + '" rx="' + (q.btn[2]*0.9) + '" ry="' + (q.btn[3]*0.9) + '" fill="#8a8076" opacity="' + (0.08 + (i*7 % 5)*0.03) + '"/>'; });
  return { svg:s + '</g>', K:K, MX:MX, MY:MY, W:320, H:H, mh:mh };
}
function fvmBuild(w){
  if (FVM.sc[w]) return;
  var Q = fvmScene(w), img = new Image(); FVM.sc[w] = { K:Q.K, MX:Q.MX, MY:Q.MY, H:Q.H, c:null };
  img.onload = function(){
    var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = 320; c.height = Q.H; var x = c.getContext('2d'); x.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
    try { var im = x.getImageData(0, 0, 320, Q.H), d = im.data; for (var i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4) { var q = pkInk(d[i], d[i + 1], d[i + 2]); d[i] = q[2]; d[i + 1] = q[3]; d[i + 2] = q[4]; } x.putImageData(im, 0, 0); } catch (e) {}
    FVM.sc[w].c = c;
  };
  img.src = 'data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent('<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 320 ' + Q.H + '" width="320" height="' + Q.H + '">' + Q.svg + '</svg>');
}
function fvmClamp(v){ var S = FVM.sc[FVM.w]; return S ? Math.max(0, Math.min(S.H - 180, v)) : 0; }
function fvmHit(e){
  var S = FVM.sc[FVM.w], D = FVM_DEFS[FVM.w], R = e.target.getBoundingClientRect(); if (!S) return null;
  var vx = ((e.clientX - R.left)/R.width*320 - S.MX)/S.K, vy = ((e.clientY - R.top)/R.height*180 + FVM.viewY - S.MY)/S.K;
  FVM.hx = vx; FVM.hy = vy;
  for (var i = 0; i < D.slots.length; i++) { var q = D.slots[i]; if (vx >= q.x && vx <= q.x + q.w && vy >= q.y && vy <= q.y + q.h) return q; }
  return null;
}
function fvmLabel(q){
  if (q.id === '_out' || q.id === '_so') return '売切 · sold out';
  if (SHOP_ONCE[q.id]) return '"' + F_MYSTERY[['f_mys_book', 'f_mys_robot', 'f_mys_tickets'].indexOf(q.id)].join(' ') + '"  · ' + shopName(q.id).price + ' д';
  var n = shopName(q.id); return n.jp + ' · ' + n.en + ' · ' + n.price + ' д' + (shopLeft(q.id) > 0 ? '' : ' · 売切');
}
function fvmClickAt(e){
  var q = fvmHit(e), D = FVM_DEFS[FVM.w], x = FVM.hx, y = FVM.hy, inR = function(r){ return r && x >= r[0] - 1 && x <= r[0] + r[2] + 1 && y >= r[1] - 1 && y <= r[1] + r[3] + 1; };
  if (q) { fvmBuy(q); return; }
  if (inR(D.tray)) { fvmFlap(); return; }
  if (inR(D.coin)) { FVM.shake = performance.now() + 120; fvmCap('you jiggle the coin return. nothing comes back.'); return; }
  if (inR(D.note)) { fvmCap('千円札 · the note slot. it doesn\'t want your notes. it never has.'); return; }
}
function fvmBuy(q){
  if (FVM.busy) return;
  var D = FVM_DEFS[FVM.w], i = D.slots.indexOf(q);
  FVM.press = { i:i, t:performance.now() }; fvmSnd('btn');
  if (q.id === '_out' || q.id === '_so') { FVM.disp = '--'; fvmCap(D.out); setTimeout(function(){ FVM.disp = ''; }, 1400); return; }
  if (shopLeft(q.id) <= 0) { FVM.disp = '--'; fvmCap(SHOP_ONCE[q.id] ? 'there was only ever one of those.' : '売切 · sold out. (it comes back next visit.)'); setTimeout(function(){ FVM.disp = ''; }, 1400); return; }
  if (FVM.flap) { fvmCap('there\'s already something in the tray.'); return; }
  var price = shopName(q.id).price;
  FVM.busy = true; FVM.disp = '' + price;
  fvmCap(FVM.first ? 'you search your pocket. you don’t know how, but the money is there.' : 'the money is there.'); FVM.first = false;
  setTimeout(function(){
    var left = price;
    (function drop(){ if (!FVM.open) { FVM.busy = false; return; } FVM.coin = performance.now(); fvmSnd('coin'); setTimeout(function(){ left--; FVM.disp = left > 0 ? '' + left : '0'; if (left > 0) setTimeout(drop, 160); else paid(); }, 260); })();
  }, 700);
  function paid(){
    FVM.shake = performance.now() + 650; fvmSnd('hum');
    setTimeout(function(){
      SHOP.got[q.id] = (SHOP.got[q.id] || 0) + 1;
      FVM.fall = { x:q.x + q.w/2, y:q.y + q.h*0.4, t0:performance.now(), col:q.col };
      setTimeout(function(){
        FVM.fall = null; FVM.shake = performance.now() + 180; fvmSnd('drop'); setTimeout(function(){ fvmSnd('change'); }, 240);
        FVM.flap = q.id; FVM.busy = false; FVM.disp = ''; FVM.viewT = fvmClamp(1e4);
        fvmCap((SHOP_ONCE[q.id] ? 'something wrapped in brown paper' : shopName(q.id).en) + '. something dropped into the tray. (push the flap)');
      }, 900);
    }, 700);
  }
}
function fvmFlap(){
  if (!FVM.flap) { fvmSnd('flap'); fvmCap('the flap swings. nothing inside.'); return; }
  var id = FVM.flap; FVM.flap = null; FVM.flapT = performance.now(); fvmSnd('flap');
  setTimeout(function(){ var ok = pocketPut(id); fvmCap(ok ? '(' + (SHOP_ONCE[id] ? 'a brown-paper package' : shopName(id).en) + ' · it\'s in your pocket now.)' : 'you already have one. you leave it for someone else.'); }, 500);
}
function fvmFrame(now){
  if (!FVM.open) return; FVM.raf = requestAnimationFrame(fvmFrame);
  var cv = document.getElementById('fvmv'), g = cv.getContext('2d'), S = FVM.sc[FVM.w], D = FVM_DEFS[FVM.w];
  if (cv.width !== 320*(FVM.hi || 1)) { cv.width = 320*(FVM.hi || 1); cv.height = 180*(FVM.hi || 1); }
  g.setTransform(FVM.hi || 1, 0, 0, FVM.hi || 1, 0, 0); g.imageSmoothingEnabled = false; g.fillStyle = '#0a0908'; g.fillRect(0, 0, 320, 180);
  if (!S || !S.c) return;
  FVM.viewY += (FVM.viewT - FVM.viewY)*0.18;
  var sh = now < FVM.shake ? Math.round(Math.sin(now*0.09)*1.4) : 0, Y0 = -Math.round(FVM.viewY);
  g.drawImage(S.c, sh, Y0, 320, S.c.height/(S.hi || 1));   /* (Oct 31: the scene is drawn at three times the pixels, f7f) */
  function V(x, y){ return [S.MX + x*S.K + sh, S.MY + y*S.K + Y0]; }
  /* the lit window: a soft glow off the glass, very slowly breathing */
  var W = D.win, a = V(W[0], W[1]); g.save(); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,236,200,' + (0.05 + 0.012*Math.sin(now/2600)) + ')'; g.fillRect(a[0], a[1], W[2]*S.K, W[3]*S.K); g.restore();
  /* the display */
  var d0 = V(D.disp[0], D.disp[1]), dw = D.disp[2]*S.K, dh = D.disp[3]*S.K;
  g.fillStyle = '#121211'; g.fillRect(Math.round(d0[0]), Math.round(d0[1]), Math.round(dw), Math.round(dh));
  g.fillStyle = '#e0a040'; g.font = '900 ' + Math.round(dh*0.78) + 'px "IBM Plex Mono",monospace'; g.textBaseline = 'middle'; g.textAlign = 'right';
  g.fillText(FVM.disp || '0', Math.round(d0[0] + dw - 2), Math.round(d0[1] + dh/2 + 1));
  g.textAlign = 'left';
  /* a pressed button lights */
  if (FVM.press && now - FVM.press.t < 260) { var q = D.slots[FVM.press.i], b = q.btn || [q.x + q.w/2 - 2, q.y + q.h - 2, 4, 1.6], p = V(b[0], b[1]); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,220,150,0.55)'; g.fillRect(p[0] - 1, p[1] - 1, b[2]*S.K + 2, b[3]*S.K + 2); }
  /* a coin going in */
  if (FVM.coin && now - FVM.coin < 260) { var cp = V(D.coin[0] + D.coin[2]/2, D.coin[1] - 3 + (now - FVM.coin)/260*(D.coin[3] + 2)); g.fillStyle = '#b7b4ae'; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(cp[0], cp[1], 2.4, 1, 0, 0, 6.3); g.fill(); }
  /* the thing falling behind the glass, out of sight at the window's foot */
  if (FVM.fall) { var t = Math.min(1, (now - FVM.fall.t0)/650), bot = W[1] + W[3], fy = FVM.fall.y + t*t*(bot + 8 - FVM.fall.y), fp = V(FVM.fall.x, fy), top = V(0, W[1])[1], cut = V(0, bot)[1];
    g.save(); g.beginPath(); g.rect(0, top, 320, cut - top); g.clip(); g.fillStyle = FVM.fall.col || '#c8b496'; g.fillRect(fp[0] - 1.6*S.K, fp[1] - 2.5*S.K, 3.2*S.K, 5*S.K); g.restore(); }
  /* something waiting in the tray, a faint shape behind the flap; the flap pushed in */
  var T = D.tray;
  if (FVM.flap) { var fl = V(T[0] + 3, T[1] + T[3]*0.35); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(224,160,64,' + (0.12 + 0.05*Math.sin(now/600)) + ')'; g.fillRect(fl[0], fl[1], (T[2] - 6)*S.K, T[3]*0.4*S.K); }
  if (FVM.flapT && now - FVM.flapT < 500) { var f2 = V(T[0], T[1]); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)'; g.fillRect(f2[0], f2[1], T[2]*S.K, T[3]*S.K*(1 - (now - FVM.flapT)/500)); }
  /* there's more above or below */
  if (FVM.viewY > 3) { var tg = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 16); tg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(10,9,8,0.8)'); tg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(10,9,8,0)'); g.fillStyle = tg; g.fillRect(0, 0, 320, 16); }
  if (FVM.viewY < S.H - 183) { var bg = g.createLinearGradient(0, 164, 0, 180); bg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(10,9,8,0)'); bg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(10,9,8,0.8)'); g.fillStyle = bg; g.fillRect(0, 164, 320, 16); }
}
function fvmOpen(w){
  if (!FVM_DEFS[w]) return;
  FVM.open = true; FVM.w = w; FVM.flap = FVM.flap && FVM.flapW === w ? FVM.flap : null; FVM.flapW = FVM.flap ? FVM.flapW : w; if (!FVM.busy) FVM.disp = ''; FVM.hov = null; keys = {}; drag = null; stage.classList.remove('dragging');
  fvmBuild(w); var D = FVM_DEFS[w], S = FVM.sc[w]; FVM.viewY = FVM.viewT = Math.max(0, S.MY + D.top*D.K - 14);
  fvmEl.classList.add('open'); cancelAnimationFrame(FVM.raf); FVM.raf = requestAnimationFrame(fvmFrame);
  fvmCap(w === 'smile' ? 'スマイル · smile soda. press the button under the one you want.' : 'たばこ · tobacco. press the button under the one you want.');
}
function fvmClose(){ if (!FVM.open) return; FVM.open = false; FVM.drag = null; cancelAnimationFrame(FVM.raf); fvmEl.classList.remove('open'); }

/* ---- copied from null.grey-furusatostation/src/c7-tags.js ---- */
/* ================================================================
   THE TAGS. easter eggs: the tags Aisling and her friends used to write —
   sky · PLUR · Rain · Faint · Sin · Faust · tabb. small, in odd corners,
   almost hidden. Faint is hers (so: Null Aisling / Constant / Blue's).
   no graffiti font can be counted on offline, so each tag is drawn here as
   single handwritten strokes — slanted, letters run together, smoothed through
   their points — with its own flourish (a swoosh with an arrow, a star, a
   crown, drips, a heart), then rasterised once into a small mask that the wall
   painters read from.
   ================================================================ */
var TAG_G = {   /* single-stroke letters. y: 0 cap top, 0.45 x-height, 1 baseline, 1.35 descender */
  P:[[[0,1],[0,0],[0.4,0],[0.55,0.12],[0.55,0.33],[0.4,0.45],[0,0.45]]],
  L:[[[0.02,0],[0,1],[0.5,0.98]]],
  U:[[[0,0],[0,0.75],[0.12,1],[0.4,1],[0.52,0.75],[0.52,0]]],
  R:[[[0,1],[0,0],[0.4,0],[0.55,0.12],[0.55,0.33],[0.4,0.45],[0,0.45]], [[0.22,0.45],[0.58,1.02]]],
  F:[[[0.6,0],[0,0.02],[0,1]], [[0,0.46],[0.42,0.44]]],
  S:[[[0.52,0.1],[0.35,0],[0.12,0.02],[0.02,0.2],[0.15,0.42],[0.4,0.55],[0.52,0.78],[0.4,0.98],[0.15,1],[0,0.88]]],
  s:[[[0.36,0.5],[0.22,0.45],[0.06,0.5],[0.06,0.64],[0.3,0.74],[0.38,0.88],[0.28,1],[0.04,0.96]]],
  k:[[[0,0],[0,1]], [[0.38,0.45],[0.04,0.72],[0.4,1]]],
  y:[[[0,0.45],[0.18,0.92]], [[0.42,0.45],[0.14,1.35],[-0.02,1.28]]],
  a:[[[0.4,0.55],[0.25,0.45],[0.06,0.55],[0.02,0.85],[0.15,1],[0.35,0.9],[0.4,0.6],[0.4,0.45],[0.42,0.95],[0.52,1]]],
  i:[[[0.06,0.5],[0.06,1]], [[0.05,0.26],[0.08,0.3]]],
  n:[[[0,1],[0,0.45],[0,0.6],[0.15,0.46],[0.32,0.46],[0.4,0.6],[0.4,1]]],
  t:[[[0.14,0.08],[0.14,0.9],[0.24,1],[0.36,0.95]], [[0,0.45],[0.32,0.43]]],
  u:[[[0,0.45],[0,0.85],[0.12,1],[0.3,0.95],[0.4,0.8],[0.4,0.45],[0.42,1]]],
  b:[[[0,0],[0,1],[0,0.7],[0.15,0.5],[0.32,0.5],[0.42,0.7],[0.36,0.92],[0.18,1],[0,0.95]]]
};
var TAG_W = { P:0.58, L:0.52, U:0.55, R:0.6, F:0.52, S:0.55, s:0.4, k:0.42, y:0.45, a:0.52, i:0.12, n:0.44, t:0.36, u:0.46, b:0.44 };
/* style: w stroke width (px at mask scale), skew, gap (letter spacing), and extras */
var TAG_DEFS = {
  sky:   { w:5,  skew:0.32, gap:-0.04, swoosh:true, star:true, seed:3 },
  PLUR:  { w:6,  skew:0.18, gap:0.02,  heart:true, seed:7 },
  Rain:  { w:15, skew:0.22, gap:-0.02, throwie:true, drips:true, seed:11 },
  Faint: { w:6,  skew:0.36, gap:-0.06, swoosh:true, star:true, seed:13 },
  Sin:   { w:9,  skew:0.26, gap:0.0,   drips:true, seed:17 },
  Faust: { w:5,  skew:0.3,  gap:-0.03, underline2:true, seed:19 },
  tabb:  { w:5,  skew:0.24, gap:-0.05, crown:true, seed:23 }
};
var TAGS = {};
(function(){
  var MW_ = 320, MH_ = 120, CH = 58, BASE = 88;
  function rng(seed){ var s = seed*9301 + 49297; return function(){ s = (s*9301 + 49297) % 233280; return s/233280; }; }
  Object.keys(TAG_DEFS).forEach(function(word){
    var D = TAG_DEFS[word], cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = MW_; cv.height = MH_;
    var g = cv.getContext('2d'), R = rng(D.seed);
    g.lineCap = 'round'; g.lineJoin = 'round'; g.strokeStyle = '#fff'; g.fillStyle = '#fff';
    /* lay the word out, then scale it to fit the mask */
    var total = 0; for (var c = 0; c < word.length; c++) total += TAG_W[word[c]] + D.gap;
    var sc = Math.min(CH, (MW_ - 60)/total), x0 = 24, strokes = [], lows = [];
    for (var c2 = 0; c2 < word.length; c2++) {
      var ch = word[c2], lift = (R() - 0.5)*6, size = sc*(0.92 + R()*0.16);
      TAG_G[ch].forEach(function(st){
        strokes.push(st.map(function(p){
          var yy = BASE - size + p[1]*size + lift;
          return [x0 + p[0]*size + D.skew*(BASE - yy) + (R() - 0.5)*2.2, yy + (R() - 0.5)*2.2];
        }));
      });
      lows.push([x0 + TAG_W[ch]*size*0.5, BASE + lift]);
      x0 += (TAG_W[ch] + D.gap)*sc;
    }
    function path(pts){
      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]);
      if (pts.length === 2) { g.lineTo(pts[1][0], pts[1][1]); return; }
      for (var k = 1; k < pts.length - 1; k++) { var mx = (pts[k][0] + pts[k + 1][0])/2, my = (pts[k][1] + pts[k + 1][1])/2; g.quadraticCurveTo(pts[k][0], pts[k][1], mx, my); }
      g.lineTo(pts[pts.length - 1][0], pts[pts.length - 1][1]);
    }
    function drawAll(w){ g.lineWidth = w; strokes.forEach(function(st){ path(st); g.stroke(); }); }
    drawAll(D.w);
    if (D.throwie) {   /* bubble letters: thick outline, hollow middle */
      g.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out'; drawAll(D.w*0.45); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
    }
    g.lineWidth = Math.max(2.5, D.w*0.55);
    var endX = x0 + 6;
    if (D.swoosh) {   /* underline swoosh with an arrow */
      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(18, BASE + 14); g.quadraticCurveTo((18 + endX)/2, BASE + 26, endX + 10, BASE - 8); g.stroke();
      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(endX + 10, BASE - 8); g.lineTo(endX - 4, BASE - 6); g.moveTo(endX + 10, BASE - 8); g.lineTo(endX + 6, BASE + 6); g.stroke();
    }
    if (D.underline2) { for (var q = 0; q < 2; q++) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(20, BASE + 10 + q*7); g.lineTo(endX, BASE + 6 + q*7); g.stroke(); } }
    function star(cx, cy, r){ g.beginPath(); for (var k = 0; k < 10; k++) { var a = -Math.PI/2 + k*Math.PI/5, rr = k & 1 ? r*0.42 : r; g.lineTo(cx + Math.cos(a)*rr, cy + Math.sin(a)*rr); } g.closePath(); g.fill(); }
    if (D.star) star(endX + 4, BASE - CH - 2, 9);
    if (D.crown) { g.beginPath(); var cx0 = 30, cy0 = BASE - CH - 6; g.moveTo(cx0, cy0 + 12); g.lineTo(cx0 + 2, cy0); g.lineTo(cx0 + 9, cy0 + 7); g.lineTo(cx0 + 15, cy0 - 3); g.lineTo(cx0 + 21, cy0 + 7); g.lineTo(cx0 + 28, cy0); g.lineTo(cx0 + 30, cy0 + 12); g.closePath(); g.stroke(); }
    if (D.heart) { var hx = endX + 6, hy = BASE - 22; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(hx, hy + 12); g.bezierCurveTo(hx - 16, hy, hx - 8, hy - 12, hx, hy - 3); g.bezierCurveTo(hx + 8, hy - 12, hx + 16, hy, hx, hy + 12); g.fill(); }
    if (D.drips) { g.lineWidth = 2.5; lows.forEach(function(p, k){ if (R() < 0.7) { var len = 8 + R()*22; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(p[0] + (R() - 0.5)*10, p[1] - 2); g.lineTo(p[0] + (R() - 0.5)*10, p[1] + len); g.stroke(); } }); }
    var id = g.getImageData(0, 0, MW_, MH_).data, m = new Uint8Array(MW_*MH_);
    for (var i = 0; i < MW_*MH_; i++) m[i] = id[i*4 + 3];
    TAGS[word] = { w:MW_, h:MH_, m:m };
  });
})();
/* is (a, b) — 0..1 across and down the tag's box — on the tag? */
function tagAt(word, a, b){
  var T = TAGS[word]; if (!T || a < 0 || a >= 1 || b < 0 || b >= 1) return 0;
  return T.m[((b*T.h) | 0)*T.w + ((a*T.w) | 0)]/255;
}
/* a tag laid on a wall: s across (world units), h up; box from (s0, top) w wide.
   the mask is 320x120, so the box is w x w*0.375 */
function tagOn(word, s, h, s0, top, w){ return tagAt(word, (s - s0)/w, (top - h)/(w*0.375)); }

/* in this room there's just one (Aisling hides the rest around the world):
   Faint — hers — low on the machine's side, in plum-lavender paint marker.
   the other tags are drawn and ready: tagOn('sky' | 'PLUR' | 'Rain' | 'Sin' | 'Faust' | 'tabb', ...) */
/* Aisling's real Faint tag (her high-school drawing, scanned Sept 30):
   traced from the scan into a 360 x 204 mask, strokes thickened a touch so
   they survive in pixels — 1 = the dark outline, 2 = the paint. run-length
   packed: value, then the run length in base 36. */

function faintAt(a, b){
  if (a < 0 || a >= 1 || b < 0 || b >= 1) return 0;
  return FAINT_SCAN.m[((b*FAINT_SCAN.h) | 0)*FAINT_SCAN.w + ((a*FAINT_SCAN.w) | 0)];
}
/* on the machine's side, low down: pink-plum marker with a dark outline and a
   bit of gloss down each stroke, like the drawing. box 0.24 wide from 0.03 in,
   its top at 0.5 up. */
function vendTag(e, h, flip){
  if (flip) return -1;
  var sw = e*(VBOXM.y1 - VBOXM.y0), W = 0.235, H = W*204/360, a = (sw - 0.03)/W, b = (0.5 - h)/H;
  var PXQ = 110, a = (Math.floor(a*PXQ) + 0.5)/PXQ, b = (Math.floor(b*PXQ*0.567) + 0.5)/(PXQ*0.567);   /* chunkier, like it was drawn in the room's own pixels */
  var f = faintAt(a, b);
  if (f === 2) {
    var up = faintAt(a, b - 0.03), gl = up === 2 && faintAt(a - 0.012, b) === 2 && faintAt(a + 0.012, b) === 2;
    return PK(R_HEART, clamp(0.56 + (gl ? 0.1 : -0.05) + (vn(a*60, b*60) - 0.5)*0.08, 0, 1), 0);
  }
  if (f === 1) return PK(R_BLACK, 0.1, 0);
  return -1;
}

/* ================================================================
   F03 — DETAILS, ROUND 1 (Aisling, Oct 25, with reference pictures; she's on her desktop now).
   - a newspaper someone left on the bench. you can't pick it up; you can look at it close: LOST & NEVER FOUND.
   - newspaper clippings taped to the side of the vending machine; click and they come up close, like the shrine's
     fortunes: a strange light in the sky (1917), the old line about all the people who go missing ("where in the world
     are all these people going to?"), the internet being a passing fad (2000), and the town council asking whether
     you'd know if you were dreaming (check a clock; look away; check again).
   - a missing poster on one of the road's poles. its title is HAVE YOU SEEN ME? (Aisling: "i want to reference nicole
     dollanganger who i love so much and her song"). the face washed out by rain, the name too; last seen walking
     down the road, october 31. click it to read it close.
   - one pole cut through: it hangs from its own wires, a metre and a half off the ground, its bottom nowhere; a newer
     pole stands beside it doing its job (her photo).
   - far out in the corn, east of the road, an oddly tall old windmill, its wheel still, its vane lettered.
   - the vending machine a little bigger (it read small).
   the pictures here are drawn new, in the station's inks; nobody's real paper, poster or photo is copied in.
   ================================================================ */

/* one clipping on its own: the same picture, framed on just that piece */
function f3Focus(i){
  var P = document.getElementById('f3c-pic'), sv = P.querySelector('svg'), b = i >= 0 ? F3C.parts[i].box : [0, 0, F3C.vb[0], F3C.vb[1]];
  F3C.focus = i; sv.setAttribute('viewBox', b.join(' ')); for (var k = 0; k < F3C.parts.length; k++) { var g = sv.querySelector('#f3k' + k); if (g) g.style.display = (i < 0 || k === i) ? '' : 'none'; }   /* just that one, the others put aside */ sv.style.cursor = i >= 0 ? 'zoom-out' : 'zoom-in';
  document.getElementById('f3c-cap').textContent = i >= 0 ? F3C.parts[i].cap + '  (click to see them all again.)' : F3C.cap + '  (click one to look at it on its own.)';
}

function f3Open(svg, vb, cap, rot, parts){
  F3C.parts = parts || null; F3C.focus = -1; F3C.vb = vb; F3C.cap = cap;

  P.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 ' + vb[0] + ' ' + vb[1] + '" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' + svg + '</svg>';
  P.classList.remove('zoom'); document.getElementById('f3c-cap').textContent = cap + (parts ? '  (click one to look at it on its own.)' : '  (click it to look closer.)'); f3cEl.classList.add('open'); f3Fit(P); try { stage.classList.add('drb-open'); } catch (e) {}
}
/* nothing runs over: any line wider than its room on this computer is squeezed to fit; the rest are left as they are */
function f3Fit(P){ try { P.querySelectorAll('text[data-max]').forEach(function(t){ var m = +t.getAttribute('data-max'); if (t.getComputedTextLength() > m) { t.setAttribute('textLength', m); t.setAttribute('lengthAdjust', 'spacingAndGlyphs'); } }); } catch (e) {} }
function f3Close(){ F3C.open = false; try { pkTrap(false); } catch (e) {} f3cEl.classList.remove('open'); try { stage.classList.remove('drb-open'); } catch (e) {} }

var F3P = { paper:'#e6dcc4', old:'#dccb9e', ink:'#2a2420', grey:'#8a8076', tape:'#d8c79a' };
function f3Lines(x, y, w, n, gap, seed){ var s = ''; for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) { var l = w*(i === n - 1 ? 0.55 : 0.88 + 0.12*hsh(i, seed || 1)); s += '<rect x="' + x + '" y="' + (y + i*gap) + '" width="' + l.toFixed(1) + '" height="' + (gap*0.42).toFixed(1) + '" fill="' + F3P.grey + '" opacity="0.75"/>'; } return s; }
function f3Txt(t, x, y, sz, o){ o = o || {}; return '<text x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" font-size="' + sz + '" fill="' + (o.c || F3P.ink) + '" font-family="' + (o.f || 'Georgia, Times New Roman, serif') + '" font-weight="' + (o.w || 400) + '"' + (o.a ? ' text-anchor="' + o.a + '"' : '') + (o.st ? ' font-style="' + o.st + '"' : '') + (o.ls ? ' letter-spacing="' + o.ls + '"' : '') + (o.op ? ' opacity="' + o.op + '"' : '') + (o.tl ? ' data-max="' + o.tl + '"' : '') + '>' + t + '</text>'; }   /* tl: the most room it has; if this computer's font comes out wider, it's squeezed to fit (f3Fit) */
function f3Para(lines, x, y, sz, lh, o){ o = o || {}; return lines.map(function(l, i){ var oo = {}; for (var k in o) oo[k] = o[k]; if (o.just) oo.tl = o.just; else if (!o.tl) delete oo.tl; return f3Txt(l, x, y + i*lh, sz, oo); }).join(''); }   /* just: newspaper columns, every line but the last spread to the width */
function f3Grain(w, h, n, seed){ var s = ''; for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) s += '<circle cx="' + (hsh(i, seed)*w).toFixed(1) + '" cy="' + (hsh(i, seed + 1)*h).toFixed(1) + '" r="' + (0.4 + hsh(i, seed + 2)*1.6).toFixed(1) + '" fill="#6a5a44" opacity="' + (0.08 + 0.14*hsh(i, seed + 3)).toFixed(2) + '"/>'; return s; }

  s += '<path d="M60,110 L60,286 M58,118 l8,0" stroke="#1c1814" stroke-width="3"/>' + f3Grain(430, 300, 260, 9).replace(/<circle cx="([\d.]+)" cy="([\d.]+)"/g, function(m, a, b){ return '<circle cx="' + (36 + +a) + '" cy="' + (100 + +b) + '"'; });
  s += f3Txt('the road south of the station, where the last sighting was. (courier photo)', 36, 418, 11, { st:'italic', c:'#4a4440' });
  /* the headline, the only thing that matters */
  s += f3Txt('LOST &amp;', 486, 168, 58, { f:'Arial Black, Helvetica Neue, Arial, sans-serif', w:900, tl:252 }) + f3Txt('NEVER', 486, 234, 58, { f:'Arial Black, Helvetica Neue, Arial, sans-serif', w:900, tl:242 }) + f3Txt('FOUND', 486, 300, 58, { f:'Arial Black, Helvetica Neue, Arial, sans-serif', w:900, tl:252 });

  s += f3Txt('HARVEST FEST GOES ON — page 4', 528, 518, 12, { f:'Helvetica Neue, Arial, sans-serif', w:700 });
  s += '<defs><filter id="f3grainN" x="0" y="0" width="1" height="1"><feTurbulence type="fractalNoise" baseFrequency="0.85" numOctaves="3" seed="5"/><feColorMatrix values="0 0 0 0 0.25  0 0 0 0 0.2  0 0 0 0 0.12  0 0 0 0.9 -0.35"/></filter><radialGradient id="f3yelN" cx="0.5" cy="0.45" r="0.75"><stop offset="0.6" stop-color="#d6ceb6" stop-opacity="0"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#b89c62" stop-opacity="0.45"/></radialGradient></defs><rect width="' + W + '" height="' + H + '" fill="url(#f3yelN)"/><rect width="' + W + '" height="' + H + '" filter="url(#f3grainN)" opacity="0.3"/>';   /* newsprint: yellowed edges, the paper's grain */
  s += f3Grain(W, H, 140, 21) + '<path d="M0,536 Q200,548 420,538 T800,542 L800,560 L0,560 Z" fill="#c9bb9e" opacity="0.45"/>';   /* (Oct 25: nothing runs off the edge now; the damp only at the very bottom) */   /* the bottom gone soft from the damp */
  return s;
}

/* ---- 2. the clippings taped to the vending machine's side (Oct 25, redone: real newsprint) ----
   cheap grey-cream paper gone yellow at the edges, cut a little crooked with scissors (one torn), the ink not quite black
   and a little soft, set in narrow justified columns with a column rule; the grain of the paper over everything; a
   halftone photo. every line is fitted to its width so no font, on any computer, runs over the edge. */
function f3Cut(x, y, w, h, seed, torn){   /* a clipping's outline: scissor-cut, a little crooked; torn along one side if asked */
  var p = [], n = 9, i, j;
  for (i = 0; i <= n; i++) p.push([x + w*i/n, y + (hsh(i, seed) - 0.5)*2.2]);
  for (i = 1; i <= n; i++) p.push([x + w + (hsh(i, seed + 1) - 0.5)*2.2, y + h*i/n]);
  for (i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) { var ty = y + h; if (torn === 'b') { ty += (hsh(i*3, seed + 2) - 0.5)*9; p.push([x + w*(i + 0.5)/n, y + h + (hsh(i*3 + 1, seed + 2) - 0.5)*7]); } p.push([x + w*i/n, ty + (torn === 'b' ? 0 : (hsh(i, seed + 3) - 0.5)*2.2)]); }
  for (j = n - 1; j > 0; j--) p.push([x + (hsh(j, seed + 4) - 0.5)*2.2, y + h*j/n]);
  return '<path d="M' + p.map(function(q){ return q[0].toFixed(1) + ',' + q[1].toFixed(1); }).join(' L') + ' Z"';
}
function f3Newsprint(id, x, y, w, h, seed, torn, tone){   /* the paper itself: the cut, the yellowing, the grain (clipped to the cut) */
  var cut = f3Cut(x, y, w, h, seed, torn);
  return '<clipPath id="' + id + '">' + cut + '/></clipPath>' + cut + ' fill="' + (tone || '#d6ceb6') + '"/>' +
    '<g clip-path="url(#' + id + ')"><rect x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + h + '" fill="url(#f3yel)"/><rect x="' + (x - 5) + '" y="' + (y - 5) + '" width="' + (w + 10) + '" height="' + (h + 10) + '" filter="url(#f3grain)" opacity="0.32"/></g>';
}

/* (Nov 17) "sky": the infamous Columbus tag (the hub's rule: twice in the hub, once in every other place). here it's on the
   dumpster's east side, low, quick, in white paint marker. one picture, so Seam and Embroidery draw the same tag */
fpic('f3sky', 0.62, 0.2325, 600, 320, 120, '', function(){
  var W = 320, H = 120, c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = W; c.height = H; var g = c.getContext('2d'), id = g.createImageData(W, H), d = id.data;
  for (var j = 0; j < H; j++) for (var i = 0; i < W; i++) { var m = typeof tagAt === 'function' ? tagAt('sky', (i + 0.5)/W, (j + 0.5)/H) : 0, n = ((i*73856093) ^ (j*19349663)) & 255;
    if (m > 0.18 + n/255*0.24) { var o = (j*W + i)*4; d[o] = 232; d[o + 1] = 226; d[o + 2] = 214; d[o + 3] = 255; } }   /* paint marker: a little uneven, ragged at the edges */
  g.putImageData(id, 0, 0); return '<image href="' + c.toDataURL() + '" x="0" y="0" width="320" height="120"/>';
}, true);

/* a texel of one of the pictures, for a box's top (u, v from its top left), as the ink the world uses */
function f3PicTexel(nm, u, v){ var P = FPIC && FPIC[nm]; if (!P || !P.pix || u < 0 || u >= 1 || v < 0 || v >= 1) return PK(R_HCREAM, 0.5, 0); var t = P.pix[((v*P.H) | 0)*P.W + ((u*P.W) | 0)]; return t === HPIC_NONE ? PK(R_HCREAM, 0.5, 0) : t; }

/* ---- clicking: the close-ups. (installed after everything else has wrapped the click, so it's asked first) ---- */
setTimeout(function(){
  F3_SAYS.chopped = ['a pole hanging from its own wires, cut clean through a metre and a half up. the bottom of it is nowhere. a newer pole stands beside it, doing its job.', 'it sways a little, the cut pole, when the wind comes over the corn. it never touches the ground.'];
  F3_SAYS.missing = ['a poster stapled to the pole. HAVE YOU SEEN ME? (click again to read it close.)'];   /* (F3_SAYS lives in f03-road, which comes after this file) */
  var _hb = hubBoxAction;
  hubBoxAction = function(P, r){
    var B = P.box;
    if (B && B.say === 'dump') return r(2.4, function(){ var D = F3_DUMP; D.searched++;
      if (D.searched === 1) say('a big dumpster behind the station, its paint gone brown-black, rust coming through. NO DUMPING · ゴミ捨て禁止. the lid is down. (click again to search it.)', 5200);
      else if (D.find && !D.found) { D.found = true; say(D.find.say, 6000); if (D.find.act) try { D.find.act(); } catch (e) {} }
      else say(['you lift the lid. inside: dark, and the smell of wet leaves. nothing.', 'you look again. wet leaves, a broken umbrella, the dark at the bottom. nothing you want.'][D.searched & 1], 4600); });
    if (B && B.say === 'news') return r(2.6, function(){ f3Open(f3NewsSvg(), [800, 560], 'someone left a newspaper on the bench, folded open. LOST & NEVER FOUND. you don\'t pick it up. it isn\'t yours, and it feels like it\'s waiting for someone.', -1.2); });
    if (B && B.say === 'missing') return r(2.6, function(){ f3Open(f3MissSvg(), [600, 800], 'HAVE YOU SEEN ME? last seen walking down the road from the station, october 31. the rain has taken her face, and her name.', 1.5); });
    if (B && B.say === 'vending' && me.x > 27.9) return r(2.4, function(){ f3Open(f3ClipSvg(), [1070, 600], 'clippings, taped to the side of the machine, yellowed. someone has been collecting them.', 0, F3_CLIPS); });
    return _hb(P, r);
  };
}, 0);

  try { F3_PIT.got = localStorage.getItem('nullgrey.f3.pit') === '1'; } catch (e) {}
  if (F3_PIT.got) return;
  var x0 = 12.63, y0 = 7.69, w = 0.07, d = 0.09;
  var B = fHang(x0, x0 + w, y0, y0 + d, 0.41, 0.432, ['fp_f3pitSide', 'fp_f3pitSide', 'fp_f3pitSide', 'fp_f3pitSide']); B.say = 'pit';
  B.topFn = function(x, y){ var P = FPIC.f3pit, u = (x - x0)/w, vv = (y0 + d - y)/d, v = (!P || !P.pix || u < 0 || u >= 1 || vv < 0 || vv >= 1) ? HPIC_NONE : P.pix[Math.min(P.H - 1, ((1 - vv)*P.H) | 0)*P.W + Math.min(P.W - 1, (u*P.W) | 0)]; return v === HPIC_NONE ? f3BenchSeatAt(x, y) : v; };
  F3_PIT.box = B;
})();
function f3BenchSeatAt(x, y){ return PK(R_HWOOD, 0.3 + 0.08*vn(x*30, y*4), 0); }   /* (round the pit: the bench's own slats) */
(function(){
  var _hb = hubBoxAction;
  hubBoxAction = function(P, r){
    var B = P.box;
    if (B && B.say === 'pit' && !F3_PIT.got) return r(2.4, function(){
      F3_PIT.got = true; try { localStorage.setItem('nullgrey.f3.pit', '1'); } catch (e) {}
      B.h1 = B.h0 + 0.0005; B.topFn = function(x, y){ return f3BenchSeatAt(x, y); };   /* gone from the bench */
      pocketPut('f_pit'); say('a pit from some stone fruit, dried out, on the bench by the newspaper. you put it in your pocket.', 4200);
    });
    return _hb(P, r);
  };
})();

/* ---- Seam's sound engine is here only for the pocket's own sounds (picking things up, cans, bottles, a bite, a
   wrapper). Embroidery has its own switch and its own world sounds, so Seam's switch is never made, its footsteps and
   tube hum are turned off, and its volume follows Embroidery's switch. ---- */
SND.el = document.createElement('button');   /* (never shown) */
sndStep = function(){};
var _egSndBuild = sndBuild;
sndBuild = function(){ _egSndBuild(); SND.master.gain.value = SND_VOL; };
var _egOneShot = sndOneShot;
sndOneShot = function(k){ var r = _egOneShot.apply(null, arguments); try { if (CC_TEXT[k]) EG_SEAM.cc(k, CC_TEXT[k]); } catch (e) {} return r; };
EG_SEAM.soundOn = function(heard, cc){
  SND.on = !!(heard || cc);
  if (SND.on) { try { if (!SND.ctx) sndBuild(); SND.ctx.resume(); SND.master.gain.setTargetAtTime(heard ? SND_VOL : 0, SND.ctx.currentTime, 0.05); } catch (e) {} }
  else if (SND.ctx) SND.master.gain.setTargetAtTime(0, SND.ctx.currentTime, 0.05);
};
/* ---- (the bridge, end) hand Embroidery what it needs from inside Seam's closure, then close it ---- */
EG_SEAM.pocket = {
  open:function(){ if (typeof pkOpen === 'function') pkOpen(); },
  close:function(){ if (typeof pkClose === 'function') pkClose(); },
  isOpen:function(){ return !!(typeof POK !== 'undefined' && POK.open); },
  put:function(id){ if (typeof pocketPut === 'function') return pocketPut(id); },
  have:function(){ return typeof PK_HAVE !== 'undefined' ? PK_HAVE.slice() : []; },
  art:function(){ return typeof PK_ART !== 'undefined' ? Object.keys(PK_ART).length : 0; }
};
/* clicking a thing: Seam's own click actions first (close-ups, the dumpster, the pit...); true if one answered */
EG_SEAM.click = function(sayKey){
  var hit = false;
  try { hubBoxAction({ box:{ say:sayKey } }, function(d, fn){ hit = true; fn(); return true; }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
  return hit;
};
EG_SEAM.where = function(x, z, a){ me.x = x; me.y = z; me.a = a; };
EG_SEAM.closeUpOpen = function(){ return !!((typeof F3C !== 'undefined' && F3C.open) || (typeof FVM !== 'undefined' && FVM.open)); };
EG_SEAM.closeAll = function(){ try { if (typeof F3C !== 'undefined' && F3C.open) f3Close(); } catch (e) {} try { if (typeof FVM !== 'undefined' && FVM.open) fvmClose(); } catch (e) {} };
EG_SEAM.pic = function(name){ return FPIC[name] || null; };
EG_SEAM.liftFirst = function(){ try { var it = (POK.items || []).filter(function(i){ return !i.deep; })[0]; if (it) { pkLift(it); return it.id; } } catch (e) {} return null; };   /* (tests: lift the first thing, as a click on it would) */
EG_SEAM.picNames = function(){ return Object.keys(FPIC); };   /* (for the text check, t/text.js) */
/* (the train) going between pages, as every Seam page does (shared/hy2-journey.js), and the ride's line map and words */
EG_SEAM.ride = { stops:RIDE_STOPS, pages:F_STOP_PAGES, say:RIDE_SAY, map:RIDE_MAP, svg:RIDE_MAP_SVG, warn:F_WARN, here:RIDE_HERE,
  arrived:(typeof ngArrivedHere !== 'undefined' ? ngArrivedHere : null), go:function(page, at){ keys = {}; ngGoTo(page, at); }, fade:function(on){ ngFade(on); }, leaving:function(){ return NGJ.leaving; } };
EG_SEAM.step = function(now, dt){ try { if (POK.open) pkFrame(now); seamStep(now, dt); }   /* (as Seam's d2-frame: while the pocket is open, it's what's drawn) */ catch (e) { if (!EG_SEAM.err) { EG_SEAM.err = e; console.error(e); } } };
})();
